## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa、扩散语言模型与 flow matching language model。

### [1] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (71.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出“任务加权图表”（task-weighted charts）：在指定泛函及其度量下，将语言模型连续表征拟合为低维坐标系并归结为最小二乘蒸馏。实验发现 next-token 预测需要残差流 70–90% 的宽度，而方差几乎不预测功能（GPT-2 两个方向占 90% 方差却几乎不承载功能）；维度随泛函与深度变化，且按泛函度量训练的图表在极限压缩下优于方差基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “语言模型的计算到底使用了多少维度”这一问题在未指定泛函前是不适定的。作者希望引入任务加权图表使其变为适定问题，并检验激活方差能否预测功能承载，从而为语言模型表征理解与压缩提供依据。

Method: 在 6 个模型（3 个家族，70M–7B 参数）上，针对所选泛函（如 next-token 预测、模型自身不确定性）以泛函自身度量拟合低维坐标系，将蒸馏归结为最小二乘问题；进而比较按泛函度量训练的图表与基于方差或最优线性压缩的预测保真度，并分析维度随深度与泛函的变化。

Result: next-token 预测需保留残差流 70–90% 的宽度才能将困惑度维持在完好模型的 5% 以内，该宽度由语言的稀有尾部消耗，且方差轮廓完全无法预测它：GPT-2 的两个方向承载 90% 激活方差却几乎不承载功能。维度是泛函相关的——模型自身的不确定性仅从 6 个坐标即可读出，且维度随深度增长。在仅保留少数维度时，按泛函度量训练的图表比基于方差或最优线性压缩更好地保留模型预测。

Conclusion: 语言模型的功能与其激活方差显著解耦，本征维度必须相对于具体任务泛函来定义；任务加权图表既是分析语言模型连续潜空间几何的工具，也构成了优于方差基线的低维蒸馏/压缩方法。

Classification reason: 论文核心是对语言模型连续潜空间（残差流）的功能性维度分析，提出任务加权的低维坐标图并研究 latent 蒸馏压缩，与 canonical 子主题 continuous_language_flow（连续 embedding/latent 空间语言建模）松散相关，故优先复用该子主题；论文不涉及多模态、生成或世界模型，其他方向不匹配。

Importance reason: A solid interpretability/compression finding (function decouples from variance in the residual stream) that touches model efficiency but lacks the continuous/multimodal or generative content driving the user's top priorities. Signals: task-weighted low-dimensional charts, variance vs function dissociation, next-token needs 70-90% residual width, functional-metric compression beats variance baselines, six models 70m-7B

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [2] [When Edit Flows are Edit Jumps: replicating Edit Flows and EvoFlows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18745)
*Gabriel Bénédict, Melanie Buechler, Gerard Riera-Solà, Chloé de Ancos, Yves Gaetan Nana Teukam, Moritz Freidank*

Main category: cs.LG, stat.ML

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (74.97) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文从理论上统一了 Edit Flows 与 EvoFlows 两种基于编辑操作的序列生成模型，证明二者本质都是连续时间中按学习速率逐一触发的纯跳过程，并发布首个开源实现 EditJumps，用单一在 1.66M 抗体同源对上训练的通用编辑器对抗体先导序列做零样本优化，同时揭示复现过程中的隐藏超参数与评测样本量敏感性陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗体先导优化需要对已有候选序列做少量、有界的编辑（替换、插入、删除），编辑型生成模型是唯一能分配编辑预算且不预先固定编辑位置、编辑数量与输出长度的方式。然而已有方法 Edit Flows 与 EvoFlows 均未公开代码和完整训练规范，可复现性缺失阻碍了该方向的研究。

Method: 作者从理论上证明两种方法遵循同一底层过程：编辑在连续时间中按学习到的速率逐一触发，即有限序列上 generator matching 的纯跳（pure-jump）特例。据此提出 EditJumps——该框架的首个开源实现，在 1.66M Observed Antibody Space 同源序列对上训练单一通用抗体编辑器，可对未见先导序列零样本生成类同源变异，无需按抗体家族重训练，并附自动化测试套件。

Result: 从零复现暴露了开放代码对生成式生物学的必要性：为调和已发表的编辑分布，需逆向工程一个未文档化的速率缩放超参数，它直接决定实际突变数量；此外，已发表的评测指标对参考样本量高度敏感，常导致方法排名翻转。EditJumps 作为通用抗体编辑器在零样本设置下可对未见先导提出类同源变异。

Conclusion: Edit Flows 与 EvoFlows 本质上同为连续时间纯跳编辑过程，EditJumps 提供了首个开源、可复现的通用抗体序列编辑框架，零样本即可优化新先导序列；研究同时凸显了生成式生物学中开源代码与稳健评测协议（警惕小样本导致的排名翻转）的关键意义。

Classification reason: 论文核心是对 Edit Flows / EvoFlows 这类基于编辑操作的连续时间序列生成流匹配框架的理论统一与开源复现（generator matching 的纯跳特例），属于 flow matching 序列/语言生成建模范畴，与规范子主题 continuous_language_flow 相关；虽应用场景为抗体序列，但方法论上与其最匹配，且无更贴合的其他方向或子主题。

Importance reason: A solid theoretical unification and open-source replication of edit-based jump-process sequence models, but its entire application is antibody/protein sequence optimization, a domain the user explicitly deprioritizes, with no language or multimodal content. Signals: replication study, antibody/protein domain (negative), edit-based jump processes not flow matching, discrete sequence editing, open-source release

Abstract: Antibody lead optimization calls for a small, bounded set of edits to an existing candidate: substitutions, but also insertions and deletions. Edit-based generative models are the only ones that allocate such an edit budget without fixing the edit positions, the edit count, or the output length in advance. However, the existing approaches Edit Flows and EvoFlows did not release code or complete training specifications. Here, we show that both methods follow the same underlying process -- edits firing one at a time, at learned rates, in continuous time -- the pure-jump case of generator matching over finite sequences. With EditJumps we introduce the first open implementation of this framework, with a single generalist antibody editor trained on 1.66M Observed Antibody Space homolog pairs to propose homolog-like variants of a seed sequence, editing unseen leads zero-shot, without the per-family retraining original approaches require. Replicating this system from scratch exposes why open code is essential for generative biology: reconciling published edit distributions required reverse-engineering an undocumented rate-scaling hyperparameter that dictates realized mutation counts. Moreover, we show that published evaluation metrics are highly sensitive to reference sample size, frequently flipping method rankings. We release our full codebase, automated test suite, and configurations at:

</details>

### [3] [RAF-VLA: Representation Alignment with the Future for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17728)
*Dogun Kim, Yongjae Lee, Joonhee Lim, Yeina Lee, Junhyeok Park, Moogeun Park, Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (83.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RAF-VLA 提出面向端到端自动驾驶的 VLA 框架：不再显式生成未来驾驶场景，而是通过 Future-Aligned 监督微调，将策略隐状态与预训练世界编码器的未来帧表征对齐，从而以表征对齐方式获得规划相关的内部表征；在 NAVSIM 上以极少的训练样本达到与 SOTA VLA 规划器相当的规划性能，训练开销仅 3.8%、推理开销约 1 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期自动驾驶 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型通过同时预测未来驾驶场景与驾驶动作来引入世界建模，未来场景作为稠密监督促使策略学到有利于规划的内部表征；但这类 World-Modeling VLA 依赖显式的未来生成，带来额外训练负担与推理延迟两大局限。

Method: 提出 RAF-VLA：在 VLA 驾驶框架中引入 Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning，用一个简单的正则项将策略的隐状态与预训练世界编码器（world encoder）提取的未来帧表征对齐，在只学习驾驶动作的同时塑造规划相关的内部表征，从而完全避免显式的未来场景生成。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上，RAF-VLA 取得了与 SOTA VLA 规划器相当甚至有竞争力的规划性能，且所需训练样本数显著更少；训练开销仅增加 3.8%，推理开销仅约 1 ms，几乎可忽略。

Conclusion: 用预训练世界编码器的未来帧表征对 VLA 隐状态进行直接对齐，可替代昂贵的未来场景生成来塑造规划相关表征，在自动驾驶规划任务上以极小的训练与推理开销达到有竞争力的性能，为世界模型信息融入 VLA 提供了一种轻量范式。

Classification reason: 论文属于世界模型方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子话题具身世界模型与规划控制：它面向端到端自动驾驶的 VLA 规划控制，利用预训练世界编码器的未来帧表征作为监督来塑造规划相关的内部表征，属于世界模型表征服务于具身规划/控制的典型工作；虽然显式避免生成未来视频，因此不归入视频世界模型子话题。

Importance reason: A solid efficiency-oriented VLA driving paper that uses pretrained world-encoder future representations for alignment rather than generative world modeling, making it adjacent to the user's world-model interests but outside their core continuous-token and video/image generation subtopics. Signals: VLA end-to-end autonomous driving, future-frame representation alignment replaces explicit future generation, world encoder as supervision signal, 3.8% training overhead and ~1 ms inference overhead, NAVSIM benchmark

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving have incorporated world modeling by predicting future driving scenes alongside driving actions, demonstrating strong planning performance. Future driving scenes are utilized as dense supervision, encouraging the policy to learn rich internal representations useful for planning. However, these World-Modeling VLAs rely on explicit future generation to learn such representations, thereby introducing two key limitations: additional training burden and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose RAF-VLA (Representation Alignment with the Future), a VLA-based autonomous driving framework that shapes planning-relevant internal representations through direct guidance from future-frame representations. RAF-VLA employs Future-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning, in which a straightforward regularization aligns the policy's hidden states with future-frame representations obtained from a pretrained world encoder while learning driving actions. This simple alignment allows RAF-VLA to avoid the training burden and inference latency associated with future generation. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that RAF-VLA achieves competitive planning performance against state-of-the-art VLA planners with substantially fewer training samples seen. Moreover, RAF-VLA incurs only 3.8% training overhead and a negligible 1 ms inference overhead.

</details>


### [4] [CALOS: Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety Layer for Safe Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17758)
*Fabrizio Cesareo, Sebastiano Mengozzi, Nicola Mimmo, Andrea Acquaviva*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (52.07) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CALOS：一种面向四旋翼深度强化学习控制的运行时安全层，将倾角约束与 Lyapunov 下降条件表述为单个二次规划，对策略输出的名义力矩做最小范数修正，并通过主动集枚举在三维力矩空间中精确实时求解；在 NVIDIA Isaac Lab 的轨迹跟踪任务中将横向跟踪误差降低 55–60%，实现零姿态约束违反，同时加速训练收敛并提升数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在四旋翼控制中表现出色，但学习到的策略在训练和部署过程中无法保证满足安全约束（如姿态/倾角限制）；需要一个无需修改学习算法、计算开销足够低、可在大规模并行 RL 训练中实时运行的运行时安全层，将策略探索限制在安全状态空间区域内。

Method: 提出 CALOS 运行时安全层：将四个倾角不等式约束与一个 Lyapunov 下降条件统一表述为单个二次规划，其解为对策略名义力矩输出的最小范数修正；由于系统为控制仿射且力矩空间仅三维，采用主动集枚举对 QP 精确求解，计算开销足够低，可在现代大规模并行 DRL 训练所需的数千个并行仿真环境中实时施加约束，且不修改底层学习算法。

Result: 在 NVIDIA Isaac Lab 的轨迹跟踪任务上，相对于无约束 PPO 基线，CALOS 将横向跟踪误差降低 55–60%，并在训练轨迹上实现零姿态约束违反；由于将探索限制在安全区域内，安全层还加速了训练收敛并提升数据效率，且未产生次优策略。

Conclusion: 基于控制仿射形式与 Lyapunov 条件的安全层可在不修改学习算法的情况下为四旋翼 RL 控制提供硬性安全保证，并通过将探索限制在安全区域内同时提升跟踪精度、训练收敛速度与数据效率，适用于大规模并行 DRL 训练场景。

Classification reason: 该论文属于四旋翼机器人的深度强化学习控制与运行时安全保证，属于具身智能/机器人控制范畴，与 world_model 方向下覆盖机器人、动作规划与控制的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model 最匹配，故复用该子主题；不涉及连续语言模型、多模态生成或世界模型动力学学习，其他方向不匹配。

Importance reason: A technically solid Lyapunov/QP-based safety layer for safe RL quadrotor control with strong results, but it falls outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and generative world models. Signals: Lyapunov safety QP, safe deep RL for quadrotors, real-time active-set QP solving, zero attitude-constraint violations, faster RL training convergence

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning has demonstrated remarkable capability in quadrotor control, yet learned policies offer no guarantee of respecting safety constraints during training or deployment. We present CALOS (Control-Affine Lyapunov On-manifold Safety), a runtime safety layer that enforces attitude constraints on a quadrotor without modifying the underlying learning algorithm. CALOS formulates four tilt-angle inequalities and a Lyapunov descent condition as a single quadratic program whose solution is the minimum-norm correction to the nominal torque output of the policy. The quadratic program is solved exactly via active-set enumeration over the three-dimensional torque space, with a computational cost low enough to enforce constraints in real time across thousands of parallel simulation environments, as required by modern massively parallel Deep Reinforcement Learning training. Evaluated on trajectory-tracking tasks in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, CALOS reduces lateral tracking error by 55-60% relative to an unconstrained Proximal Policy Optimization baseline while achieving zero attitude-constraint violations on the training trajectory. By restricting exploration to safe regions of the state space, the safety layer also accelerates training convergence and improves data efficiency without producing suboptimal policies.

</details>


### [5] [HINT-Plan: Human Intention-Aware Robot Task Planning in Context-Rich Environments using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17771)
*Yuchen Liu, Luigi Palmieri, Lujun Li, Radu State, Ilche Georgievski, Marco Aiello*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (58.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HINT-Plan 利用 VLM 从第三人称图像中推断高层人类意图，结合层级场景图与形式化规划语言，实现人机联合任务规划；在逼真仿真中成功率达 69.71%，最高超越基线 35.29%，并减少功能冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将人类意识纳入移动机器人决策的方法主要集中于低层运动规划中的避碰，忽视了人类在场及高层行为对任务规划带来的挑战；缺乏能够主动预测人类意图并将其融入机器人任务规划的机制。

Method: 提出 HINT-Plan 框架：利用 VLM 从第三人称图像观测中预测高层人类意图并转化为目标状态；以层级场景图（Scene Graphs）作为富上下文环境的高层表征，将环境拓扑与可执行知识翻译为形式化规划语言，进而求解人机联合任务规划问题。

Result: 在逼真仿真环境中，HINT-Plan 在人机联合任务规划上取得 69.71% 的总体成功率，较基线最高提升 35.29%，同时减少了功能冲突（functional conflicts）。

Conclusion: 将推断的人类意图显式融入形式化多智能体任务规划，可以实现主动的人类感知机器人决策；VLM 意图预测 + 层级场景图 + 形式化规划的组合是处理富上下文环境中人机协作任务的有效范式。

Classification reason: 论文核心是具身场景下利用 VLM 视觉理解进行人类意图预测与机器人任务规划，属于世界模型方向中 [CANONICAL] 子主题「具身世界模型与规划控制」（涵盖 embodied AI、planning、control）。论文不涉及连续语言/连续潜空间建模，也非生成或多模态理解生成模型本体研究，故不匹配其他方向。

Importance reason: A solid VLM-based embodied task-planning paper, but it is symbolic planning with scene graphs rather than the generative/video world models or continuous multimodal modeling the user prioritizes. Signals: VLM human intention inference, hierarchical scene graphs, formal joint human-robot task planning, embodied planning rather than generative world model, weak keyword overlap with priority topics

Abstract: Approaches to incorporating human awareness into mobile robot decision-making mainly focus on collision avoidance in low-level motion planning, often overlooking the challenges posed by human presence and high-level behavior. To address this vacancy, we present HINT-Plan, a novel approach to integrate human intention prediction into robot task planning. HINT-Plan employs Vision Language Models (VLMs) to anticipate high-level human intentions from third-person image observations, convert them into goal states, and solve joint task-planning problems. To effectively enable scene awareness in context-rich environments, we use hierarchical Scene Graphs (SGs) as high-level representations of the environment, and translate environmental topology and actionable knowledge into formal planning language to ensure executable plans. Evaluated in a photorealistic simulation, HINT-Plan achieves an overall success rate of 69.71% in joint human-robot task planning, substantially outperforming the baselines by up to 35.29%, while also reducing functional conflicts. The results show the effectiveness of explicitly incorporating inferred human intentions into formal multi-agent task planning for proactive human-aware robot decision-making.

</details>

Method: 提出基于附着接口的去中心化对象中心控制框架：每个人形机器人被分配共享物体上的一个局部附着区域，通过无夹爪的双臂夹捏完成拾取与搬运。该附着式抽象为单机器人拾取、多机器人协作搬运与机器人间传递提供统一接口，无需逐任务重新设计。策略先在单机器人拾取任务上训练，再进行显式多机器人协作训练，并在不同团队规模与物体几何的仿真中验证，最后进行 sim-to-real 硬件迁移。

Result: 仅在单机器人拾取上训练的策略已能非平凡地迁移到协作场景，表明该控制抽象捕获了协调所需的大部分结构；显式多机器人训练进一步显著提升性能，说明共享物体耦合带来的协调动态值得直接学习。方法在不同团队规模与物体几何的仿真中得到验证，并在真实人形机器人硬件上成功演示了协作操控任务。

Conclusion: 附着区域式的去中心化对象中心控制是一种有效且可泛化的协作操控抽象，能统一覆盖单机器人拾取、多机器人搬运与机器人间传递任务；单机器人预训练与多机器人协作训练相结合，可实现从仿真到真实人形机器人团队的协作操控。

Importance reason: Although tagged under world_model, this is a decentralized multi-humanoid manipulation control paper with no world model, generative modeling, or multimodal content, giving it minimal overlap with the user's continuous multimodal and video/image generation priorities. Signals: decentralized object-centric control, multi-humanoid cooperative pickup and transport, sim-to-real on real humanoid hardware, single-robot policy transfers to multi-robot settings, no world model or neural simulator component

Abstract: We study cooperative multi-humanoid pickup and transport of objects with varying size, weight, and geometry, requiring robot teams of different sizes. Our approach uses decentralized object-centric control, where each humanoid is assigned a local attachment region on the shared object and learns to realize pickup and transport through gripperless bimanual pinching. This attachment-based interface provides a common control abstraction spanning single-robot pickup, cooperative multi-robot transport, and robot-to-robot handover, without per-task redesign. We find that policies trained only on single-robot pickup already transfer nontrivially to cooperative settings, suggesting that this abstraction captures much of the structure needed for coordination. At the same time, explicit multi-robot training further improves performance, showing that shared-object coupling introduces coordination dynamics that are beneficial to learn directly. We validate the approach in simulation across varying team sizes and object geometries, and demonstrate sim-to-real transfer on hardware, where the learned controllers enable real humanoids to perform cooperative manipulation tasks.

</details>

*Akshay Naik, Ramavarapu S. Sreenivas, Dustin Nottage, Ahmet Soylemezoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VCTP（车辆条件地形规划）：在八个车体朝向下评估共享高程与表面层，用车体几何约束通行性、由承载车轮接触计算表面代价并预测俯仰/侧滚，再用 D* Lite 与车辆状态搜索规划越野路线；车轮接触采样比车体中心采样降低 39.3% 建模表面代价且缩短路径，选择性增量更新在 474 次比较中与全量重计算完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆的行驶朝向会同时改变轮胎下方的地形表面与车体的俯仰、侧滚姿态，而传统以距离为中心的越野规划忽略了这种地形—车体几何耦合关系，导致代价建模不准、路线选择次优。

Method: 在八个车体朝向下评估共享的高程与表面 ID 层，用车体几何约束可行通行性；由承载车轮接触点计算建模表面代价并预测俯仰与侧滚，结合 D* Lite 与车辆状态搜索规划含前进/倒车动作的路线；观测变化时仅对受影响的车体或接触查询做选择性增量重计算。

Result: 在全观测双轨仿真中，以车轮接触点采样代替车体中心采样使建模表面代价降低 39.3% 且路线更短；在 RGator 真实录制数据的离线规划上，相对距离导向规划也降低了建模代价（但覆盖不完整使全路线排序未完全确定）；在 474 次基于录制地图变化的比较中，选择性更新与全量重计算结果完全一致。

Conclusion: VCTP 表明车轮接触点布局与车体朝向会实质性影响越野路线选择；保留地形—车体几何耦合的规划可得到更低成本且更短的路径，而选择性增量更新使其在实际观测变化下保持高效。

Classification reason: 论文属于 cs.RO 的越野车辆地形规划与导航，核心是用高程/表面层建模并结合车体几何、车轮接触预测俯仰与侧倾，再用 D* Lite 进行路径规划与增量更新。这契合世界模型方向中"具身智能环境模型与规划控制"主题，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子话题 embodied_world_model（面向机器人与具身智能的动作规划控制），非连续语言/多模态或生成方向。

Importance reason: A solid but niche robotics terrain-planning contribution whose "world model" label refers to embodied off-road navigation, not the generative video/physical world models the user prioritizes. Signals: off-road vehicle planning, terrain traversability modeling, D* Lite search, wheel-contact sampling 39.3% cost reduction, not generative world model

Abstract: A vehicle's heading affects both the surfaces beneath its tires and its pitch and roll. We present Vehicle-Conditioned Terrain Planning (VCTP), which retains these relationships by evaluating shared elevation and surface-ID layers at eight headings. Body geometry constrains admissibility, while loaded wheel contacts determine modeled surface cost and predicted pitch and roll. Established D* Lite and vehicle-state search use these evaluations to plan routes with forward and reverse motion. When observations change, VCTP recomputes every affected body or contact query. In fully observed two-track simulations, sampling at wheel contacts rather than at the vehicle center lowers modeled surface cost by 39.3% while shortening the route. In offline planning on RGator recordings, VCTP also reduces modeled costs over identified surfaces and observed support relative to distance-focused planning, although incomplete coverage leaves full-route rankings unresolved. Selective updates match full recomputation in all 474 comparisons using recorded map changes. These results identify when wheel-contact placement and vehicle heading affect route choice.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言导航与路线规划工作对组合式语言理解（关系、比较、嵌套描述）以及有序必经区域、必避要求、多类软偏好、空间与路线阶段范围等复杂约束的测试不充分，缺乏系统性基准来衡量语言条件路线规划的真实能力与差距。

Method: 构建人工策划的 Map2Route 基准：在 40 个预建语义地图上设置 1,000 个 episode，指令涵盖关系/比较/嵌套描述、有序必经与必避区域、五类软偏好以及空间与路线阶段范围约束；提出 Grounding2Route 基线，将语言指令转化为可执行代码作为 grounding（code-as-grounding），并引入验证引导的修复（verification-guided repair）；同时适配七个代表性现有方法进行系统对比。

Result: Grounding2Route 在所有指标上显著优于七个适配的现有基线，验证了可执行代码 grounding 与验证引导修复的有效性；但与人类演示相比仍存在明显差距，说明该基准难度较高、提升空间大。

Conclusion: Map2Route 填补了组合式语言理解与约束满足式路线规划的评测空白；代码化 grounding 加验证修复是当前有效范式，但整体性能远未达人类水平，为后续具身导航与语言条件规划研究提供了明确的改进空间与测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce Map2Route, a human-curated benchmark for compositional language-grounded route planning over pre-built semantic maps. Map2Route contains 1,000 episodes across 40 scenes, where instructions use relational, comparative, and nested descriptions to identify route-relevant objects and regions, while specifying ordered must-pass regions, must-avoid requirements, five categories of soft preferences, and spatial and route-stage scopes, which is partially tested by existing works. Alongside Map2Route, we propose Grounding2Route, which combines executable code-as-grounding with verification-guided repair and scope-aware seven representative adapted baselines, Grounding2Route substantially outperforms existing methods in all metrics. Despite these gains, a substantial gap to human demonstrations remains, highlighting the difficulty of Map2Route and the considerable headroom for future progress. Additional qualitative results and resources are available on .

</details>


### [9] [Causal-History Test-Time Scaling for Failure Recovery in Autoregressive World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18016)
*Lin Li, Long Chen, Kwunhang, Wong, Jiaming Lei, Song Jin, Shucheng Du, Chuhan Zhang, Songchen Ma, Weihao Zhang, Jun Xiao, Kwang-Ting, Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (64.52) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 CHARTS（Causal-History Test-Time Scaling），将自回归世界-动作模型的执行失败恢复形式化为"对因果历史的测试时缩放"，通过进度感知触发、历史前缀恢复与假设验证三阶段修复被污染的因果 KV 历史，在仿真与真实机器人操作中一致提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAMs）通过联合建模未来视觉动力学与机器人动作，成为机器人操作的有前景范式。但现有 WAM 主要在成功轨迹上训练，当真实执行偏离所学动力学时容易失败；该问题在自回归 WAM 中被放大，因为执行错误会进入因果历史并持续影响后续预测。论文旨在解决自回归 WAM 执行失败后的恢复问题。

Method: 提出免训练框架，将失败恢复形式化为对因果历史的测试时缩放，包含三个耦合阶段：1) 进度感知恢复触发——检测持续无进展状态，仅在当前执行状态允许干预时触发恢复；2) 历史前缀恢复——定位不可靠的历史后缀，检索与当前物理状态匹配的历史锚点，基于保留的前缀重建因果 KV 状态，并以最新真实观测为条件；3) 假设验证——比较完整历史、恢复前缀与完全重置三种假设诱导的未来推演，提交最有支持的假设用于后续执行。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作环境中，该方法均带来一致的任务成功率提升；消融实验确认了触发、前缀恢复与假设验证各阶段的独立贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，自回归世界-动作模型的真实部署可靠性可通过免训练的测试时缩放来显著提升：将失败恢复分解为触发、历史前缀恢复与假设验证三个环节，使执行误差不会污染后续预测，从而在仿真与真实环境中稳定提升任务成功率，为 WAM 在非平稳真实条件下的长期执行提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究面向机器人操作的 World-Action Models（WAM），核心是通过测试时缩放对自回归世界模型的因果历史进行失败恢复，属于具身智能世界中动作条件动力学建模与规划控制的范畴，最贴合 [CANONICAL] 子话题 embodied_world_model；与视频世界模型生成有交集但重心在机器人操作执行，故主方向为 world_model。

Importance reason: A novel training-free test-time-scaling formulation for autoregressive world-action models matches the world_model direction, but its embodied robot-manipulation focus diverges from the user's video/generative world model priorities. Signals: autoregressive world-action model, test-time scaling, KV causal-history repair, training-free failure recovery, embodied robot manipulation

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and robot actions. However, existing WAMs are trained predominantly on successful trajectories, making them prone to failure when real-world execution diverges from the learned dynamics. This issue is amplified in autoregressive WAMs, where execution errors become part of the causal history and continue to influence subsequent predictions. To this end, we introduce \method{}, a training-free framework that reformulates failure recovery as \emph{test-time scaling over causal histories}. This formulation decomposes recovery into three coupled decisions: \emph{when} to revise the causal history, \emph{where} to recover a reliable history prefix, and \emph{which} history configuration best supports subsequent execution. Specifically, \method{} realizes these decisions through three stages: 1) \textbf{Progress-Aware Recovery Trigger} detects persistent non-progress and triggers recovery only when the current execution state permits intervention; 2) \textbf{History-Prefix Recovery} identifies the unreliable history suffix, retrieves a historical anchor matching the current physical state, and reconstructs the causal KV state from the retained prefix while conditioning on the latest real observation; and 3) \textbf{Hypothesis Verification} compares the future continuations induced by complete-history, recovered-prefix, and full-reset hypotheses, and commits the best-supported hypothesis. Experiments in both simulated and real-world manipulation settings demonstrate consistent improvements in task success, while ablations confirm the contribution of each recovery stage.

</details>


### [10] [RoboVAD: A Large Cross-Domain Evaluation Benchmark for Anomaly Detection in Robotic Arm Manipulation Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17843)
*Alexandru-Bogdan Dura, Sebastian Balmus, Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (56.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 RoboVAD：首个面向机械臂操作视频的大规模跨域视频异常检测（VAD）评测基准，评估协议中部分任务（动作）与异常类型在训练时保持未见。作者训练并评测多种 SOTA VAD 方法及一个针对机械臂操作适配的新方法；最困难设置下所有方法 micro-averaged frame-level AUC 均低于 70%，凸显基准挑战性与现有方法跨域泛化不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测（VAD）在公共监控、道路交通等场景被广泛研究；在机械臂操作场景中，VAD 可支持更好的交互操作学习、异常发生时触发恢复流程等下游应用。但该方向探索受限于可用资源稀缺，且现实中机械臂会执行未预见任务并遇到新异常类型，现有方法缺乏针对这种跨域（unseen 任务、unseen 异常）情况的系统性评测。

Method: 构建 RoboVAD 大规模基准，设计跨域评估协议：将机械臂执行的动作（任务）与发生的异常类型划分为训练可见与训练未见（unseen）场景，模拟机械臂执行未知任务并遭遇新型异常的真实情况。在该基准上训练并评测多个 SOTA VAD 方法，并提出一个针对机械臂操作视频适配的新 VAD 方法。

Result: 所提出的机械臂适配方法优于多数 SOTA 竞争方法，但在最具挑战性的评估设置下，所有方法的 micro-averaged frame-level AUC 均低于 70%，证实了该基准的显著难度；数据集与代码已公开。

Conclusion: RoboVAD 填补了机械臂操作视频异常检测的资源空白，其跨域（未见任务与未见异常类型）评估协议揭示了当前 VAD 方法的泛化短板，为机器人操作场景的异常检测及下游的恢复策略与交互学习研究提供了标准化评测平台。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦机械臂操作（具身/机器人）场景下的视频异常检测，涉及机器人与环境交互动态的建模与感知，且 arXiv 类别含 cs.RO，与"世界模型"方向中具身智能/机器人建模最相关，故复用其下覆盖面最广的 canonical 子话题"具身世界模型与规划控制"；未选 video_understanding_temporal 是因其聚焦视频 LLM/问答类任务，而本文核心是机器人交互场景的异常动态感知与评测。

Importance reason: 尽管标注为具身世界模型方向，但实质是机械臂视频异常检测的评测基准，与用户关注的视频生成、连续多模态、世界模型建模等优先方向几乎无交集。 Signals: 视频异常检测基准, 机械臂操作视频, 跨域泛化评测, 首个大规模机器人VAD资源, 无生成建模或世界模型学习内容

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is an actively studied task, having wide applications in typical scenarios such as public surveillance and road traffic safety. The task is also relevant for robotic arm interactions, where it has several downstream applications, including learning better interaction and manipulation abilities, triggering recovery procedures when anomalies occur, etc. Despite its relevance, the exploration of anomaly detection in robotic arm manipulation videos is limited by the low number of available resources. To this end, we introduce RoboVAD, a large-scale benchmark for video anomaly detection that comprises challenging cross-domain evaluation scenarios, where certain actions (tasks executed by a robotic arm) and anomaly types (mistakes that occur while performing certain tasks) remain unseen during training. RoboVAD is designed to benchmark VAD methods in realistic scenarios, where robotic arms can perform unforeseen tasks, and thereby encounter new anomaly types. We train and evaluate several state-of-the-art VAD methods, including a novel method specifically adapted for robotic arm manipulation. While the proposed method outperforms many state-of-the-art competitors, all methods remain below a micro-averaged frame-level AUC threshold of 70% in the most challenging evaluation setup, confirming the difficulty of the proposed benchmark. We publicly release our dataset and code at .

</details>


### [11] [Risk-Aware World Modeling with Flow-Guided Occupancy Evolution for Selective Trajectory Planning in Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18442)
*Rongxiang Zeng, Linsen Cai, Jiafu Zhang, Yijie Zhong, Yide Tao, Shuai Wang, Nan Zheng, Hai L. Vu, Alvaro Garcia Hernandez, Yongqi Dong*

Main category: cs.AI, eess.SY, cs.LG, cs.ET, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (83.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 RiskWorld：一个用于自动驾驶的风险感知世界模型，融合空间风险场、时序参与者上下文与 BEV 视觉特征，通过流引导演化传输占据和场景特征并用带符号残差校正；每个规划步生成一次预测并跨候选复用，与"维持当前状态"参考对比得到非负碰撞分数修正，仅在高风险触发且备选轨迹满足约束时替换规划锚点轨迹。在 nuScenes 开环规划上 3 秒时域取得最低碰撞率、平均 L2 误差第二，单卡 RTX 4090 上 11.5 FPS（90.81M 参数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶的安全运动规划需要预判不断演化的交通风险，并决定何时修改当前已规划的轨迹；现有方法缺乏对风险感知的未来占据预测以及选择性轨迹替换机制。

Method: 将空间风险场与时序交互参与者上下文与视觉 BEV 特征融合；采用流引导演化（flow-guided evolution）输运占据与场景特征，并用带符号残差对输运后的占据进行校正；每个规划步仅生成一次预测并在候选间复用；将每个候选轨迹与当前状态持续参考对比，得到非负的碰撞分数修正；当前世界评估选出的轨迹作为规划锚点，仅当额外预测风险触发干预且备选轨迹满足预测风险与轨迹误差的逐分量约束时才替换，候选几何保持不变。

Result: 在 nuScenes 开环规划（相机特征 + 标注获得的当前/历史参与者状态 + 数据集地图上下文）上，3 秒评估时域取得最低碰撞率，平均 L2 误差位居各 SOTA 基线第二；单张 RTX 4090 上以 90.81M 参数达到 11.5 FPS；消融实验显示其碰撞率低于当前状态重打分基线，且预测复用能以极低的边际计算成本评估更多候选轨迹。

Conclusion: 流引导占据演化与风险感知的世界模型能有效预测未来交通风险，并通过选择性轨迹替换机制提升长时程规划安全性，同时保持高运行效率，为自动驾驶提供了一种安全高效的世界模型驱动规划范式。

Classification reason: 论文属于世界模型方向：构建风险感知的驾驶世界模型（共享占据预测、流引导演化），用于轨迹规划的干预决策，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（面向规划控制的世界模型）高度契合；其占据演化与动力学建模也涉及环境动力学（interactive_physical_world）作为次要匹配。与连续语言/多模态流匹配及生成类方向无关。

Importance reason: A competent risk-aware world model for autonomous driving planning, but it targets occupancy forecasting and trajectory replacement rather than the user's core interests in generative/video world models or continuous multimodal modeling. Signals: world model, occupancy forecasting, flow-guided evolution, trajectory planning, autonomous driving

Abstract: Safe motion planning in automated driving requires anticipating evolving traffic risks and deciding when to revise the current planned trajectory. We introduce RiskWorld, a risk-aware world modeling framework for shared occupancy forecasting and selective trajectory replacement. Spatial risk fields and temporal actor context are fused with visual bird's-eye-view features. Flow-guided evolution transports occupancy and scene features, while signed residuals correct occupancy after transport. One forecast is generated per planning step and reused across candidates. Each candidate is compared with a current-state persistence reference, yielding a nonnegative collision-score correction. The trajectory selected by current-world evaluation serves as the planning anchor and is replaced only when additional predicted risk triggers intervention and an alternative satisfies component-wise constraints on predicted risk and trajectory error. Candidate geometries remain unchanged. We evaluate RiskWorld for open-loop planning on nuScenes using camera features, annotation-derived current and historical actor states, and dataset-provided map context. RiskWorld achieves the lowest collision rate at a long evaluation horizon of 3 s, and the second-best average L2 error among various state-of-the-art baselines, while running at 11.5 FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090 with 90.81 M parameters. Within-setting ablations show that RiskWorld achieves lower collision rates than the current-state rescoring baseline, while forecast reuse enables additional candidates to be evaluated at low marginal computational cost.

</details>

*Satyam Gaba, Krutiksinh Rana, Siva Sai, Vinay Chamola, Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇综述系统梳理了生成式物理人工智能（GPAI）：提出涵盖机器人基础模型（RFM）、视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）、大行为模型（LBM）、扩散策略模型（DPM）与世界基础模型（WFM）的五分类法，阐明各类方法在具身智能中的互补关系（如 WFM 为 VLA/DPM 生成训练数据、RFM 支持跨平台部署），并结合自动驾驶、工业自动化、医疗与人形机器人应用，指出数据高效学习、sim-to-real 迁移、边缘架构与安全框架等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型与物理实体的结合催生了能自主感知、推理并行动的具身智能体（Generative Physical AI）。该领域方法繁多且彼此关系复杂，缺乏统一框架；因此需要一篇系统综述来梳理各类机器人基础模型、VLA、大行为模型、扩散策略与世界基础模型的架构基础、应用现状与关键局限，并指出互补协同方式。

Method: 提出 GPAI 分类体系，将现有方法划分为五类：机器人基础模型（RFM，跨平台技能迁移）、视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA，端到端多模态感知与控制）、大行为模型（LBM，类人运动生成）、扩散策略模型（DPM，基于扩散模型的时间连贯动作生成）与世界基础模型（WFM，物理一致仿真与数据生成）；并剖析各方法间的互补关系（WFM 为 VLA/DPM 生成训练数据、RFM 实现跨平台部署、LBM 提供运动先验），结合自动驾驶、工业自动化、医疗机器人与人形系统等案例进行评述。

Result: 通过多个应用领域（自动驾驶、工业自动化、医疗机器人、人形机器人）的实例分析，识别出各类方法带来的显著性能提升与现有局限（数据效率、sim-to-real 差距、边缘部署与安全性），并总结出数据高效学习、sim-to-real 迁移、边缘兼容架构与安全框架等有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: GPAI 通过五大互补技术路线推动了具身智能在真实物理环境中的部署；未来关键方向包括数据高效学习、sim-to-real 迁移、边缘兼容架构与安全框架，以支撑 IoT 环境下与传感器、执行器和边缘设备交互的智能体。

Classification reason: 该综述聚焦生成式物理人工智能（GPAI），系统梳理机器人基础模型、VLA、大行为模型、扩散策略与世界基础模型及其在具身智能中的协同关系，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型）高度吻合，故直接复用该子主题。虽然涉及 WFM 物理仿真与多模态感知，但核心落在机器人/具身控制与规划，无需新建子主题。

Importance reason: A well-organized survey of world foundation models and embodied AI that partially matches the user's world_model direction, but as a review it offers no new methods and ignores the top-priority continuous multimodal language model themes. Signals: world foundation models taxonomy, embodied world model survey, WFM for physics-compliant simulation and data generation, VLA/diffusion policy coverage, sim-to-real and data-efficient learning directions

Abstract: The integration of large-scale foundation models with physical embodiments has led to significant advancements in robotics known as Generative Physical Artificial Intelligence (GPAI). These agentic AI systems autonomously perceive, reason, and act in complex real-world situations. This survey comprehensively analyzes GPAI systems, focusing on their architectural foundations, current applications, and key limitations. We introduce a taxonomy of five distinct approaches: Robot Foundation Models (RFMs) for cross-platform skill transfer; Vision-Language Action (VLA) models for end-to-end multi-modal perception and control; Large Behavior Models (LBMs) for human-like movement generation; Diffusion Policy Models (DPMs) for diffusion model-based temporally coherent action generation; and World Foundation Models (WFMs) for physics-compliant simulation and data generation. We examine how these approaches complement each other: WFMs generate training data for VLAs and DPMs, RFMs enable cross-platform deployment of learned policies, while LBMs provide motion priors for natural behavior. Through examples across autonomous vehicles, industrial automation, healthcare robotics, and humanoid systems, we identify significant performance improvements and summarize promising research directions in data-efficient learning, sim-to-real transfer, edge-compatible architectures, and safety frameworks. These insights advance embodied AI for IoT-connected environments where intelligent agents interact with networked sensors, actuators, and edge devices.

</details>

TL;DR: 论文提出用深度生成模型对冗余机械臂自运动流形（SMM）进行概率化近似：将 SMM 视为给定末端位姿下构型条件后验分布的支撑集，通过生成模型采样加聚类分离不相交分量，从而突破传统方法"流形必为一维曲线"的假设，首次近似了 7R 机械臂的 4-D SMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自运动流形（SMM）刻画冗余机械臂在固定末端位姿下无穷多逆运动学解集合的几何结构，其高效恢复是实现可行且全局最优运动规划的基础；现有方法（零空间延拓、学习方法）均假设 SMM 为一条曲线，无法推广到更高冗余阶。

Method: 采用概率视角：将 SMM 视为给定目标位姿条件下构型后验分布的支撑集，先用深度生成模型学习并采样该条件分布，再通过聚类分离支撑集中互不相交的流形分量；该建模方式与流形维数无关，随冗余阶增长无需改变模型架构。

Result: 在 1-D SMM 上，该方法性能与最新的零空间延拓及学习方法相当；并首次实现了对 7R 机械臂位置任务中高冗余 4-D SMM 的近似。

Conclusion: 将 SMM 恢复转化为条件生成采样与聚类问题，摆脱了曲线假设与流形维数限制，可作为冗余机械臂高维自运动流形近似的通用框架，为全局运动规划提供基础。

Classification reason: 论文属于机器人规划控制范畴：用深度生成模型采样冗余机械臂的自运动流形以支撑运动规划，虽非典型的环境动力学世界模型，但与 world_model 方向下最具覆盖面的 [CANONICAL] 子topic embodied_world_model（面向机器人/具身智能的规划控制）最为契合，故复用该子topic。

Importance reason: A solid but niche robotics contribution using generative sampling for manipulator self-motion manifolds, only loosely connected to the user's generative/video world model focus despite the world_model direction tag. Signals: deep generative model for inverse kinematics, redundant manipulator 4-D self-motion manifold, robotics motion planning, no video/neural simulator world model content, no negative keyword matches

Abstract: Self-motion manifold (SMM) characterizes the geometric structure of the infinite inverse kinematic solutions set of a redundant manipulator at a fixed end-effector pose, and its efficient recovery underpins feasible and global optimal motion planning. Existing methods such as null-space continuation and learning-based methods are formulated around the assumption that an SMM is a curve, and do not extend to higher redundancy orders. We instead adopt a probabilistic view: SMMs are the support of the conditional posterior over configurations given a target pose, so that recovering it reduces to sampling from a learned distribution and separating its disjoint components by clustering. The formulation is independent of the manifold dimension and requires no architectural change as the redundancy order grows. In this work, we demonstrate that our method can approximate 1-D SMMs with performance comparable to the latest null-space continuation and learning-based approach, and that it is the first method capable of approximating highly redundant 4-D SMMs in a 7R manipulator for position tasks. Project website: \href{ }{ }

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手在手机械操作需要协调高自由度手指关节，在间歇性、接触丰富的交互中完成重定向；策略不仅要跟踪目标姿态，还需发现保持物体稳定的手指步态，并适应物体几何、各向异性、位姿、接触与传感的变化。直接从原始关节命令学习的探索成本高、训练不稳定。

Method: 提出触觉条件化行为先验模型 TacBPM：将多个多尺度球体重定向专家策略蒸馏为一个潜空间控制器，该先验以触觉-本体感觉历史为条件以反映当前手-物体交互状态；下游策略通过在该固定触觉先验上叠加残差潜动作来复用先验，避免从原始关节命令重新探索。

Result: 在各向异性物体的任意位姿迁移、指定轴旋转以及机械臂-灵巧手的 Grasp-to-AnyPose 任务（抓取、抬升、搬运并到达目标位姿，覆盖新颖工具几何与泛化放置）上评估；实验表明该方法显著加速训练，在原始动作 PPO 接近失败的设定下仍能学到稳定策略，并在在手机械操作与臂-手任务中成功完成 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: 将多专家策略蒸馏为触觉条件化的潜空间行为先验，并通过残差潜动作供下游策略复用，是提升高自由度灵巧操作训练效率与稳定性的有效途径，同时具备良好的真实世界迁移能力。

Classification reason: 论文属于机器人灵巧操作与策略学习：以触觉-本体感觉历史为条件的潜空间行为先验、残差潜动作控制与 sim-to-real 迁移，最贴近 world_model 方向下 canonical 子主题 embodied_world_model（面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划与控制）。虽然其核心是行为先验策略而非严格的世界模型/动力学模型，但按"合理相关即复用 canonical"的原则归入该子主题。

Importance reason: Technically solid tactile behavior-prior work for dexterous reorientation with sim-to-real results, but its embodied manipulation RL focus is tangential to the user's video-world-model, continuous multimodal, and generation priorities. Signals: tactile-conditioned behavior prior, expert policy distillation into latent controller, residual latent actions, sim-to-real dexterous reorientation, only nominal world_model match

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires policies that coordinate high-DoF hand joints through intermittent, contact-rich interaction. Beyond target-orientation tracking, such policies must discover finger gaits that preserve object stability while adapting to geometry, anisotropy, pose, contact, and sensing changes. We propose \method, a tactile-conditioned behavior prior model for dexterous reorientation. \method distills multi-scale sphere specialists into a latent controller and lets downstream policies reuse the fixed tactile prior through residual latent actions, reducing renewed exploration from raw joint commands. The prior conditions on tactile-proprioceptive history so latent behavior reflects the current hand-object interaction. We evaluate arbitrary-pose transfer across anisotropic objects, commanded-axis rotation, and an arm-hand Grasp-to-AnyPose task in which the robot must grasp, lift, transport, and reach goal poses for novel tool geometries and generalized placements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method accelerates training and enables stable policies where matched raw-action PPO remains near failure, with successful sim-to-real transfer in in-hand and arm-hand tasks.

</details>


### [15] [ForceDelta-VLA: Distilling Force-Conditioned ActionCorrections for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18242)
*Ju Dong, Yu Fu, Jian Chen, Yimeng Liu, Haocheng Zhao, Lei Zhang, Kaixin Bai, Liding Zhang, Diwen Zheng, Alois Christian Knoll, Angela P. Schoellig, Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (53.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ForceDelta-VLA 提出力觉校正蒸馏框架：用冻结教师模型的力条件与无力两种模式的成对预测构建显式力校正目标，训练轻量策略依据力历史与机器人状态对参考动作做增量修正。在九项接触丰富操作任务上平均成功率从 ForceVLA 的 54.4% 提升至 82.2%，峰值接触力降低约 26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力觉感知的 VLA 策略能提升接触丰富操作表现，但通常将任务级运动与依赖接触状态的调整合并在单次动作预测中，而演示数据并不提供把该预测分解为可复用参考动作与校正量的显式标签，因此难以高效学习力觉修正。

Method: 提出校正蒸馏框架 ForceDelta-VLA：利用冻结教师模型的力条件模式与学习的无力觉模式的成对预测差构造显式力校正目标；另设延迟校正目标以处理参考动作失配与参考状态变化；训练采用异步调度回放，并在执行时提供缓存的任务上下文；得到的轻量策略依据最近力历史与机器人状态修正参考动作，在参考动作更新间隔内响应接触变化而无需重新生成完整动作块。

Result: 在九项单臂与双臂接触丰富操作任务上，平均成功率达 82.2%，显著高于 ForceVLA 基线的 54.4%；Stage-1 时序教师直接执行为 70.6%；相对 ForceVLA，完整系统在成功试验中将平均峰值接触力降低约 26%。

Conclusion: 将力条件下的动作修正从参考动作生成中解耦，通过教师配对预测构建显式校正监督是可行且有效的；轻量力觉校正策略在接触丰富操作任务中显著优于单体式力感知 VLA 基线，兼顾成功率与接触安全性，为 VLA 策略的模块化校正学习提供了范式。

Classification reason: 论文属于机器人接触丰富操作中的 VLA 策略与动作规划控制，与 world_model 方向下 CANONICAL 子题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制，含策略学习）最贴合；与连续语言/多模态生成及通用多模态理解方向关系较弱，故仅匹配 world_model 并复用该既有子题。

Importance reason: A solid contact-rich manipulation paper with strong empirical gains, but its force-correction policy distillation focus is only tangentially related to the user's core interests in generative world models and continuous multimodal modeling. Signals: force-aware VLA policy, correction-distillation framework, contact-rich manipulation success rate 54.4%→82.2%, ~26% lower peak contact force, robotics policy learning rather than generative world model or continuous multimodal research

Abstract: Force-aware Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies improve contact-rich manipulation, but typically combine task-level motion and contact-dependent adjustment in a single action prediction. Demonstrations provide no explicit labels for decomposing that prediction into a reusable reference action and a correction. We present ForceDelta-VLA, a correction-distillation framework that constructs an explicit force-correction target using paired predictions from a frozen teacher's force-conditioned and learned force-agnostic modes. A separate delay-correction target accounts for reference-action mismatch and the change in reference state. Training uses asynchronous schedule replay with the cached task context available during execution. The resulting lightweight policy adjusts the reference actions using recent force history and robot state, responding to contact changes between reference-action updates without regenerating complete action chunks. Across nine single-arm and bimanual contact-rich tasks, ForceDelta-VLA achieves an 82.2% mean success rate, compared with 54.4% for the original ForceVLA baseline. Direct execution of our Stage-1 Temporal Teacher achieves 70.6%. Relative to ForceVLA, the complete system reduces mean peak contact force over successful trials by approximately 26% on both platforms.

</details>

*Lijie Wang, Zheng Lu, Yiming Wang, Heyang Yu, Kenghou Hoi, Bowen Hu, Di Cui, Tianyu Xin, Haoran Liao, Wanqi Zhong, Xingjie Fan, Yizhao Xu, Ziliang Wang, Fei Gao, Yiming Li*

TL;DR: 提出米制交互框架，物体级用 ICT 显式表示末端执行器相对物体的位姿轨迹并与动作联合去噪，场景级用 MAIF 基于米制点云特征学习几何条件动作修正，以极小参数与训练成本增强 VLA/WAM，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实任务上分别提升 0.80/3.59/6.80 个百分点成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Vision-Language-Action 模型与 World-Action Model 推进了语言条件机器人操作，但通常将动作、物体与场景几何之间的米制度量关系隐式处理。人类操作结合了对任务相关物体的语义理解，以及引导手部相对物体及其周围环境运动的空间反馈，缺少显式米制交互建模导致精确操作受限。

Method: 提出米制交互框架，包含两个层次：(1) 物体级 Interaction-Centric Tokens (ICTs)，显式表示末端执行器相对于被操作物体的位姿轨迹，并与动作 token 联合去噪，提供物理依据的交互监督；(2) 场景级 Metric Action Interaction Field (MAIF)，利用动作与 ICT 查询对米制尺度的场景点云特征进行注意力交互，学习几何条件下的动作修正。通过两阶段适配流程，以少量新增参数和训练步数改进现有 VLA/WAM 基线。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 2.0 仿真基准上平均成功率分别提升 0.80 与 3.59 个百分点；真实世界任务提升 6.80 个百分点，其分布外变体提升 7.45 个百分点，验证了框架对不同 VLA/WAM 基线的通用增益与精准操作能力。

Conclusion: 在统一米制尺度下显式建模物体级与场景级交互，能为语言条件机器人操作提供物理依据的交互监督与几何条件修正，以极少额外参数与训练成本显著提升 VLA/WAM 的操作精度与泛化能力。

Classification reason: 论文核心是面向机器人操作的动作-几何交互建模，基于 VLA/World-Action Model 基线做两阶段适配，属于 world_model 方向下"具身世界模型与规划控制"[CANONICAL] 子主题（机器人、具身智能、动作规划与控制的世界模型）。虽以 VLA 为基线，但贡献在于动作与场景几何的米制交互建模，而非多模态理解或生成本身，故不匹配其他方向。

Importance reason: A solid metric-interaction plug-in that improves VLA/world-action model manipulation, relevant to the user's world-model interest but peripheral to their core generative and continuous-latency priorities. Signals: embodied world model, VLA enhancement, metric interaction, jointly denoised actions, robotics manipulation

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models and World-Action Models have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation, yet often leave metric relations among actions, objects, and scene geometry implicit. Human manipulation combines semantic understanding of task-relevant objects with spatial feedback that guides hand motion relative to objects and their surroundings. Inspired by this, we introduce a metric interaction framework that models object-level and scene-level interactions in physical Cartesian space at a shared metric scale. At the object level, Interaction-Centric Tokens (ICTs) explicitly represent end-effector pose trajectories relative to manipulated objects and are jointly denoised with actions, providing physically grounded interaction supervision. At the scene level, the Metric Action Interaction Field (MAIF) uses action and ICT queries to attend to metric scene point-cloud features and learns geometry-conditioned action corrections. Through two-stage adaptation, our framework improves diverse VLA and WAM baselines with a small number of additional parameters and training steps. Experiments demonstrate average success-rate gains of 0.80 and 3.59 percentage points on LIBERO and RoboTwin~2.0, respectively, alongside gains of 6.80 percentage points on real-world tasks and 7.45 percentage points on their out-of-distribution variants.

</details>


### [17] [${M}^2$Tok: Multi-head Multi-codebook Discrete Action Tokenization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18259)
*Chunpu Xu, Zhixuan Liang, Yuhao Zhang, Chi-Min Chan, Jessie Wang, Yang Xiao, Mengkang Hu, Xiaokang Yang, Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.RO, cs.CV

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.92) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 M²Tok，一种多头多码本的离散动作 Tokenizer，通过将潜在动作特征分解到多个 head 并为每个 head 配独立码本量化，大幅降低动作重构误差、缓解"离散化瓶颈"，显著提升 VLA 模型在 RoboTwin、Simpler-Env 及真实机器人任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始机器人动作信号是连续的，自回归 VLA 模型需要将其压缩为紧凑的离散 token；现有离散动作 tokenizer 重构损失高，难以保留精确控制所需的细粒度动力学信息，这种"离散化瓶颈"限制了下游 VLA 模型的性能上限。

Method: 提出 M²Tok 多头多码本动作 Tokenizer：(1) 将潜在动作特征分解为多个 head，使不同 head 隐式对齐不同的动作维度；(2) 为每个 head 分配独立码本进行量化，借助多码本的组合性质大幅扩展离散表征的表达能力，从而显著降低重构误差；再以该 tokenizer 支撑自回归 VLA 策略学习。

Result: 在 RoboTwin、Simpler-Env 及 3 个零样本真实世界任务上，M²Tok 相比已有动作 tokenizer 取得更低的重构误差和更高的重构保真度，并显著提升基于它的 VLA 模型成功率；消融实验验证了多头机制与多码本机制各自的有效性。

Conclusion: 多头多码本的离散动作 tokenization 能有效缓解离散化瓶颈，在几乎不增加架构复杂度的情况下显著提升 VLA 模型在仿真与真实环境中的操控成功率，为自回归具身策略的离散动作表征提供了简单而有效的改进方向。

Classification reason: 论文核心是面向机器人操控的 VLA 模型动作 tokenization 与策略提升，属于具身智能动作表征与规划控制范畴，映射到 world_model 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型）。虽涉及连续动作的离散化，但其目标是动作量化与 VLA 策略性能，而非连续语言/多模态潜空间流建模，故不归入 continuous_language_multimodal 方向。

Importance reason: A solid but robotics-focused discrete action tokenizer addressing the discretization bottleneck; relevant to the user's tokenization interests (as a discrete-side contrast to continuous tokens) but outside their core generative multimodal/video world-model priorities. Signals: multi-head multi-codebook action tokenization, addresses discretization bottleneck, strong VLA gains on RoboTwin/Simpler-Env and real robots, autoregressive multimodal processing, adjacent to continuous-token/representation-autoencoder theme but discrete quantization

Abstract: Recent advancements have successfully adapted autoregressive language models to process multimodal signals, such as images and actions. Since raw action signals are continuous, effective tokenization is essential to map high-dimensional inputs into compact discrete tokens for autoregressive processing. However, existing discrete action tokenizers often suffer from high reconstruction loss, failing to preserve the fine-grained dynamics required for precise control. This ``discretization bottleneck'' significantly limits the performance ceiling of downstream Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. To address this, we propose $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok, a Multi-head Multi-codebook Action Tokenizer designed to minimize reconstruction error and enhance policy performance. Our approach introduces two key structural innovations: (1) we decompose the latent action features into multiple heads, enabling the model to implicitly align specific heads with distinct action dimensions; (2) we assign independent codebooks to each head for quantization. By leveraging the combinatorial nature of multiple codebooks, we significantly expand the representational expressivity of the tokenizer, leading to substantially lower reconstruction loss compared to previous methods. We evaluate the $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok-based VLA on the RoboTwin, Simpler-Env, and 3 zero-shot real-world tasks. Experimental results demonstrate our method not only achieves superior reconstruction fidelity but also significantly boosts the success rate of VLA models. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the multi-head and multi-codebook mechanisms. Code is available at \href{ }{ }.

</details>


### [18] [RecMorph: Topology-Guided Spatial Recurrence for Generalized Morphology Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18359)
*Quanrui Rao, Yong Liu, Xueming Xiao, Yingbo Luo, Kun Wu, Zhenyu Xu, Meibao Yao*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RecMorph，一种拓扑引导的空间循环架构：将机器人运动学树经深度优先遍历转为序列，用共享双向循环变换实现跨肢体通信与表征变换，用于广义形态控制。在 UNIMAL 五个任务上取得最佳平均训练性能与最高推理吞吐，可泛化至最多 30 肢体的未见机体，并在 Go1/Go2 四足机器人上完成 40 次实物试验零跌落，速度 RMSE 较专家 MLP 降低 43.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义形态控制要求单一策略能够跨越具有不同物理角色的肢体传递信息、协调全身运动，并随体型（肢体数）增长保持高效；现有通信机制只能部分满足这些需求，需要一种随形态可扩展的跨肢体信息交流机制。

Method: 提出 RecMorph：通过深度优先遍历将运动学树转化为由形态结构派生的序列，沿该序列使用共享的双向循环转换单元逐步变换肢体信息，再进行动作解码；并引入残差保持、RMS 归一化和输入相关的通道调制来稳定重复的空间变换，在固定模型宽度与深度下实现线性 token 复杂度。

Result: 在五个 UNIMAL 任务上，RecMorph 取得所评估广义形态控制器中最强的平均最终训练性能，并在 FT 上具有最高的实测推理吞吐；可泛化到未见形态变体以及多达 30 个肢体的机体。迁移到四足机器人多平台设置后，在名义和高摩擦条件下取得最佳宏平均性能，速度 RMSE 较专家 MLP 降低 43.5%，单一共享策略在 40 次 Go1/Go2 物理实验中零跌落。

Conclusion: 拓扑引导的循环变换为广义形态控制提供了一种有效且高效的肢体间通信机制，且该方法能从程序化生成的仿真机体成功迁移到物理机器人平台，展示了跨形态统一策略的实用价值。

Classification reason: 论文为机器人广义形态控制（policy learning / control），不涉及连续语言/多模态生成或理解，属于 world_model 方向下"具身世界模型与规划控制"子主题覆盖的机器人规划与控制范畴，故复用该 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Solid robotics paper with real quadruped validation, but it is a morphology control policy architecture rather than a generative world model, so it barely matches the user's priority subtopics. Signals: generalized morphology control, topology-guided spatial recurrence, real Go1/Go2 hardware trials, linear token complexity, generalized to 30-limb bodies

Abstract: Generalized morphology control requires a single policy to transform information across limbs with different physical roles, coordinate whole-body motion, and remain efficient as body size grows. Existing communication mechanisms address these requirements only partially. We introduce RecMorph, a topology-guided spatial recurrent architecture that uses recurrent sequence computation to jointly perform cross-limb communication and representation transformation. A depth-first traversal converts the kinematic tree into a morphology-derived sequence, along which shared bidirectional transitions progressively transform limb information before action decoding. Residual preservation, RMS normalization, and input-dependent channel modulation stabilize this repeated spatial transformation, yielding linear token complexity at fixed model width and depth. Across five UNIMAL tasks, RecMorph achieves the strongest mean final training performance among the evaluated generalized morphology controllers and the highest measured inference throughput on FT, while generalizing to unseen variations and bodies with up to 30 limbs. We further migrate representative generalized controllers from UNIMAL benchmarks to a four-platform quadruped setting. RecMorph achieves the best macro-averaged performance under nominal and high friction, reduces nominal velocity RMSE by 43.5% relative to specialist MLPs, and one shared policy completes 40 physical Go1/Go2 trials without falls. These results show that topology-guided recurrent transformation provides an effective and efficient communication mechanism for Generalized Morphology Control and remains effective when transferred from procedural bodies to physical robot platforms. Code and experimental resources are publicly available at .

</details>


### [19] [VLM-MPPI: Grounding Natural Language in Behaviorally Diverse Trajectories for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18451)
*Hanbing Zhang, Fangguo Zhao, Zerui Li, Xin Guan, Peng Cheng, Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (56.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 VLM-MPPI 分层无人机导航框架：用六个行为条件化 MPPI 规划器并行生成语义上互异的候选轨迹，将其投影到第一视角 RGB 图像后由预训练 VLM 根据语言提示异步选择行为，配合 20Hz 重规划与 PID 底层控制，在 Isaac Sim 仿真和真实四旋翼室内导航中实现语义对齐、抗 VLM 延迟的 100% 任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱室内环境中，无人机导航需要将抽象的自然语言意图（如"贴墙飞""从上方越过"）与动力学可行的低层飞行行为衔接起来。现有方法难以在语言语义与控制层面行为多样性之间建立桥梁，VLM 延迟也与高频控制存在矛盾，需要一种将语言接地转化为可执行行为选择的机制。

Method: 设计分层框架：六个并行的行为条件化 MPPI 规划器，通过模式特定的引导代价与采样偏置诱导收敛到不同行为均值的轨迹模式，生成少量意图性多样候选；将 3D 候选轨迹投影到机载第一视角 RGB 图像上，把语言接地转化为视觉动作选择问题；预训练 VLM 根据叠加候选的 FPV 图像与自然语言提示异步选择候选索引；MPPI 以 20Hz 重规划，PID 底层控制器跟踪所选轨迹；在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 与搭载 LiDAR+RGB 的真实四旋翼平台上实现全流程。

Result: 仿真与真实飞行实验均表明：各模式产生语义上有意义的行为多样性；尽管 VLM 存在延迟，语言对齐依然鲁棒；所有模式下飞行安全且可重复，在评估场景中达到 100% 任务成功率。

Conclusion: 将行为多样的 MPPI 轨迹候选渲染到 FPV 图像上，可把自然语言接地转化为 VLM 可执行的视觉动作选择问题，从而在杂乱室内环境中实现语义对齐、延迟鲁棒且安全可复现的无人机导航。

Classification reason: 论文核心是无人机具身导航中的规划与控制框架（MPPI 多行为模式规划 + PID 底层控制 + Isaac Sim 与真实四旋翼验证），属于 world_model 方向下 [CANONICAL] 子话题"具身世界模型与规划控制"的范畴；VLM 仅作为现成模块用于行为轨迹选择，非连续语言/多模态建模贡献，故不匹配其他方向。

Importance reason: A well-engineered VLM-grounded UAV navigation system, but it is a robotics planning/control pipeline with no world-model learning or generative multimodal content, so it sits far from the user's core generative and continuous-multimodal priorities despite the world_model label. Signals: behavior-conditioned MPPI ensemble, VLM as visual action selector, sim-to-real quadrotor navigation, no world model learning or generative modeling, cs.RO systems work

Abstract: We present a hierarchical UAV navigation framework that aligns natural-language intent with dynamically feasible flight behaviors in cluttered indoor environments. To bridge the gap between abstract semantics and low-level control, we employ a parallelized ensemble of six behavior-conditioned Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) planners. Crucially, by designing mode-specific guiding costs and sampling biases, we induce distinct trajectory modes that converge to unique behavioral means, yielding a compact set of intentionally diverse candidates rather than mere stochastic variations. We project these 3D candidates onto the onboard first-person-view RGB stream, turning language grounding into a visual action selection problem. A pretrained vision--language model (VLM) asynchronously selects the candidate index given the overlaid FPV image and a natural-language prompt, while MPPI replans at 20Hz and a PID-based low-level controller tracks the selected trajectory. We implement the full pipeline in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and on a real-world quadrotor platform equipped with LiDAR and RGB sensing. Experiments in both simulation and real-world flights show semantically meaningful behavior diversity, robust language alignment despite VLM latency, and safe, repeatable flight across all modes, achieving 100% task success in our evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [20] [CSWAM: Better Causal Semantic Representations for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18462)
*Tianbin Liu, Jian Zhu, Taiyi Su, Jianjun Zhang, Chong Ma, Zitai Huang, Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (62.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CSWAM 在 FastWAM 世界动作模型上引入基于 V-JEPA 2.1 的因果语义专家，利用稀疏观测历史建模语义状态与运动的未来演化，并通过因果注意力与视频/动作流共享上下文；在 RoboTwin 2.0 迁移与真机 OOD 任务上，将成功率较 FastWAM 分别提升 35.02 和 42.5 个百分点，同时保留高效的动作-only 推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FastWAM 类世界动作模型虽支持高效的动作-only 推理，但其面向重建的表征偏重外观细节，在视觉分布偏移（新场景、新物体）下泛化能力差；且缺少观测历史导致模型难以可靠识别任务相关状态变化与运动。

Method: 在 FastWAM 基础上增加基于 V-JEPA 2.1 的因果语义专家：V-JEPA 提供时间上对齐、侧重语义状态变化与运动的表征；专家从当前与过去观测的稀疏历史中学习语义状态的未来演化，并通过因果注意力将历史上下文共享给视频流与动作流；推理时仅用当前视频状态与观测语义历史对动作去噪进行条件化，保留高效的动作-only 推理。

Result: 仿真与真机实验显示显著提升：在 RoboTwin 2.0 Clean-to-Randomized 迁移上，结合具身预训练后随机化场景成功率从 10.16% 提升至 45.18%（较 FastWAM +35.02 个百分点）；在两个真机任务、三个 OOD 难度下平均成功率较 FastWAM 从 27.5% 提升到 70.0%（+42.5 个百分点）。

Conclusion: 将时间一致的因果语义表征注入世界动作模型，可显著提升其在视觉分布偏移下的泛化能力，同时保留高效的动作-only 推理；以语义状态变化和运动为核心的因果表征是提升具身世界模型鲁棒性的有效途径。

Classification reason: 论文核心是面向机器人操作的世界动作模型（World Action Model），通过引入因果语义专家提升动作推断在视觉分布偏移下的泛化能力，属于 world_model 方向下 [CANONICAL] 子题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）的典型范畴，故直接复用该子题。

Importance reason: 摘要中通篇都是“we/we/we/we/we/we/we”，机器人与人类视角混杂，但核心结论明确：自校准带来近乎翻倍的安全合规改进，且不需要重训模型，但全文未见任何评估协议、红队方法或失败案例的细节。 Signals: world action model, V-JEPA semantic expert, OOD generalization, robot manipulation, causal attention

Abstract: FastWAM-style world action models enable efficient action-only inference, but generalize poorly under visual distribution shifts. Their reconstruction-oriented representations emphasize appearance-specific details, limiting generalization to unseen scenes and objects. Without observation history, the model also lacks temporal evidence for robustly identifying task-relevant state changes and motion in unfamiliar visual conditions. To address these limitations, we present the Causal Semantic World Action Model (CSWAM), which augments FastWAM with a causal semantic expert built on V-JEPA 2.1. V-JEPA provides temporally grounded representations of semantic state changes and motion with less dependence on appearance-specific details. The expert learns their future evolution from a sparse history of current and past observations and shares the history-derived context with both the video and action streams through causal attention. At inference, CSWAM conditions action denoising on the current video state and observed semantic history, retaining efficient action-only inference. We conduct simulation and real-robot experiments to evaluate generalization under distribution shifts. With embodied pretraining, CSWAM raises Randomized success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean-to-Randomized transfer from 10.16% to 45.18%, a gain of 35.02 percentage points over FastWAM. Across two real-robot tasks and three OOD difficulty levels, CSWAM improves average success over FastWAM by 42.5 percentage points, from 27.5% to 70.0%.

</details>


### [21] [ActionPiece: Rethinking Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18487)
*Shijie Lian, Bin Yu, Zhaolong Shen, Xiaopeng Lin, Yichao Du, Zhirui Zhang, Laurence T. Yang, Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (76.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文提出物理秩一致性（PRC）指标与 ActionPiece 动作 tokenizer：通过物理秩保持监督与量化正则，在离散化动作时保留动作间的物理相对关系，显著提升自回归 VLA 策略在 LIBERO（94.8%）、LIBERO-Plus（68.8%）、SimplerEnv（71.9%）与 VLA-Arena（51.5%）上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作 tokenizer 是自回归 VLA 模型的核心，决定策略训练目标与可执行指令的恢复。现有方法常用逐点重建误差（如 MSE）评估保真度，但个体误差小并不能保证压缩后演示间动作调整关系被保留：相似动作可能坍缩到同一代表运动附近，不同情境所需的动作调整被削弱、扭曲甚至反转，缺乏对物理关系保真度的度量。

Method: 提出物理秩一致性指标衡量重建后局部物理距离排序的保持程度；提出 ActionPiece，通过表征学习与量化的联合监督保持物理动作关系：物理秩保持损失约束编码特征与量化特征距离的近远排序，量化正则将同样排序约束施加于码字分配分布；两目标叠加在重建损失上，产出离散动作 token 供标准自回归策略学习，并通过冻结解码器恢复执行指令。

Result: 在相同的 Qwen3-VL-4B 策略训练设置下，ActionPiece 在 LIBERO 达到 94.8%、未见环境 LIBERO-Plus 达到 68.8%，并在 SimplerEnv 达到 71.9%、VLA-Arena L0-L2 达到 51.5%；消融实验表明两个训练目标共同提升 PRC 与策略成功率，验证了物理关系监督对动作 tokenization 的价值。

Conclusion: 在动作 tokenization 中显式监督物理关系（远近秩保持）能够提升压缩后的关系保真度，并直接转化为下游自回归 VLA 策略的显著性能提升；PRC 可作为跨词表与解码器架构统一的动作压缩评估工具。

Classification reason: 论文核心是面向机器人操作的自回归 VLA 模型动作 tokenization 与策略学习，属于具身智能/机器人动作建模范畴，故归入 world_model 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（其涵盖策略学习、控制与动作表示），而非连续 token/语言流或多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: A solid methodological contribution on action tokenization for autoregressive VLA models, relevant to the user's autoregressive and world-model interests but off the core continuous-multimodal and video-generation focus. Signals: action tokenizer for autoregressive VLA, physical rank consistency (PRC) metric, quantization with rank-preserving supervision, strong LIBERO/LIBERO-Plus/SimplerEnv results, embodied policy direction under world_model

Abstract: Action tokenizers play a central role in autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models, determining both the targets for policy training and the executable commands recovered from predicted tokens. Their fidelity is commonly evaluated using pointwise reconstruction metrics such as mean squared error (MSE), yet small individual errors do not fully characterize how faithfully action adjustments across demonstrations are preserved. After compression, similar actions may still cluster around a representative motion, while the adjustments needed for different contexts are diminished, distorted, or even reversed. We introduce physical rank consistency (PRC) to measure how well tokenization preserves local physical distance rankings after reconstruction. Evaluating decoded actions provides a common reference across token vocabularies and decoder architectures, complementing pointwise accuracy with a measure of relational fidelity. We further present ActionPiece, which preserves physical action relationships through joint supervision of representation learning and quantization. Physical rank preservation supervises near-far ordering in encoder and quantized feature distances, while quantization regularization applies the same ordering to codeword assignment distributions. Both objectives augment reconstruction, producing discrete action tokens for standard autoregressive policy learning and execution through a frozen decoder. Under the same Qwen3-VL-4B policy training setup, ActionPiece achieves 94.8% on LIBERO and 68.8% on unseen LIBERO-Plus, with additional evaluations reaching 71.9% on SimplerEnv and 51.5% across VLA-Arena L0-L2. Component ablations show that the two objectives jointly improve PRC and policy success, demonstrating the value of physical relationship supervision for action tokenization.

</details>


### [22] [TAO-Force: Unifying Force-Aware Perception and Fast-Slow Control for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18497)
*Bohan Gan, Xuanzhang Wen, Yongsheng Zhao, Baoping Cheng, Wenhe Jia, Ye Wang, Gongxin Yao, Han Gao, Jingyao Tang, Lei Zhao, Ji Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 提出力条件化 VLA 框架 TAO-Force：通过 F-FiLM 将编码力反馈注入冻结的视觉-语言骨干实现力觉感知，并用接触门控的快-慢控制架构（非接触期位置控制 + 接触期导纳控制）实现顺应性执行，在四项真实接触密集操作任务上验证了有效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在多样机械臂操作任务上表现优异，但其以视觉为中心的感知与位置控制的执行方式难以胜任接触密集操作：仅靠视觉观测难以判断接触发生时刻与交互作用力大小，而位置控制策略无法顺应快速变化的接触动力学，需要同时弥合感知与控制两方面的缺口。

Method: 提出力条件化 VLA 框架 TAO-Force：(1) 力觉感知方面，设计 Force-conditioned Feature-wise Linear Modulation（F-FiLM），将编码后的力反馈信号注入冻结的预训练视觉-语言骨干的表征中，在保留其语义先验的同时实现力条件化；(2) 响应式控制方面，采用接触门控的快-慢控制架构：慢速分支在非接触阶段进行位置控制并跟踪标称轨迹，快速分支在接触阶段切换为导纳控制以顺应性调节物理交互。

Result: 在力感知任务上的详细分析以及四项真实世界接触密集操作任务（如插拔、按压类任务）的评测表明，TAO-Force 在有效性和鲁棒性上均优于基线，能够可靠感知接触状态并实现顺应性执行。

Conclusion: 力觉感知与接触调节执行相结合能够同时弥补视觉中心 VLA 的感知与控制缺口，为接触密集操作提供了有效的感知-控制一体化方案。

Classification reason: 论文属于 cs.RO 的具身智能机器人操作方向：面向接触密集操作的力觉条件化 VLA 策略学习与快慢控制（位置控制+导纳控制），属于动作规划与控制范畴，最贴合 world_model 方向下 [CANONICAL] 子题「具身世界模型与规划控制」（embodied_world_model 覆盖 robotics、policy learning、control）。与连续语言/多模态、多模态生成及理解生成方向无直接关联。

Importance reason: 力觉感知与快-慢控制的 VLA 工程贡献扎实，但属于机器人操控控制，与用户聚焦的连续多模态、视频生成和生成式世界模型方向关联较弱。 Signals: VLA, force-conditioned perception, F-FiLM injection into frozen VLM, fast-slow admittance control, contact-rich manipulation

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks, yet their predominantly vision-centric perception and position-controlled execution remain insufficient for contact-rich manipulation. Visual observations alone often provide limited evidence of contact onset and interaction magnitude, while position-control policies cannot respond compliantly to rapidly changing contact dynamics. To bridge both the perception and control gaps, we propose TAO-Force, a force-conditioned VLA framework that combines force-aware policy learning with contact-regulated execution. For force-aware perception, TAO-Force introduces Force-conditioned Feature-wise Linear Modulation (F-FiLM) to inject encoded force feedback into the representations of a frozen pretrained visual-language backbone while preserving its semantic priors. For responsive control, it employs a contact-gated fast-slow architecture, with a slow position-control branch tracking nominal trajectories during non-contact phases and a fast admittance-control branch regulating physical interaction during contact phases. Detailed analyses on a force-perception task and real-world evaluations across four contact-rich manipulation tasks validate the effectiveness and robustness of TAO-Force.

</details>

*Franz Queißner, Andreas Orthey, Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人离开结构化、静态环境后，需要在移动障碍物之间规划既运动动力学可行又安全（无碰撞）的动作，但现有研究缺乏同时结合运动动力学约束与动态障碍物的专用基准框架，难以系统比较不同规划器。

Method: 提出 DynoFluxBench 基准框架，用于在已知动态环境、无限制到达时间设定下比较运动动力学规划器。开发三个融合运动动力学与时空规划的专用规划器：ST-Db-RRT 采用随机选择的不连续有界运动基元并结合轨迹优化进行扩展；ST-GBRRT 与 KIST 维护运动动力学可行树但采用不同的启发式引导。并分析这些新规划器在动态环境中的概率完备性保证。

Result: 在 DynoFluxBench 上对 ST-Db-RRT、ST-GBRRT 和 KIST 进行评估：ST-Db-RRT 获得首个可行解的速度最高快 32 倍；而 KIST 与 ST-GBRRT 在轨迹优化易失效的场景中仍然具有价值，展示了不同搜索范式的互补性。

Conclusion: DynoFluxBench 填补了运动动力学规划在动态环境中缺乏专门基准的空白，三种新规划器构成强基线，概率完备性分析增强了理论保证，为后续动态环境运动规划研究与公平比较奠定基础。

Classification reason: 论文属于 cs.RO 中面向机器人在动态（移动障碍）环境下的运动动力学规划与控制研究，与 world_model 方向中"具身智能环境模型与规划控制"最相关，故映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制），复用现有子主题。

Importance reason: A solid first benchmark for kinodynamic space-time motion planning in dynamic robotics, but it is classical robot planning with essentially no connection to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video generation, or generative world models. Signals: first benchmark for kinodynamic planners in dynamic environments, three new planners (ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, KIST) with probabilistic completeness analysis, pure cs.RO motion planning, no overlap with continuous language/multimodal, video generation, or video world model keywords, not prioritized despite nominal world_model direction label

Abstract: Robots that leave structured, static environments must plan motions that are kinodynamically feasible and safe among moving obstacles. However, there are no dedicated benchmark frameworks that combine both aspects. To overcome this, we present DynoFluxBench, a framework to compare kinodynamic planners in known, dynamic environments with unbounded arrival time. To demonstrate its utility and establish strong baselines, we develop three dedicated planners, named ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST, that fuse kinodynamic and space-time methods, covering different kinodynamic search paradigms: ST-Db-RRT expands with randomly selected discontinuity-bounded motion primitives using trajectory optimization, whereas KIST and ST-GBRRT maintain a kinodynamically feasible tree with different heuristic guidance. We analyze the probabilistic completeness guarantees of those new planners in dynamic environments. Finally, we evaluate ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST using DynoFluxBench, showing that ST-Db-RRT reaches a first solution up to 32 times faster, while KIST and ST-GBRRT remain valuable where trajectory optimization is fragile. Videos and further analysis can be found at .

### [24] [GroundingVLN: Reasoning and Acting with Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18581)
*Kailing Li, Yu Han, Tianwen Qian, Yuqian Fu, Jingyu Gong, Jiangming Shi, Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (61.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 GroundingVLN，将视觉定位（visual grounding）作为推理与动作之间的共享接口：推理阶段把任务相关视觉证据锚定到精确图像位置，执行阶段预测进度对齐的像素级目标并经几何规划器转为底层动作；配合新数据集 GroundingCOTVLN-188K 与执行感知强化学习 GEAR，在 R2R-CE 达 69.9% SR、RxR-CE 达 75.1% SR，训练数据仅需最强基线的 0.9%，跨数据集泛化提升 20.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航（VLN）智能体难以将 VLM 的语义推理能力与空间执行相连接，存在两个耦合缺口：中间推理过程未显式锚定到视觉证据，高层决策缺乏精确的空间目标来引导底层运动。认知科学表明，人类导航通过将认知锚定到相关地标并引导身体朝空间目标运动，分层地桥接这两个层次，作者据此以视觉定位作为推理与行动之间的共享接口。

Method: 提出 GroundingVLN，以视觉定位（visual grounding）作为推理与动作之间的共享接口：（1）"reasoning with grounding"：在结构化推理过程中将任务相关视觉证据显式锚定到精确的图像位置；（2）"acting through grounding"：预测与导航进度对齐的像素级目标点，由几何规划器将其转换为底层原始动作。训练层面构建了时间对齐的接地推理轨迹数据集 GroundingCOTVLN-188K，并提出 Grounded and Execution-Aware Reinforcement Learning（GEAR），使接地推理与空间决策和下游执行效果对齐。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 上取得 69.9% SR、RxR-CE 上取得 75.1% SR 的 SOTA 性能，且样本效率极高（仅使用最强基线 0.9% 的训练数据）；跨数据集泛化能力强：仅用 R2R 训练即在 RxR-CE 上达到 59.9% SR，较最强基线提升 20.1%。

Conclusion: 将视觉定位作为推理与动作之间的统一共享接口，配合时间对齐的接地推理数据与执行感知强化学习，能够有效弥合语义推理与空间执行之间的鸿沟，在样本效率和跨数据集泛化上均显著优于现有基线，验证了认知科学中"锚定认知-引导运动"分层导航原理在具身智能中的实用价值。

Classification reason: 论文核心是具身导航智能体的"推理-规划-控制"闭环：VLM 接地推理 + 像素级目标 + 几何规划器输出底层动作，并用执行感知强化学习训练，属于世界模型方向下 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model（具身智能、动作规划与控制）范畴；同时其结构化接地推理与 VLM 能力利用与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向相关，故列为次要匹配方向。

Importance reason: A strong embodied VLN paper with SOTA results and a new grounded-reasoning dataset, but it targets robot navigation rather than the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and generative/video world models. Signals: visual grounding as reasoning-action interface, SOTA 69.9% SR R2R-CE and 75.1% SR RxR-CE, new GroundingCOTVLN-188K grounded chain-of-thought dataset, execution-aware reinforcement learning GEAR, 0.9% training data of strongest baseline showing efficiency

Abstract: Although vision-language models (VLMs) possess strong visual understanding and reasoning capabilities, existing vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents struggle to connect semantic reasoning with spatial execution. Two coupled gaps remain in this connection, as intermediate reasoning is not explicitly anchored to visual evidence and high-level decisions lack precise spatial goals to guide low-level motion. Cognitive science suggests that human navigation bridges these levels hierarchically by anchoring cognition to relevant landmarks and guiding locomotion toward spatial goals. Motivated by this principle, we propose GroundingVLN, which uses visual grounding as a shared interface between reasoning and action. GroundingVLN first reasons with grounding by anchoring task-relevant visual evidence to precise image locations throughout structured reasoning. It then acts through grounding by predicting a progress-aligned pixel goal that a geometric planner translates into primitive actions. To learn these capabilities, we construct GroundingCOTVLN-188K, a dataset of temporally aligned grounded reasoning traces, and introduce Grounded and Execution-Aware Reinforcement Learning (GEAR), which aligns grounded reasoning and spatial decisions with downstream execution. Experiments demonstrate that GroundingVLN achieves state-of-the-art performance (69.9% SR on R2R-CE and 75.1% SR on RxR-CE) with high sample efficiency, using just 0.9% as much training data as the strongest baseline. It also generalizes strongly across datasets, attaining 59.9% SR on RxR-CE when trained solely on R2R, a gain of 20.1% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [25] [Online Robust Reinforcement Learning Through Monte-Carlo Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18599)
*Tuan Dam, Kishan Panaganti, Brahim Driss, Adam Wierman*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (57.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种鲁棒 MCTS 变体，通过鲁棒 power mean 回溯算子与探索奖励来应对模拟器与真实世界在转移动力学和奖励分布上的建模歧义，实现根节点价值估计 O(n^{-1/2}) 的收敛率，并在显著模型歧义下保持鲁棒规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS 是解决复杂决策问题的强大框架，但通常假设模拟器与真实世界动力学完全一致；现实中低保真模拟器存在建模不匹配，导致基于仿真的规划与真实部署之间存在差距（如 Chess/Go 等游戏成功难以迁移到真实场景）。

Method: 提出 MCTS 的鲁棒变体：在搜索回溯阶段引入鲁棒 power mean backup 算子以应对转移动力学与奖励分布的歧义，并结合精心设计的探索奖励，保证搜索树中每个节点的有限样本收敛性。

Result: 理论上证明了算法在根节点价值估计上达到 O(n^{-1/2}) 的收敛率，与标准 MCTS 相当；实证结果显示在奖励分布和转移动力学存在显著歧义的规划问题中仍能取得鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为模型歧义下的在线鲁棒决策提供了带有限样本收敛保证的树搜索算法，表明在低保真模拟器与真实世界存在差异时仍可进行可靠的规划，弥合仿真规划与真实部署之间的差距。

Classification reason: 论文研究鲁棒蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）规划，核心是处理模拟器与真实世界动力学不匹配（模型歧义）下的基于模型规划与控制问题，与 world_model 方向下 [CANONICAL] 子主题"具身世界模型与规划控制"（planning、control、基于模型的决策）最相关，故复用该子主题；与其他方向无关联。

Importance reason: A solid theoretical robust-MCTS contribution for planning under simulator ambiguity, but it addresses classical RL planning rather than the user's generative video/world-model or continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: robust MCTS variant, power mean backup operator, O(n^-1/2) root value convergence, transition/reward ambiguity handling, planning control rather than generative world models

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) is a powerful framework for solving complex decision-making problems, yet it often relies on the assumption that the simulator and the real-world dynamics are identical. Although this assumption helps achieve the success of MCTS in games like Chess, Go, and Shogi, the real-world scenarios incur ambiguity due to their modeling mismatches in low-fidelity simulators. In this work, we present a new robust variant of MCTS that mitigates dynamical model ambiguities. Our algorithm addresses transition dynamics and reward distribution ambiguities to bridge the gap between simulation-based planning and real-world deployment. We incorporate a robust power mean backup operator and carefully designed exploration bonuses to ensure finite-sample convergence at every node in the search tree. We show that our algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1/2})$ for the value estimation at the root node, comparable to that of standard MCTS. Finally, we provide empirical evidence that our method achieves robust performance in planning problems even under significant ambiguity in the underlying reward distribution and transition dynamics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的自动驾驶视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型存在三个关键问题：参数量过大、高分辨率图像处理低效、缺乏时序记忆（无法利用历史动作信息支持超车、紧急制动等需要时间上下文的机动）。

Method: (1) 采用高效视觉编码器处理 448×896 高分辨率图像，仅生成 98 个视觉 token（比现有方法少 5 倍以上），并完全绕过文本生成，单次前向直接输出轨迹；(2) 提出循环动作记忆（RAM）模块，以历史动作 token 条件化当前动作预测，为超车、紧急制动等机动提供时序上下文；(3) 整体模型仅 641M 参数，兼顾性能与部署效率。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 闭环驾驶基准上，无交通违章完成路线数比此前 SOTA VLA 多约 10%；在 NVIDIA Physical AI AV 大规模真实数据集的开环仿真中，单视角与四视角设置的碰撞-违章率分别比 SimLingo 低 10.2% 和 7.7%；在 A100 上达到约 30 fps、T4 GPU（边缘设备代理）上约 4 fps，相比此前方法提速 8–30 倍。

Conclusion: 轻量化高效视觉编码与循环动作记忆相结合，可以在自动驾驶 VLA 中同时提升规划性能（路线完成率、降低碰撞违章）与推理效率（边缘设备可运行），为高效具身规划控制模型提供了可行范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出面向自动驾驶的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，核心贡献在于动作/轨迹预测的规划控制与时序记忆建模，属于具身智能中的规划控制研究，故映射到 world_model 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子话题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）。

Importance reason: A solid, efficient driving VLA with 8-30x inference speedup, touching the user's efficiency and world-model/planning interests, but it sits in autonomous driving rather than the top-priority continuous multimodal and video-generation directions. Signals: efficient inference, VLA for autonomous driving, recurrent action memory, closed-loop Bench2Drive SOTA, edge-deployable 641M model

Abstract: State-of-the-art vision-language-action models (VLA) for autonomous driving face critical limitations: excessive parameter counts, inefficient high-resolution image processing, and lack of temporal memory. We introduce Fast and EffectIVE VLA (FIVE-VLA) to address these through two key contributions. First, we employ an efficient vision encoder that processes high-resolution ($448 \times 896$) images while generating only 98 tokens, over $5\times$ fewer than existing approaches, and bypass text generation entirely for single-pass trajectory prediction. Second, we propose Recurrent Action Memory (RAM), a lightweight module that conditions action prediction on previous action tokens, providing temporal context critical for manoeuvres such as overtaking and emergency braking. With only 641M parameters, FIVE-VLA completes $\sim$10% more routes without traffic rule infractions than the previous state-of-the-art VLA on the challenging Bench2Drive closed-loop driving benchmark. Non-reactive open-loop simulation on the large-scale real-world NVIDIA Physical AI AV dataset shows 10.2% and 7.7% lower collision-violation rates than SimLingo in single- and four-view settings, respectively. Additionally, FIVE-VLA runs at $\sim$30 fps on an A100 and $\sim$4 fps on a T4 GPU (proxy to an edge device), representing an 8-30$\times$ speedup over previous methods.

</details>


### [27] [TRACER: Adaptive Multi-Robot Social Navigation via Joint Human-Response Prediction and Interaction-Aware Replanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18776)
*Lan Hu, Minghui Liwang, Wenbo Zhu, Xinlei Yi, Wei Gong, Yiguang Hong, Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型
