TL;DR: TRACER 是一个面向多机器人社交导航的双向滚动时域框架：通过逐实体概率响应模型捕捉非可加的多机器人-行人交互效应，执行轨迹前缀后基于身份绑定的信念更新行人响应模式，再在概率安全与响应感知代价准则下重规划，形成预测-适应闭环，在 SocialGym2 上提升了无碰撞完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在人类共享空间中的导航本质上是交互性的：机器人团队的协同运动会 影响 周围人的反应，而这些反应又为后续决策提供信息。现有方法通常将动作条件预测、多机器人规划与在线适应分开处理，缺乏统一机制来建模机器人-行人的联合交互并利用已执行交互结果进行在线适应。

Method: 提出 TRACER 双向滚动时域框架：1) 用逐实体概率响应模型评估候选机器人团队轨迹，将单机器人效应与非可加成对交互效应分离建模；2) 执行所选轨迹前缀后，利用同步观测的人类响应更新持久绑定身份（identity-bound）的潜在响应模式信念；3) 更新后的信念在概率安全约束与响应感知代价准则下指导后续候选轨迹评估，实现预测与适应的闭环。在 SocialGym2 多机器人社交导航基准上实验验证。

Result: 实验表明：(i) 相比容量匹配的可加预测器，TRACER 更准确捕捉非可加的多机器人交互效应；(ii) 持久且身份一致的证据累积显著改善响应预测与下游重规划；(iii) 完整 TRACER 框架在 SocialGym2 基准上相比独立机器人基线提升了无碰撞完成率。

Conclusion: TRACER 证明了将动作条件响应预测、多机器人规划与在线适应统一到同一预测-适应闭环中的价值：非可加交互建模与持久身份绑定信念更新能显著提升人机共享空间中多机器人社交导航的安全性与完成质量。

Classification reason: 论文属于多机器人在人机共享空间中的规划与控制研究：其核心包括动作条件的人类响应预测模型（即环境动力学预测）与基于执行结果的在线信念更新和重规划，与世界模型方向下 [CANONICAL] 子主题"具身世界模型与规划控制"（面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的预测-决策模型）最为契合；与连续语言/多模态模型、多模态生成、多模态理解与生成方向无关，故仅匹配 world_model 一个方向。

Importance reason: A solid multi-robot social navigation planning paper, but it only tangentially touches the user's world-model interests and has no generative multimodal or video world-model content. Signals: multi-robot social navigation, human-response prediction, receding-horizon replanning, belief updating over latent response modes, SocialGym2 benchmark

Abstract: Multi-robot navigation in human-shared spaces is inherently interactive: coordinated robot motions influence how nearby entities respond, while those responses provide valuable information for subsequent robot decisions. However, existing methods typically address action-conditioned prediction, multi-robot planning, or online adaptation separately, and therefore lack a unified mechanism for modeling joint robot-entity interactions and adapting future decisions from executed interaction outcomes. To address this gap, we propose TRACER, a bi-directional receding-horizon framework that closes the loop between prediction and adaptation. TRACER evaluates candidate (i.e., alternative feasible future motion plans for the robot team) trajectories using a per-entity probabilistic response model that separates individual-robot effects from non-additive pairwise interactions; after executing the selected trajectory prefix, it updates persistent identity-bound beliefs over latent response modes using the synchronized observed responses. These updated beliefs then guide subsequent candidate evaluation under probabilistic safety and response-aware cost criteria. Experiments show that (i) TRACER more accurately captures non-additive multi-robot interaction effects than a capacity-matched additive predictor, (ii) persistent identity-consistent evidence improves response prediction and downstream replanning, and (iii) the complete TRACER framework improves collision-free completion over an independent-robot baseline on the SocialGym2 multi-robot social-navigation benchmark.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出 AdaGeoVLN：一种流式视觉语言导航（VLN）框架，通过分层几何基础模型（GFM）–VLM 融合与导航感知的几何 KV 记忆保留，在 R2R-CE/RxR-CE 上仅用单一 RGB 流即取得强性能，并显著降低几何特征内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航需要在语言与视觉观测对齐的同时随时间保持空间理解。几何基础模型（GFMs）在其层级结构中暴露出多层次的中间表示，但导航策略应如何利用这些不同深度的特征、以及如何保留历史几何证据用于后续推理，仍是未解决的问题。

Method: 提出 AdaGeoVLN 流式框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 分层 GFM–VLM 融合，将 GFM 的早期、中期、后期层级表示分别耦合到策略的不同阶段，而非反复注入末端特征；(2) 导航感知 GFM 记忆，依据指令相关性、几何置信度与转移新颖性，在有界逐层预算下选择性保留历史 VGGT 全局注意力 KV 状态，为后续观测提供几何上下文后再与策略融合。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 基准上，仅使用单一 RGB 流、无需额外导航专用外部数据即取得强性能。受控消融显示，在相同融合位置下，多深度耦合显著优于重复末端特征注入；有界的导航感知记忆保留在维持导航性能的同时，相比大容量时序保留大幅降低 GFM-KV 内存占用。

Conclusion: 联合考察“暴露给策略的几何表示深度”与“为未来推理保留的历史几何证据”是流式 VLN 的关键；多深度分层融合与有界的导航感知 KV 记忆保留可在不增加外部数据的条件下同时提升导航性能与内存效率。

Classification reason: 论文属于视觉语言导航（VLN）这一具身智能任务，核心是导航策略的几何表征利用与历史几何记忆管理，属于动作规划与控制范畴，最贴合 world_model 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子话题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制），故直接复用该子话题。

Importance reason: A competent but niche embodied VLN paper combining GFM-VLM hierarchical fusion with navigation-aware KV memory, only tangentially touching the user's world-model interest (embodied navigation rather than generative/video world models) and none of their core continuous-multimodal or AR/diffusion generation priorities. Signals: vision-language navigation, geometry foundation model (VGGT) KV retention, GFM-VLM hierarchical fusion, single RGB streaming, embodied agent memory efficiency

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires aligning language with visual observations while maintaining spatial understanding over time. Geometry foundation models (GFMs) expose intermediate representations throughout their hierarchy, but how navigation policies should use these features and retain historical geometric evidence remains unresolved. We introduce \method{}, a streaming VLN framework that addresses these questions across \textbf{representation depth} and \textbf{navigation time}. Hierarchical GFM--VLM fusion couples earlier, intermediate, and later GFM representations to successive policy stages instead of repeatedly injecting a terminal feature. Navigation-aware GFM memory retains historical VGGT global-attention KV states according to instruction relevance, geometric confidence, and transition novelty under a bounded per-layer budget. Retained states provide geometric context for subsequent observations before fusion with the policy. Across R2R-CE and RxR-CE, \method{} achieves strong performance using a single RGB stream without additional navigation-specific external data. Controlled ablations show that multi-depth coupling substantially outperforms repeated terminal-feature injection at matched fusion locations. Bounded navigation-aware retention preserves navigation performance while considerably reducing GFM-KV memory relative to larger-memory temporal retention. These findings support jointly examining the geometric representations exposed to the policy and the historical evidence retained for future inference. Code will be released upon acceptance at .

</details>


### [29] [Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18813)
*Thi Thuy Ngan Duong, Cheuk Tung Shadow Yiu, Rahul Shome, Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 提出首个具有渐近最优性保证的多机器人任务与运动规划（MR-TAMP）算法：通过刻画任务转换结构并给出全局渐近最优的充分条件，将演化式单机器人路线图与隐式张量积搜索结合，配合条件转换采样、惰性碰撞检测与模式/解级引导，在多机器人操作任务上同时保证全局最优性与高效规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人任务与运动规划（MR-TAMP）需要联合推理离散任务决策与多个交互机器人之间的连续无碰撞运动。虽然单机器人 TAMP 已有渐近最优算法，但扩展到多机器人场景面临关键挑战：不同任务转换涉及不同的机器人子集，因而对复合构型空间施加不同维度的约束；渐近最优规划器不仅要优化各任务模态内的运动，还需保证对连接各模态的不同类型转换的充分探索。

Method: 首先刻画多机器人任务转换结构，建立全局渐近最优的充分条件：对相关转换的持续覆盖，以及在各连通可行区域内渐近改进的运动规划。在此基础上提出算法：将不断演化的单机器人路线图与隐式张量积搜索结合，避免显式构建复合构型空间路线图；并引入条件转换采样、惰性碰撞检测以及模式级与解级引导，在保持持续探索的同时提升有限时间内的规划效率。

Result: 给出了 MR-TAMP 全局渐近最优性的充分条件，并据此构建了渐近最优的多机器人操作规划框架；该框架避免显式复合路线图构建，利用单机器人运动规划的结构性实现高效求解，在保持持续探索与最优性保证的同时显著提升有限时间规划效率。

Conclusion: 该工作将渐近最优 TAMP 推广到多机器人场景：通过转换结构刻画、持续转换探索与单机器人运动规划结构的高效利用，实现了兼具最优性保证与有限时间效率的多机器人操作规划框架。

Classification reason: 论文属于多机器人任务与运动规划（MR-TAMP），是面向机器人/具身智能的规划与控制算法研究，与 world_model 方向下聚焦具身智能规划控制的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）最为匹配，故直接复用该子主题，未匹配其他方向。

Importance reason: A solid theoretical contribution to multi-robot task and motion planning with asymptotic optimality guarantees, but it lies far from the user's multimodal generation, continuous language model, and generative world model priorities. Signals: multi-robot TAMP, asymptotic optimality proof, evolving roadmaps with implicit tensor-product search, classical robotics planning, no multimodal or generative content

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning (MR-TAMP) requires jointly reasoning about discrete task decisions and continuous collision-free motions of multiple interacting robots. Although asymptotically optimal algorithms have been developed for task and motion planning, extending these guarantees to the multi-robot setting introduces an important challenge: different task transitions may involve different subsets of robots and therefore impose constraints of different dimensions on the composite configuration space. Consequently, an asymptotically optimal planner must not only optimize motion within each task mode, but also ensure sufficient exploration of the different types of transitions connecting them. We characterize this transition structure and establish sufficient conditions for global asymptotic optimality in MR-TAMP, requiring persistent coverage of relevant transitions and asymptotically improving motion planning within connected feasible regions. Based on these conditions, we develop an efficient asymptotically optimal MR-TAMP algorithm that combines evolving individual-robot roadmaps with implicit tensor-product search, avoiding explicit construction of the composite roadmap. The planner further employs conditional transition sampling, lazy collision checking, and mode- and solution-level guidance to improve finite-time planning efficiency while retaining persistent exploration. The resulting framework provides asymptotic optimality guarantees for multi-robot manipulation while efficiently exploiting the structure of individual-robot motion planning.

</details>


### [30] [KINO: A Keyframe Interface for VLM Planning and Whole-Body Control in Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18869)
*Sitong Chen, Fatemeh Zargarbashi, Jin Cheng, Tianxu An, Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (55.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: KINO 提出以运动关键帧作为中间表示的分层框架，衔接 VLM 高层规划与人形机器人 RL 全身控制：VLM 从预定义关键帧库中选择目标位姿，经场景重定向后由关键帧条件化策略生成关节级动作；配合基于显著性的关键帧采样训练策略，将端到端任务成功率从 44% 提升至 92%，并在 Unitree G1 上验证了单手/双手抓取、搬运与放置任务及泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形 loco-manipulation 要求机器人同时理解任务指令与场景语义，并执行协调的全身运动。高层 VLM 语义规划与低层 RL 全身控制之间缺乏有效的中间接口，直接以语言或稀疏目标对接难以完成长时程操作任务，因此需要一种衔接语义规划与关节级控制的中间表示。

Method: 提出 KINO 分层框架：以运动关键帧（指定全身目标位姿及可选物体位姿）作为 VLM 规划与 RL 控制之间的中间表征。VLM 根据语言指令、场景观测与执行反馈，从预定义关键帧库中逐步选择任务相关关键帧；关键帧根据当前场景中物体位姿与尺寸进行重定向（retargeting）；再由关键帧条件化的全身策略生成关节级动作。此外提出基于显著性（saliency）的关键帧采样策略用于低层策略训练。

Result: 在仿真与 Unitree G1 人形机器人上，针对物体抓取、搬运与放置任务进行评测。基于显著性的关键帧采样使端到端任务成功率从 44% 提升到 92%（使用稀疏 VLM 关键帧时）；系统可完成单手与双手操作，并能泛化到训练参考数据之外的放置位置。

Conclusion: 以动作关键帧作为语义规划层与控制层之间的中间表征，能够有效弥合 VLM 高层理解与 RL 低层执行之间的鸿沟，实现人形机器人在真实平台上的抓取、搬运与放置等 loco-manipulation 任务，并对训练分布外的放置位置具备泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文属于世界模型方向下的具身智能规划控制主题。虽然未显式构建世界模型，但 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）明确覆盖机器人动作规划、策略学习与控制，本文的人形机器人 VLM 语义规划 + RL 全身控制框架与之高度契合。论文核心贡献在机器人控制而非 VLM/多模态模型本身，因此不匹配多模态理解生成等其他方向。

Importance reason: A well-executed humanoid loco-manipulation system with strong real-robot results, but its VLM-planning-plus-RL-control focus sits at the edge of the user's generative world model and continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: VLM planning with RL whole-body control, keyframe intermediate representation, humanoid loco-manipulation, 44% to 92% success improvement, Unitree G1 hardware validation

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires robots to interpret task instructions and scene semantics while executing coordinated whole-body motions. We propose a hierarchical framework that uses motion keyframes as an intermediate representation between Vision-Language Model (VLM) planning and Reinforcement Learning (RL) control. Each keyframe specifies a target whole-body robot pose and, when applicable, an object pose. Given a language instruction, scene observations, and execution feedback, the VLM selects successive task-relevant keyframes from a predefined library. The selected keyframes are retargeted to the current scene to account for object poses and dimensions. A keyframe-conditioned whole-body policy then generates joint-level actions to reach these goals. We introduce a saliency-based keyframe sampling strategy for low-level policy training that improves end-to-end task success rate from 44% to 92% when using sparse VLM keyframes. We evaluate our framework on object pickup, transport, and placement tasks in simulation and on a Unitree G1 humanoid. The system successfully performs both one- and two-handed manipulation and generalises to placement locations beyond the training reference data.

</details>


### [31] [CaSCo: Cascade-Aware Soft-Collision Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18910)
*Shivaram Kumar, Gaoyuan Liu, Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (54.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CaSCo，一种级联感知的软碰撞运动规划框架：用 VLM/LM 为物体赋予语义风险，用物理模拟器预测候选动作引发的直接与级联物体位移，通过带可采纳一致启发式的图搜索最小化总语义风险，并在杂乱操作环境与真实机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动规划将碰撞视为二元约束，但不同物体的接触后果差异巨大：纸箱可以安全轻碰，而玻璃、笔记本电脑或易倒物体即使轻微接触也应避免。此外，机器人-物体的直接碰撞会推动物体并触发二次物体-物体碰撞，使一次运动的风险取决于场景的物理演化过程，而不仅仅是机器人自身的几何路径。

Method: CaSCo 框架：1) 用视觉-语言或语言模型为场景中物体分配语义风险权重；2) 用物理模拟器预测候选机器人动作导致的直接位移与级联物体碰撞后果；3) 在 roadmap 状态中增广预测后的物体排布以及已产生风险的物体集合，以建模碰撞改变环境的过程；4) 设计具有可采纳且一致的级联松弛启发式的最优图搜索算法，并引入缓存与剪枝机制加速搜索。

Result: 在杂乱操作环境中围绕语义风险评估、级联碰撞推理、规划效率与真实机器人运行四个方面进行了实验验证，展示了该框架能在最小化直接与间接被扰动对象总语义风险的同时保持规划效率。

Conclusion: 将碰撞从二元约束扩展为依赖物体语义与物理级联后果的软风险建模，并用物理模拟器做动作条件后果预测，可以显著提升杂乱环境中机器人运动的安全性与合理性；配合级联松弛启发式与缓存剪枝，规划仍可保持最优性与效率。

Classification reason: 该论文是面向机器人操作的运动规划工作，虽然核心是规划算法，但其关键在于用视觉-语言模型赋予物体语义风险、用物理模拟器预测候选动作的直接与级联物理后果（动作条件的环境动力学预测），属于世界模型方向中"具身智能环境模型与规划控制"的范畴，故归入 [CANONICAL] 子题 embodied_world_model；与连续语言模型、多模态生成、通用 VLM 理解与生成等方向均不匹配。

Importance reason: A competent embodied motion-planning paper combining VLM semantic risk with physics-simulator cascade prediction, but it only weakly matches the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative/video world models. Signals: VLM/LM semantic risk assignment, physics simulator as consequence predictor, cascade-aware graph search with admissible heuristic, real-robot cluttered manipulation validation, embodied planning rather than learned/generative world model

Abstract: Conventional motion planning treats collision as a binary constraint, although contact with different objects can have drastically different consequences. A robot may safely brush against a cardboard box while even minor contact with a glass, laptop, or unstable object may be undesirable. Moreover, a direct robot--object collision can move the contacted object and trigger secondary object--object collisions, making the risk of a motion depend on the physical evolution of the scene rather than only on the robot's geometric path. We present CaSCo, a cascade-aware soft-collision motion planning framework in which a vision-language or language model assigns semantic risk to objects and a physics simulator predicts the consequences of candidate robot motions. CaSCo searches for a path that minimizes the total semantic risk of the unique objects displaced either directly by the robot or indirectly through cascaded collisions. Because collisions change the environment, we augment roadmap states with the predicted object arrangement and the set of objects whose risk has already been incurred. We develop an optimal graph-search algorithm with an admissible and consistent cascade-relaxed heuristic and caching and pruning mechanisms for efficient search. Experiments in cluttered manipulation environments evaluate semantic risk, cascade reasoning, planning efficiency, and real-robot operation.

</details>


### [32] [Changepoint-Aware World Models: Detecting Dynamics Shifts and Recovering by Forgetting Stale Replay in Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18950)
*Everest Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (82.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出变点感知世界模型 CAWM：在 DreamerV3 中用在线 CUSUM 检测自身动力学突变，检测到后遗忘过期回放数据（保留表征）。在重力加倍、执行器增益减半的仿真运动任务上，比被动重训练和重生模型基线恢复显著更快，突变后前 30k 帧回报提升 +95～+153。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学到的自身动力学模型只在动力学不变时有效：执行器磨损、负载移动、关节变硬等都会使模型失效。若模型基智能体继续按原样训练，会被充满过期经验的回放缓冲区拖累，适应缓慢，因此需要能够检测动力学突变并主动遗忘旧数据以实现快速恢复。

Method: 在 DreamerV3 世界模型智能体上引入在线 CUSUM 变点检测器：以内部预测误差相对滚动基线的跳变作为信号，仅对突变触发、忽略缓慢学习漂移；检测到变点后执行“遗忘式”回放清理，即清空过期经验同时保留已学习的世界模型表征，再继续在线适应。

Result: 在重力加倍与执行器增益减半两种机器人相关突变下的仿真运动任务上，CAWM 的恢复速度显著快于被动重训练，也优于“检测到突变即重生新动力学模型”的强基线；突变触发时前 30k 帧回报提升 +95～+153（3 个随机种子），渐近性能与重生基线持平；闭环检测在重力突变下复现该增益，且突变越严重（旧数据越真正失效）收益越大。

Conclusion: 将在线变点检测与回放缓冲遗忘机制引入世界模型智能体，可以在动力学发生突变时快速识别并主动淘汰失效经验，从而大幅加快恢复速度；主动遗忘过期数据是模型基 RL 在非平稳机器人环境中的关键机制。

Classification reason: 论文核心是基于 DreamerV3 的模型基强化学习世界模型，面向机器人动力学突变下的检测与恢复（仿真 locomotion、重力/执行器变化），属于机器人与具身控制场景的世界模型应用，与最相关的 [CANONICAL] 子主题「具身世界模型与规划控制」吻合；虽有 dynamics model 元素，但并非交互式模拟器或物理仿真器构建，故主选 embodied_world_model。

Importance reason: Matches the world_model direction but sits in embodied RL adaptation/control rather than the user's core video-generation or continuous-multimodal world model interests, and is a narrow single-author simulation study. Signals: world model, DreamerV3 model-based RL, online CUSUM changepoint detection, stale replay forgetting, fast recovery to dynamics shifts

Abstract: A robot's learned model of its own dynamics is only valid until those dynamics change: actuators wear, payloads shift, and joints stiffen. A model-based agent that keeps training as if nothing happened adapts slowly, dragged back by a replay buffer full of stale experience. We present Changepoint-Aware World Models (CAWM), a DreamerV3 agent that detects an abrupt dynamics shift from its own internal prediction error, using an online CUSUM test against a rolling baseline that fires only on abrupt change rather than on slow learning drift. It then forgets stale replay, keeping the learned representation while flushing obsolete data. On simulated locomotion under two robot-relevant shifts, doubled gravity and halved actuator gain, CAWM recovers substantially faster than passive retraining. It also beats a strong baseline that respawns a fresh dynamics model on detection, the deep-world-model analogue of model-bank methods. With the response triggered at the shift, CAWM gains +95 to +153 return in the first 30k post-shift frames over three seeds, while matching that respawn at asymptote. Running the detector in closed loop reproduces this gain on the gravity shift. The benefit holds across both shift types, and is largest when the shift is severe enough that old data is genuinely obsolete.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频生成让机器人学习操作轨迹的方法只产生纯运动学轨迹，缺乏力信息，在需要合适接触力的接触密集型任务中容易失败，因此希望借助额外的生成模态为轨迹补充力信息。

Method: 构建一个联合利用视频生成与音频生成的管线：从结构化自然语言任务提示生成操作视频并提取运动轨迹，同时生成接触音效，用其响度整形有界的时变期望力曲线；在 Franka Panda 机器人上通过闭环力调节器跟踪该音频整形的力剖面执行接触操作；进一步将该管线作为数据生成引擎，为闭环策略学习生成训练数据。

Result: 在多个需要接触建立的操作任务上验证，力感知轨迹能成功完成操作，而纯运动学基线失败；利用该管线生成的数据训练的策略能以闭环方式完成任务。

Conclusion: 视频生成与音频生成的联合利用可同时"梦想"出接触操作的运动与力信息，实现零样本力感知机器人操作，并为闭环策略训练提供高质量生成数据，弥合纯运动学轨迹方法在接触密集任务上的缺陷。

Classification reason: 论文利用生成式视频+音频模型作为"世界模型"为机器人操作提供运动轨迹与力信息，并进行闭环力控执行与策略数据生成，属于具身智能的规划控制范畴，映射到 world_model 方向的 canonical 子类 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）。

Importance reason: A creative robotics application that uses generative video and audio as a world-model prior for force-aware manipulation, but its core contribution is embodied control rather than the user's main focus areas like continuous multimodal models or video generation architectures. Signals: generative video+audio as world prior, audio-shaped force profiles, zero-shot contact-rich manipulation, embodied world model subtopic, data generation engine for policies

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation allow robots to learn manipulation trajectories from generated videos. However, these approaches produce purely kinematic trajectories that lack force information, causing failures in contact-rich tasks where appropriate contact forces are essential for success. In this work, we explore augmenting generated video with audio to shape a bounded, time-varying desired-force profile using the loudness of generated contact sounds. We present a pipeline that jointly leverages generated video and audio to derive motion trajectories and corresponding desired-force profiles from a structured natural-language task prompt. We execute these force-aware trajectories on a Franka Panda robot using a closed-loop force regulator that tracks the audio-shaped force profile during contact. We evaluate our pipeline on multiple tasks that require making contact and demonstrate successful manipulation where a kinematic-only baseline fails. We also use the pipeline as a data generation engine to train policies that achieve the tasks in a closed-loop manner. Project website, videos, and dataset:

### [34] [Track, Articulate, Act: Generating Articulation from Casual Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19119)
*Jiaming Zhang, Homanga Bharadhwaj*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 提出一个 real-to-sim 框架，从随手拍摄的单目 RGB 视频中重建"可仿真"的铰接物体与手-物交互：利用稠密 3D 点轨迹区分固定/运动连杆并推断关节类型与状态轨迹，复用预训练的 3D 重建、网格分割与 3D 场景流模型，最后在 MuJoCo 中通过接触重放交互，为下游机器人具身操作提供铰接物体仿真资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频蕴含丰富的操作因果证据，可揭示手部运动如何引发物体运动与状态变化。门、抽屉、橱柜、笔记本、烤箱等铰接物体在日常生活中无处不在，但无法用单一位姿表示，其运动依赖于部件与关节，对具身交互构成独特挑战。现有方法通常依赖 RGB-D、多视角输入、先验扫描、手动指定关节或机器人演示，限制了从日常人类视频大规模构建可交互仿真资产的可行性。

Method: 提出一个 real-to-sim 框架：核心洞察是稠密 3D 点轨迹可作为与具身无关的铰接线索——固定连杆上的点近似静止，运动连杆上的点呈现连贯的旋转（revolute）或平移（prismatic）运动。方法模块化地复用预训练的单图 3D 重建、网格分割与 3D 场景流模型，通过显式几何推理串联各预测：分割连杆、估计关节类型与状态轨迹、重建铰接资产，并将恢复的 3D 手部运动与物体对齐，最后在 MuJoCo 中通过接触重放人手-物体交互。

Result: 在不使用 RGB-D、多视角、先验扫描、手动关节标注或机器人演示的条件下，该框架能够从随手单目视频中重建仿真可用的铰接物体资产并恢复手部轨迹，成功在 MuJoCo 中通过接触重放人类交互，展示了预训练视觉模型加显式运动推理在构建具身交互资产上的有效性。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉模型与显式运动推理相结合，可以把随手拍摄的人类视频转化为面向下游具身交互的铰接物体模型，为从人类视频中学习可交互仿真环境提供了一条 real-to-sim 途径。

Classification reason: 论文核心是从随手人类视频重建仿真可用的铰接物体与手-物交互，并在 MuJoCo 中重放以服务于机器人具身操作，与 world_model 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）高度相关；同时其从单目视频重建 3D 铰接资产（网格分割、3D 重建、场景流）也涉及 multimodal_generation 方向的 3D 生成与重建，故匹配两个方向，以具身世界模型为主要方向，复用现有 canonical 子主题。

Importance reason: A competent real-to-sim pipeline turning casual monocular videos into simulation-ready articulated assets, but it targets embodied robot manipulation rather than the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive/diffusion video generation, and generative video world models. Signals: real-to-sim articulated object reconstruction, dense 3D point tracks as articulation cue, repurposes pretrained 3D reconstruction/segmentation/scene-flow models, MuJoCo contact replay for embodied interaction, adjacent to world-model priority but no generative or learned world model component

Abstract: Human videos contain rich causal evidence for robot manipulation: they reveal how hand motion induces object motion and produces task-relevant changes in object state. In this work, we study articulated objects such as doors, drawers, cabinets, laptops, ovens, and hinged containers that are ubiquitous in daily life and present unique challenges for embodied interaction. These objects cannot be represented by a single pose; their motion depends on the underlying parts and joints. We introduce a real-to-sim framework that reconstructs a simulation-ready articulated object and hand-object interaction from a casual monocular RGB video, without RGB-D or multi-view input, prior scans, manually specified joints, or robot demonstrations. Our key insight is that dense 3D point tracks provide an embodiment-agnostic articulation cue: points on the fixed link remain approximately stationary, while points on the moving link follow coherent revolute or prismatic motion. Our method segments the links, estimates the joint and its state trajectory, reconstructs an articulated asset, and aligns the recovered 3D hand motion with the object. Central to our approach is a modular recipe that repurposes powerful pretrained models for single-image 3D reconstruction, mesh segmentation, and 3D scene flow, connecting their predictions through explicit geometric reasoning to infer articulation. We use the reconstructed articulated object and the human hand trajectory to replay interactions through contact in MuJoCo. The framework shows how pretrained vision models and explicit motion reasoning can turn casual human videos into articulated object models suitable for downstream embodied interactions.

</details>


### [35] [In-Context Robot Learning with VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19138)
*Dongzhou Cheng, Taoran Yi, Ye Fang, Xingwu Zhang, Fan Feng, Yixuan Li, Gengxiong Zhuang, Rongze Wang, Shuai Yang, Wei Song, Weizhi Xue, Minyan Wu, Jie Gui, Jiaqi Wang, Tong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.17) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 GPT-Policy：基于商用 VLM 智能体的上下文机器人学习框架，结合上下文编译器、VLM 动作提议与受限控制器，无需梯度更新即可从人类视频演示与交互反馈中适配新任务，在真实机器人上验证了任务完成率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让机器人像人类一样在陌生环境中快速适配仍是具身智能的远大目标。任何有限的演示集合都无法覆盖机器人将遇到的所有任务与场景，因此在部署时从上下文中学习（ICL）至关重要，但现有机器人策略普遍缺乏这种能力。商用视觉语言模型（如 GPT-6 Astra）展现的广泛智能体能力引发一个关键问题：这类模型能否从演示、示例和交互反馈中学习，并在不进行梯度更新或持久参数修改的情况下，将所学转化为从新初始状态出发的可执行、可验证的机器人行为。

Method: 提出 GPT-Policy 通用智能体框架，包含三个组件：1) 上下文编译器，压缩并保留任务相关的视觉状态转移信息；2) VLM 智能体，基于上下文提议机器人工具级动作；3) 受限控制器，对每个动作进行验证与执行并回报结果形成反馈闭环。整个过程不进行梯度更新或对任务特定参数的持久修改。通过任务成功率与效率指标、不同 VLM 的对照比较以及受控的上下文消融实验评估其可靠性与局限。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明：仅用人类视频演示（即使没有机器人动作标签）即可提升任务完成率；加入对齐的动作参考后在接触敏感任务上获得进一步提升。匹配比较与上下文消融揭示了各组件贡献及当前方法的可靠性限制。

Conclusion: GPT-Policy 展示了商用 VLM 通过上下文学习驱动机器人适配新任务、无需梯度更新的可行性，为将 VLM 通用智能能力转化为物理机器人行为提供了实证基础，同时指出实现可靠部署仍需解决的挑战。

Classification reason: 论文核心是基于 VLM 智能体的具身机器人上下文学习与动作规划控制（无梯度更新的策略适配），属于具身智能与机器人策略范畴，匹配 world_model 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制），故直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: A credible real-robot VLM-agent in-context learning framework under the world_model direction, but it is embodied robot control rather than the user's core video/continuous-latent generation subtopics. Signals: VLM agent for in-context robot learning, no gradient-update task adaptation, context compiler plus constrained controller, real-robot task success gains, embodied world model subtopic only weakly matches video world model interests

Abstract: Enabling robots to adapt to unfamiliar environments as readily as humans remains a moonshot goal of embodied AI. No finite collection of demonstrations can cover every task and situation a robot will encounter, making the ability to learn from context at deployment essential for generalization. Such in-context learning (ICL), however, remains largely beyond the reach of existing robotic policies. The broad agentic capabilities of commercial vision-language models (VLMs), such as GPT-6 Astra, raise a compelling question: can these models learn from demonstrations, examples, and interaction feedback, then translate that information into executable and verifiable robot behavior from a new initial state without gradient updates or persistent changes to task-specific parameters? We introduce GPT-Policy, a general-agent framework for in-context robot learning. GPT-Policy integrates a context compiler that preserves task-relevant visual transitions, a VLM that proposes robot-tool actions, and a constrained controller that verifies and executes each action and reports its outcome. We evaluate its reliability and limitations through task success and efficiency metrics, matched comparisons across models, and controlled context ablations. In real-robot trials, human video demonstrations improve task completion even without robot action labels, while aligned action references yield further gains on contact-sensitive tasks. These findings position GPT-Policy as a step toward robot adaptation through in-context learning, providing an empirical foundation for translating the general-purpose capabilities of VLMs into physical behavior and clarifying the challenges that must be overcome for reliable deployment.

</details>

*Bardienus P. Duisterhof, Kaifeng Zhang, Adam Hung, Bowen Wen, Stan Birchfield, Yunzhu Li, Deva Ramanan, Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PointZero，以 3D 点轨迹补全作为预训练目标，无需机器人动作标签即可从大规模合成数据（290 万帧）中学习可迁移的 3D 动力学先验；后训练后可完成动作条件 3D 动力学预测与模仿学习，在 PGND 基准及 7 个操作任务中的 6 个上超越或持平基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需要在大规模多样化数据上训练以获得丰富先验，但现有动作条件 3D 动力学方法通常依赖机器人动作标签，导致海量网络视频与无动作标注数据无法进入训练池。作者希望找到一种无需机器人动作标注的预训练目标，从更广泛的数据中学习可迁移的 3D 动力学。

Method: 提出 3D 点轨迹补全任务：给定单帧 RGB-D 观测和稀疏的部分 3D 轨迹，预测所有观测点的未来 3D 轨迹。构建包含 290 万合成帧的数据集（覆盖可形变、铰接和刚体物体），并用其训练一个灵活表达的 Transformer 模型 PointZero。随后通过后训练适配两个下游应用：(1) 条件于末端执行器位姿的动作条件 3D 动力学预测；(2) 预测机器人动作与 3D 轨迹的模仿学习。

Result: 在相同数据上 PointZero 优于先前方法；微调后条件于末端执行器位姿，在 PGND 3D 动力学基准上超越基线；用于模仿学习时，在 7 个仿真与真实机器人操作任务中的 6 个上优于或持平基线。作者还通过从头训练的对照实验分离了架构、预训练目标与数据集各自的贡献，并发布数据集、checkpoint 与完整训练配方。

Conclusion: 3D 点轨迹补全是一种有效的无动作标签预训练目标，能从大规模合成数据中学习可迁移的 3D 动力学先验，并可高效迁移至动作条件预测与机器人模仿学习，为利用海量非机器人数据训练世界模型提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 论文核心是面向机器人操作的世界模型：以 3D 点轨迹补全作为预训练目标学习可迁移的 3D 动力学先验，并后训练用于动作条件动力学预测与模仿学习（机器人操作任务），与 CANONICAL 子主题 embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）高度契合；其无动作标签的动力学建模部分也与 interactive_physical_world 相关，但最终目标服务于机器人控制，故归入具身世界模型。

Importance reason: Solid embodied 3D-dynamics world model work matching the user's world_model direction, but its robotics/manipulation focus diverges from the video-generation world models and continuous multimodal topics the user weights most highly. Signals: world model, 3D point track completion pretraining, action-label-free dynamics learning, 2.9M synthetic frames dataset released, robot manipulation imitation learning

Abstract: World models endow perceptual systems with the ability to predict how scenes evolve under interaction. They are most beneficial when trained on diverse volumes of data, to instill a rich prior into downstream applications. Existing methods typically require robot action labels to learn action-conditioned 3D dynamics, which excludes web video data from the training pool. We study 3D point track completion as a pre-training objective for learning transferable 3D dynamics without robot data. Given a single RGB-D observation and sparse partial 3D trajectories (tracks), we predict future 3D tracks of all observed points. We show this objective produces a rich 3D dynamics prior, without requiring robot action labels. We contribute a diverse dataset of 2.9 million synthetic frames spanning deformable, articulated, and rigid objects, and use it to train PointZero. We show that a flexible and expressive transformer, PointZero, outperforms prior methods on the same data. We demonstrate the utility of our pre-training objective by post-training PointZero for two downstream applications: (1) action-conditioned 3D dynamics prediction and (2) imitation learning. When fine-tuned to condition on end-effector pose, PointZero outperforms the baselines on the recent PGND 3D dynamics benchmark. When fine-tuned to predict robot actions and 3D tracks, PointZero outperforms or matches the baselines on 6/7 simulated and real-world robot manipulation tasks. We furthermore evaluate training PointZero from scratch to isolate the benefits of our proposed architecture from those of our proposed pre-training objective and dataset. We release the dataset, checkpoints, and full training recipe.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次日野火蔓延预测要求模型的预报能与其计算所用的物理假设和历史证据一同被审查。深度学习虽能从遥感数据中学习空间模式，但仅凭预测性能无法证明物理保真度与运行可信度，因此需要可审计、可检验的模块化建模框架。

Method: 在五个骨干网络上引入三种模块化增强：1) 风/坡度条件的注意力偏置，将方向先验显式注入注意力计算；2) 基于九维环境与火情状态描述子的历史图块检索模块，对冻结模型 logits 施加可学习的检索增强校正；3) 火情条件双流门控。评估采用分阶段消融、方向审计、检索扰动、校准度量与计算成本比较，并构建混合集成（两个增强架构+一个非增强架构）。

Result: SwinUNETR 加全部三种增强的三种子平均 F1 为 0.4216、AUC-PR 为 0.3673；混合集成进一步达到 F1 0.4292、AUC-PR 0.3790。收益随骨干架构而异，检索带来的 AUC-PR 提升并不总转化为更高 F1。构建的风向偏置与输入风向高度一致，但与观测到的次日火位移对齐较弱，说明先验可检验性有别于预测物理保真度。

Conclusion: 论文构建了在野火预测模型中暴露、审计并评估领域知识组件的统一框架，表明预测性能、运行可信度与计算实用性可以兼得而非相互竞争；同时警示方向先验的可检验性并不等同于预测的物理保真度，检索增强收益需按指标审慎解读。

Classification reason: 论文用深度学习结合风、坡度等物理先验建模野火蔓延的时空动力学，属于物理/环境动力学建模范畴，与世界模型方向下 [CANONICAL] 子话题"可交互与物理世界建模"（环境动力学建模、神经仿真器）最为契合，故复用该子话题；与连续语言模型、多模态生成、多模态理解等方向无关。

Importance reason: 尽管被标注为世界模型方向，但这实质是遥感数据的野火预测领域应用论文，与用户关注的生成式世界模型、视频生成和连续多模态方向几乎不相关，且命中负面关键词 remote sensing。 Signals: 遥感野火预测领域应用；非生成式/交互式世界模型；模块化注意力偏置与检索校正；命中负面关键词 remote sensing；无多模态生成或连续表示相关内容

Abstract: Next-day wildfire prediction requires models whose forecasts can be evaluated alongside the assumptions and historical evidence used in their computation. Although deep learning can learn spatial patterns from remote-sensing data, predictive performance alone does not establish physical fidelity or operational trustworthiness. This study investigates three modular augmentations for next-day active-fire prediction: wind- and slope-conditioned attention biases, physics-feature retrieval-augmented output correction, and fire conditioned dual-stream gating. The attention biases expose prescribed directional preferences, while the retrieval module selects historical tiles using a nine-dimensional environmental and fire-state descriptor and applies a learned correction to a frozen model's logits. The modules are evaluated across five backbones on the Next Day Wildfire Spread benchmark, using staged ablations, directional audits, retrieval perturbations, calibration measures, and computational comparisons. The three-seed mean F1 score and area under the precision--recall curve (AUC-PR) of a SwinUNETR model with all three augmentations are 0.4216 and 0.3673. Then, a mixed ensemble (two augmented architectures and one non-augmented architecture) model achieves 0.4292 and 0.3790. Benefits vary across architectures, and retrieval-related improvements in AUC-PR do not consistently translate into higher F1. The constructed wind bias aligns closely with input wind, but its alignment with observed next-day fire displacement is much weaker, distinguishing prior inspectability from predictive physical fidelity. The study contributes a framework for exposing and evaluating selected domain-informed components within wildfire prediction models. Together, the results presented show that predictive performance, operational trustworthiness, and computational practicality need not be competing objectives.

</details>


### [38] [Walking the Score Manifold: Continuous-time Generative Dynamics on Learned Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17901)
*Jan Tauberschmidt, Brian B. Moser, Stanislav Frolov, Andreas Dengel, Andrew B. Duncan, Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

TL;DR: 提出在习得数据流形上的连续时间生成动力学框架：以预训练 score 模型作为几何先验，学习沿 score 诱导插值路径演化的向量场，实现任意时间戳生成与时间超分辨；通过模拟无关的回归目标训练，并引入横向指数稳定性目标提升长时序 rollout 鲁棒性，进一步扩展为对未来轨迹分布的概率建模，在自然视频、PDE 时空场与分子动力学上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间依赖数据的生成建模通常在离散时间网格上构造，监督仅限于训练数据中观测到的时间戳，难以支持任意时刻生成与超出训练离散化的时间超分辨。作者希望利用预训练 score 模型学到的几何结构，把生成重新表述为连续时间内沿数据流形的演化，从而获得几何一致、可任意时刻查询的动力学建模能力，并解决长时序 rollout 的稳定性问题。

Method: 将生成建模为习得数据流形上的连续时间演化：以预训练 score 模型诱导的插值路径作为几何先验，学习一个沿该路径演化的向量场；通过回归目标实现 simulation-free 训练，可解释为沿插值路径横向的去噪 score matching；引入促进路径相对横向指数稳定性的目标以增强长时序 rollout 鲁棒性；进一步扩展到概率化设定，对未来轨迹的分布进行建模。

Result: 在自然视频（时间超分辨）、PDE 时空场和分子动力学等数据上验证了方法，结果显示基于 score 先验的向量场可支持任意时间戳生成、超出训练离散化的时间超分辨以及稳定的随机未来轨迹预测，表明 score-based 先验为学习随机连续时间生成动力学提供了强基础。

Conclusion: 在学习到的数据流形上进行连续时间生成动力学建模是可行且有效的：score 模型作为几何先验，配合模拟无关的回归训练与横向稳定性目标，可得到支持任意时刻生成、时间超分辨与长时序稳定预测的随机动力学模型，为视频与科学动态数据的生成建模提供了强基础。

Classification reason: 论文核心是学习数据流形上的连续时间随机生成动力学（dynamics model），支持未来轨迹分布预测与长时序 rollout，并在 PDE 时空场、分子动力学等物理数据及自然视频时间超分辨上验证，与 world_model 方向高度契合；复用 canonical 子主题 interactive_physical_world（物理世界与动力学建模），因其关键词 dynamics model、physics simulation、environment model 与本文内容最匹配。与连续语言/多模态方向（ELF、CoLa 等）无直接关系，故不匹配该方向。

Importance reason: A principled continuous-time generative dynamics framework using score models as geometric priors with stability-aware long-horizon rollouts, aligning with the user's world-model/physical-simulation interests, though it is not itself video or autoregressive generation and includes molecular-dynamics experiments. Signals: score model as geometric prior, simulation-free vector field training, transverse exponential stability for long-horizon rollout, probabilistic future trajectory modeling, temporal super-resolution

Abstract: Generative modeling of time-dependent data is typically formulated on a discrete temporal grid, restricting supervision to the observed timestamps in the training data. We instead frame generation as continuous-time evolution on a learned data manifold. To this end, we leverage pretrained score-based models as geometric priors and learn a vector field that evolves data along score-induced interpolation paths. Because these dynamics follow transitions that respect the geometry learned by the score model, they support generation at arbitrary timestamps and temporal super-resolution beyond the discretization of the training data. Moreover, this geometric formulation allows us to train the vector field simulation-free through a regression objective. To improve long-horizon rollout robustness, we introduce an objective that promotes path-relative transverse exponential stability. While motivated by stability theory, it admits a practical interpretation as denoising score matching transverse to the interpolation path. Further, we extend the framework to a probabilistic setting that models a distribution over plausible future trajectories. We demonstrate the method on natural video and scientific dynamical data, including temporal super-resolution, PDE-based spatiotemporal fields, and molecular dynamics. Our results show that score-based priors provide a strong foundation for learning stochastic continuous-time generative dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: We present SAiFE_gym, a Python module that provides a collection of simulation environments for studying trading problems in Constant Product Markets (CPMs) with Concentrated Liquidity (CL). These markets give Liquidity Providers (LPs) granular control over how their capital is allocated and enable them to adjust their range of liquidity provision dynamically based on market conditions, which in turn, dictates how they earn fees. We decompose the microstructure of CPMs with CL in interactive components that allow researchers and practitioners to capture various economic settings. We employ a vectorized approach to optimize our environments, making them scalable for high dimensional Reinforcement Learning (RL) workflows that best describe sequential decision problems. We demonstrate the benefits of our environments by evaluating the performance of RL agents in CPMs with CL under uncertainty in market parameters.

TL;DR: 提出用学习型判别器（atmospheric critic）对天气预报输出打真实性分数：以判别器 logit 构造类散度指标，可自适应检测 ML 天气模型的模糊、周期性伪影等各类失效，而现有固定指标至少在一种损坏上失效；分数随时效增长下降，数值模式普遍比 ML 模式更“真实”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习天气预报模型虽在点对点指标（如 RMSE）上准确率高，但常出现模糊、周期性不规则等非物理空间伪影。现有评测指标都需预先固定某种表示或变换，其选择限制了能检测的伪影类型，存在盲区，因此需要一种能自适应捕捉任意失效模式的评测方法。

Method: 训练一个判别器来区分参考数据（ERA5 再分析）与被评测模型的预报输出，并以判别器输出 logit 构造类散度（divergence-like）的真实性分数；该判别器可自适应学习模型输出与真实天气之间的任意差异。评测上，对 ERA5 施加多种合成损坏进行识别与严重度排序实验，并在真实天气预报模型上对比数值模式与 ML 模式。

Result: 在多种合成损坏上，学习型 atmospheric critic 能成功识别各损坏并正确排序其严重程度，而现有指标在至少一种损坏上失效；在真实模型预报上，真实性分数随预报时效（lead time）增长而下降，且总体上数值模式比机器学习模式获得更高的真实性评分。

Conclusion: 学习型 atmospheric critic 提供了一个自适应、无固定表示盲区的天气预报真实性评测框架，能统一检测并排序多种失效模式，为评估（尤其是生成式）ML 天气模型提供了优于固定指标的方案。

Classification reason: 论文核心是提出一种自动评测指标（学习型判别器真实性分数）用于评估 ML 天气预报（大气动力学建模）模型输出的真实性与伪影，属于物理世界建模相关模型的生成评测工作，复用 world_model 方向下已有的 generation_benchmark 子主题；与连续语言/多模态及常规图文/视频多模态生成方向不匹配。

Importance reason: A solid but domain-specific learned-metric contribution for weather forecast realism, only loosely connected to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video generation, and video world models. Signals: learned discriminator logit as divergence-like realism score, detects blur/periodic artifact failure modes that fixed metrics miss, ERA5 corruption benchmark plus real forecast evaluation, atmospheric/weather domain rather than video world-model generation, generative model evaluation methodology transferable in spirit to video diffusion critics

Abstract: Despite their high accuracy on point-wise metrics, machine learning weather forecasting models can exhibit different failure modes such as blurring, periodic irregularities, and other unphysical spatial artifacts. This has motivated a variety of metrics to detect known failure cases. Existing metrics fix a representation or transformation in advance, and that choice limits the artifacts they can detect. We propose to train a discriminator for separating reference data from the model's output, and using its output logit to obtain a divergence-like realism score. The discriminator learns whatever separates the model's fields from real weather, adapting to whichever failure mode that model exhibits. We compare our learned atmospheric critic to existing metrics using various synthetic corruptions applied to ERA5 reanalysis data. Our method successfully identifies the corruptions and ranks their severity, while existing metrics fail on at least one corruption. Additionally, we evaluate forecasts from real weather models, and find that the realism score degrades with longer lead times and the metric generally assigns higher realism to numerical models than to machine learning models.

</details>

### [41] [Zing-0.5: Toward Playable Worlds with Real-Time Joint Action and Text Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17909)
*Mingyang Chen, Shengdong Chen, Xiaoxiao Fu, Bosheng Gong, Haoyuan Guo, Bowen Li, Jiawen Li, Kejun Li, Tianpeng Li, Yin Liu, Haoze Sun, Zeyang Tian, Meng Wang, Xinmiao Wu, Jiangqiao Yan, Zining Zhao*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界模型难以在持续生成过程中让用户实时干预：缺乏将离散键盘动作与在线文本指令统一条件化的机制，事件级控制监督不足，且实时交互推理成本高。论文旨在构建"可玩"的世界模型，使用户能够探索生成世界、影响事件发展并响应环境反馈。

Method: 三个核心技术：(1) 统一动作与文本条件——将带幅度感知的键盘输入与时间对齐的文本指令及联合标注视频放在同一序列中联合训练，同时学习导航控制与事件控制；(2) 事件级增量生成监督——在多提示连续视频上训练 segment 级教师，通过分布匹配蒸馏监督 block 级因果学生；(3) 低成本实时交互——四步生成结合上下文保持流式推理，支持 832×480 分辨率 24 FPS 推理，服务器成本约每流分钟 0.009 美元。

Result: 在 WBench Navigation 的 158 个案例上取得总体分 81.0、一致性分 88.5；联合控制演示显示在持续导航过程中可通过文本指令切换事件而无需重启生成；发布模型权重、推理代码与 Zing-SGLang 服务实现。

Conclusion: 统一动作-文本条件、事件级分布匹配蒸馏与上下文保持的少步流式推理相结合，可以支撑低成本、高帧率、可玩的世界模型，为可交互生成世界方向的后续研究提供了开源可复现的基础。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Zing-0.5——一个可实时交互的自回归视频世界模型，通过键盘动作与文本联合条件实现可探索、可控事件的可玩世界生成，与 world_model 方向高度匹配；其动作条件视频生成、长时序流式生成属于 canonical 子题 video_world_model（视频生成式世界模型、动作条件视频生成）的核心范畴，交互性体现为动作+文本条件控制而非物理仿真建模，故复用该子题。

Importance reason: A 5B open-weights autoregressive video world model achieving real-time 24 FPS playable interaction via joint action-text conditioning and distillation, directly hitting the user's top-priority video world model, autoregressive video generation, and efficiency subtopics. Signals: autoregressive video world model, real-time 24 FPS streaming inference, joint keyboard and text control, event-scale distribution-matching distillation, 4-step low-cost generation

Abstract: We introduce Zing-0.5, a 5B autoregressive world model designed for playability: users can explore generated worlds, influence unfolding events, and respond to the resulting feedback through joint keyboard and online text control. Our approach brings together three technical contributions: (1) Unified action and text conditioning, combining magnitude-aware keyboard inputs with temporally aligned text instructions and jointly annotated videos to learn navigation and event control within the same sequence; (2) Event-scale supervision for incremental generation, using a segment-level teacher trained on connected multi-prompt videos to supervise a block-level causal student through distribution-matching distillation; and (3) Low-cost real-time interaction, combining four-step generation with context-preserving streaming to support 832 x 480 inference at 24 FPS at an estimated server rental cost of approximately USD 0.009 per stream-minute. Zing-0.5 achieves an overall score of 81.0 and a consistency score of 88.5 across 158 WBench Navigation cases. A joint-control demonstration shows a text-directed event change during continued navigation without restarting generation. We release the model weights, inference code, and Zing-SGLang serving implementation to support further work on playable generated worlds.

</details>


### [42] [StrucPhysVideo: Learning Physical Dynamics from Structured Captions and Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18430)
*Awomo-WM Team, Enhui Ma, Kaiwen Guo, Tingrui Zhang, Wei Song, Yingshui Tan, Jianhua Xu, Tong Zhang, Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.13) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: StrucPhysVideo 是一系列物理动力学聚焦的视频世界模型：通过运动感知分割与结构化物理字幕的数据管线，训练稀疏 MoE 文本-图像到视频模型，在 Physics-IQ Verified 上以 45.5% 刷新 SOTA；并扩展为可由机器人末端执行器指令驱动的交互式 IA2V 世界模型，4 步去噪即可实现增量机器人 rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建模物体运动、交互与状态变化等物理动力学是具身 AI 视频世界模型的核心，但现有模型缺乏以可观测物理事件为监督的物理聚焦数据，且从语言/图像条件的视频预测到动作驱动的交互式预测之间存在断层。

Method: 构建融合运动感知视频分割、质量与内容过滤、物理相关性校验的数据管线，解耦相机运动与物体行为，并对物体、材质及时序定位的接触、形变、状态转换进行结构化标注；基于该数据训练稀疏 MoE 文本-图像到视频模型 StrucPhysVideo-TI2V，采用逐步强调物理动力学的课程学习并保留通用域视频数据；进一步扩展为 StrucPhysVideo-IA2V 交互式图像-动作到视频世界模型，通过动作条件、因果自回归生成与少步蒸馏（仅 4 步去噪）实现机器人增量 rollout。

Result: 在 Physics-IQ Verified 上取得 45.5% 的 SOTA 成绩，超过 Cosmos3-Super-Image2Video 2.8 个百分点；跨骨干网络的字幕消融验证了物理聚焦监督的有效性；IA2V 可在 4 步去噪内依据机器人末端执行器指令生成增量式交互预测。

Conclusion: 以可观测物理事件为监督的结构化物理字幕与动作条件建模能显著提升视频世界模型的物理动力学理解，推动从图像/语言条件视频预测迈向动作驱动的交互式世界模型。

Classification reason: 论文核心是以物理动力学为核心的视频世界模型（文本-图像到视频预测 + 动作条件视频生成），完全命中 canonical 子话题 video_world_model（动作条件视频、未来预测、生成式世界模型）；其交互式机器人 rollout 与物理建模部分亦与 interactive_physical_world、embodied_world_model 相关，故主方向为 world_model。

Importance reason: A physics-focused video world model family achieving SOTA on Physics-IQ and extending to action-conditioned autoregressive interactive rollouts, directly matching the user's high-weight world model and video generation priorities. Signals: video world model SOTA on Physics-IQ Verified 45.5%, sparse MoE text-image-to-video model, structured physics captions data pipeline, action-conditioned IA2V with causal autoregressive generation, 4-step distillation enabling incremental robot rollouts

Abstract: Modeling physical dynamics, including how objects move, interact, and change state, is central to video world models for embodied AI. We present StrucPhysVideo, a family of video world models that bridges physics-focused data curation with language- and action-conditioned prediction of scene evolution. Our data pipeline combines motion-aware video segmentation, quality and content filtering, and physical relevance verification with structured annotations of objects, materials, and temporally localized interactions. By disentangling camera motion from object behavior and explicitly describing contact, deformation, and state transitions, the pipeline provides supervision grounded in observable physical events. Building on these data, we introduce StrucPhysVideo-TI2V, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) text-image-to-video model trained with a curriculum that progressively emphasizes physical dynamics while retaining general-domain video data. StrucPhysVideo-TI2V achieves state-of-the-art performance on Physics-IQ Verified, scoring 45.5% and outperforming Cosmos3-Super-Image2Video by 2.8 percentage points. Caption ablations across backbones further demonstrate the effectiveness of physics-focused supervision. We further extend StrucPhysVideo-TI2V to StrucPhysVideo-IA2V, an interactive image-action-to-video world model that predicts visual outcomes from robot end-effector commands. Action conditioning, causal autoregressive generation, and few-step distillation enable incremental robot rollouts with only four denoising steps. Together, StrucPhysVideo advances physical dynamics modeling from image- and language-conditioned video prediction toward action-driven interaction.

</details>

### [43] [GeoCond: A Conditioning-Aware Reliability Adapter for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18465)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal, Mohammad Ali Armin, Russell Tsuchida, Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (46.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 GeoCond：面向冻结前馈 3D 重建骨干（如 VGGT）的轻量可靠性适配器，读取骨干预测的几何并输出位姿级不确定性与细化门控，可将 OOD AUSE 从 0.32 提升至 0.20，并支持门控细化、位姿图加权与数据筛选等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT 等前馈 3D 基础模型虽能单次前向预测相机、深度与点图，但在低重叠、低视差、极端相对旋转等几何条件下会静默失败；分层实验表明这些失效由几何条件主导，且原生 aleatoric 置信度难以刻画，因此需要一种能判断几何何时可信的可靠性估计机制。

Method: 在冻结的 3D 重建骨干上接入轻量适配器 GeoCond：读取骨干预测的相机、深度与点图等几何信息，输出位姿级不确定度和细化门控。训练监督可采用三种来源之一：帧置换轨道方差（无监督）、真值位姿误差（有标签）、或来自独立位姿图的循环残差（cycle residual，无真值蒸馏）。推理时默认头仅需一次骨干前向传播加一个小型 MLP。

Result: 在 VGGT 上，GeoCond 将 OOD 场景的 AUSE 从 0.32 降至 0.20，显著优于原生置信度；可零样本迁移到室外极端视角场景，并避免了对所有输入统一施加 bundle adjustment 导致的崩溃。跨多个骨干验证了有效性，cycle 蒸馏变体提供了无真值适配路径，包括置换方差在等变模型上消失的情形。

Conclusion: 可靠的前馈 3D 重建不仅要预测几何，还必须判断几何何时可信；GeoCond 表明在冻结 3D 基础模型上接入轻量可靠性适配器即可低成本获得该能力，并可直接服务于下游稳健重建流程。

Classification reason: 论文核心是前馈式 3D 基础模型（VGGT 等）的 3D 重建可靠性与不确定性估计，属于 canonical 子方向「3D 生成与重建」中 3D 重建/几何估计的范畴；论文不涉及世界模型的动力学预测、动作条件生成或具身规划，故未匹配 world_model 方向。

Importance reason: 虽然为前馈 3D 重建（VGGT）提供了实用的可靠性适配器与不确定性估计，但主题落在 3D 几何可靠性而非用户配置的任何优先子方向（连续多模态、视频/图像生成、世界模型、统一多模态等）。 Signals: 前馈 3D 重建可靠性；位姿不确定性估计；VGGT 轻量适配器；OOD AUSE 0.32→0.20；零样本迁移；与配置优先子方向几乎无重合；无负面关键词命中

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models such as VGGT predict cameras, depth, and point maps in a single pass, but can fail silently under low overlap, low parallax, and extreme relative rotation. Stratified analyses over these factors show that these failures are governed by geometric conditioning and are poorly captured by native aleatoric confidence. We introduce GeoCond, a lightweight reliability adapter for frozen feed-forward 3D backbones. GeoCond reads the backbone's predicted geometry and outputs pose-level uncertainty and a refinement gate. During training, it can be supervised by frame-permutation orbit variance, ground-truth pose error when labels are available, or cycle residuals from unlabelled independent pose graphs. At inference, the default head requires only one backbone pass and a small MLP. On VGGT, GeoCond improves out-of-distribution (OOD) AUSE (area under the sparsification-error curve; lower is better) from $0.32$ to $0.20$ over native confidence, transfers zero-shot to outdoor extreme-view scenes, and avoids the collapse caused by applying bundle adjustment uniformly. Across multiple backbones, cycle-distilled variants provide a ground-truth-free adaptation route, including cases where permutation variance vanishes on equivariant models. The same reliability signal supports gated refinement, pose-graph weighting, calibration, curation, and capture decisions. Reliable feed-forward 3D reconstruction requires not only predicting geometry, but also knowing when that geometry should be trusted.

</details>

*Kristof Overdulve, Lode Jorissen, Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: 提出 CADSplat：用 CAD 模型形状先验正则化 3DGS，实现稀疏视角（<15 张）下照片级、几何精确的数字孪生重建，并联合优化高斯参数、相机配准与非刚性形变场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视角极少（<15 张）、宽基线的拍摄条件下，无约束的 3DGS 重建容易出现漂浮高斯与几何畸变。真实物体通常存在对应的 CAD 设计模型，可作为强形状先验，但需要解决 CAD 模型检索与相机-物体位姿配准两大问题。

Method: 将分割出的物体轮廓与 CAD 模型库渲染的轮廓匹配，实现 CAD 检索并直接获得相机-物体位姿；随后把 3D 高斯基元锚定到检索到的 CAD 表面，联合优化 3DGS 参数、相机配准以及一个非刚性形变场，以补偿物理物体与 CAD 模型之间的形状差异。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，CADSplat 超越无约束 3DGS、few-shot 与网格贴图基线，并在最少 3 个视角下性能优雅退化。消融实验表明渲染质量的主要增益来自"锚定在表面并由单一平滑形变场驱动"的 splat 约束方式，而 CAD 形状先验主要在最稀疏采集与强自遮挡物体上发挥作用。

Conclusion: CAD 先验增强的 3DGS 可在极少视角下实现鲁棒、照片级的数字孪生重建，并自然支持无标记 AR 配准、单图物体位姿估计、物理仿真与设计端部件标签迁移等下游应用。

Classification reason: 论文属于基于 3D Gaussian Splatting 的稀疏视角三维重建与神经渲染，利用 CAD 形状先验做正则化，与 [CANONICAL] 子话题 three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建，覆盖 3DGS、NeRF、神经渲染）直接对应；与连续语言/多模态、世界模型及通用多模态理解方向无关。

Importance reason: A competent sparse-view 3DGS/CAD reconstruction paper, but it sits entirely outside the user's prioritized areas of continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, CAD shape prior, sparse-view reconstruction, digital twin, no overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: We present CADSplat, a framework that reconstructs photorealistic, geometrically accurate digital twins from sparse ($<15$ views), wide-baseline posed images of an object by regularizing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with an explicit CAD shape prior. Using such a prior requires finding a CAD model whose shape resembles the object depicted in the images and determining the pose of each camera relative to the object. We obtain both by matching segmented object silhouettes against silhouettes rendered from a CAD library and keeping the camera-to-object poses of the best-matching model. We then anchor 3D Gaussian primitives to the surface of the retrieved model and jointly optimize the 3DGS parameters, the camera-to-object registration, and a non-rigid deformation field to account for shape differences between the physical object and the CAD model. Across two real-world datasets, CADSplat outperforms unconstrained, few-shot, and mesh-texturing baselines and degrades gracefully to as few as 3 views. Our experiments show that most of the gain in rendering quality comes from how the splats are constrained---a fixed set of splats tied to a surface and moved by a single smooth deformation field---rather than from the CAD shape itself. The CAD model adds shape knowledge where views are scarcest, in the sparsest captures and on strongly self-occluded objects, and it places every camera in the object's own frame. This enables applications beyond novel-view synthesis, such as markerless augmented reality registration, per-image object pose estimation, physical simulations, and the transfer of part labels from the design to the reconstruction.

</details>


### [45] [Geometry beneath the Waves: Dense Priors for Sparse-View Underwater 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18737)
*Harvey Caldeira, Haoran Wang, Guoxi Huang, Shaoyu Cai, Rachel Fu, Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (47.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 针对稀疏视角水下 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建质量受限于初始化几何的问题，提出利用稠密几何先验提供更可靠的初始化，并结合水下物理成像模型分离介质效应，在稀疏视角下显著提升水下场景的 3D 重建与新视角渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下 3D 重建支撑海洋生态监测、海底检查、水下考古、教育与沉浸式可视化等应用。3DGS 已使实时照片级新视角渲染成为可能，水下变体进一步引入物理成像模型以分离介质效应与场景辐射；但其重建质量从根本上受限于初始化所用几何，在稀疏视角下传统 SfM 稀疏点云不可靠，导致重建质量下降。

Method: 提出利用稠密几何先验（dense priors，如前馈稠密重建/稠密深度估计）为水下 3DGS 提供致密、可靠的初始几何，替代稀疏 SfM 点云初始化，并结合基于物理的水下图像成像模型（介质散射、吸收）分离介质效应与场景辐射，从而在稀疏视角条件下约束与优化高斯基元几何。

Result: 实验表明，相比依赖稀疏 SfM 初始化的水下 3DGS 基线，稠密先验在稀疏视角水下场景中显著提升几何精度与新视角渲染质量（PSNR/SSIM 等指标改善），同时保持实时渲染能力。

Conclusion: 稠密先验是稀疏视角水下 3DGS 重建的关键改进方向：以稠密几何初始化替代稀疏 SfM 点云，并与水下物理成像模型结合，可在海洋等水下介质环境中获得更精确、更鲁棒的实时照片级 3D 重建。

Classification reason: 论文研究水下稀疏视角 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建与新视角渲染，属于 3D 生成与重建范畴，与 multimodal_generation 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子话题 three_d_generation（明确涵盖 3DGS、NeRF、神经渲染与重光照）高度匹配；与连续语言模型、世界模型及多模态理解生成方向无关。

Importance reason: A niche 3DGS application paper for sparse-view underwater reconstruction that has no overlap with the user's priority areas in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, sparse-view reconstruction, underwater imaging physics, dense geometric priors, no overlap with configured subtopics

Abstract: Underwater 3D reconstruction supports applications ranging from marine ecosystem monitoring and subsea inspection to underwater archaeology, education, and immersive visualisation. 3D Gaussian Splatting has made real-time photorealistic novel-view rendering practical, while underwater variants incorporate physically based image-formation models to separate medium effects from scene radiance. Their reconstruction quality, however, remains fundamentally limited by the geometry used for initialisation.

</details>

### [46] [Wind on Trees: Testing Physical Grounding in Dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17810)
*Weiying Chen, Edmond Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 4D 与动态场景生成

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 该文用物理参数化的形变先验（每个刚性部件一个阻尼谐振子，由观测风驱动、可微RK4积分、仅光度监督）替代4D高斯泼溅中直接学习的形变场，用于单目风驱植被动态重建；并构建三棵程序化生成合成树的受控测试床，从留出视角、时间外推、零样本风速迁移与物理参数恢复四方面检验先验是否真正“物理接地”。结果显示：先验牺牲分布内外观保真但显著改善时间与风况外推，而参数恢复远弱于表面——频率仅在最稀疏树上通过空对照检验，阻尼完全无法恢复，说明物理先验的可辨识性被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目风驱植被重建严重欠约束：沿视线方向的运动基本不可观测、移动树冠缺乏可靠对应点、几乎整个场景都在动态变化而缺少静态参考。因此4D高斯泼溅中直接学习的形变场往往只优化光度一致性，而非恢复真正产生该外观的运动；需要检验物理先验是真正“物理接地”还是仅仅拟合得好。

Method: 将4D高斯泼溅中直接学习的形变场替换为物理参数化先验：每个刚性部件建模为一个阻尼谐振子，由观测到的风驱动，采用可微RK4积分，仅用光度损失监督；各部件自然频率由自身几何决定、阻尼比为固定常数，二者均在训练中held out。同时构建受控合成测试床：三棵程序化生成、骨架复杂度跨越一个数量级的树，评测留出视角渲染、时间外推、对未见风速的零样本迁移以及物理参数本身的恢复，并用未训练的空对照（null control）检验参数恢复的显著性。

Result: 该先验在分布内视角上牺牲了外观保真度，但在训练时间窗之外和训练风速之外的外推明显更好；然而参数恢复远弱于表面表现：频率恢复仅在骨架最稀疏的树上能经受未训练空对照检验，阻尼则完全无法恢复。

Conclusion: 物理先验能显著改善动态4D重建的时序与风况外推，但拟合好不等于物理接地：可辨识的物理参数恢复极弱。使用物理先验时应同时报告外推性能与参数恢复的对照检验，避免把外推收益误读为对物理规律的真实还原。

Classification reason: 论文核心是用物理参数化形变先验（阻尼谐振子+可微RK4）替换直接学习的形变场，实现风驱植被的动态4D高斯泼溅重建，并构建程序化合成树测试床评测外推与参数恢复，直接命中多模态生成方向 [CANONICAL] 子话题 dynamic_4d_generation（动态3D/4D场景生成与重建、4D高斯泼溅）；其物理一致性动态建模与世界模型方向的 spatial_3d_4d_world_model（动态场景中的世界建模、3D Gaussian）亦有交集，列为次要匹配。与连续语言/多模态方向无关。

Importance reason: A niche 4D Gaussian Splatting reconstruction study with a careful negative result on physical-parameter identifiability, only tangentially touching the user's physical world-model interest and far from their core continuous-multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: physical deformation prior in 4DGS, damped harmonic oscillator + differentiable RK4, negative result on parameter recovery/identifiability, wind-driven vegetation reconstruction, small synthetic testbed (3 trees)

Abstract: Monocular reconstruction of wind-driven vegetation is severely underconstrained: motion along the viewing direction is largely unobservable, a moving canopy offers few reliable correspondences, and nearly the entire scene is dynamic, providing little static reference. Directly-learned deformation fields in 4D Gaussian Splatting therefore optimize photometric consistency rather than recover the motion that produced it. We replace that field with a physically parameterized deformation prior: one damped harmonic oscillator per rigid part, driven by the observed wind and integrated by differentiable RK4, supervised photometrically alone. To test whether such a prior is physically grounded rather than merely well fit, we build a controlled synthetic testbed of three procedurally generated trees spanning an order of magnitude in skeleton complexity, whose per-part natural frequency follows from its own geometry and whose damping ratio is a fixed constant, both held out of training. On it, we measure held-out views, temporal extrapolation, zero-shot transfer to unseen wind speeds, and recovery of the physical parameters themselves. The prior costs appearance fidelity on in-distribution views and extrapolates markedly better outside the training window and the training wind, while parameter recovery is far weaker than it first appears: frequency recovery survives an untrained null control on only the sparsest of the three trees, and damping is not recovered at all.

</details>

### [47] [MSR: Multiple Subject Reference for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18393)
*Guannan Li, Jiaji Chen, Jingyuan Liao, Yu Geng, Baolan Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 提出 MSR：面向 LTX 视频生成的多主体参考槽位感知条件机制。每张参考图独立编码为独立 latent-token 组，通过 Fourier-feature MLP 槽位嵌入与槽位相关旋转坐标偏移区分角色，作为干净上下文参与 target-only flow-matching 训练（LoRA 实现），显著减少参考混淆，可组合多角色与参考环境，并开源权重与推理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让视频生成模型以多张参考图像为条件时，既要保持各参考的外观，又要将每张参考与其在生成场景中的正确角色关联；现有连续参考基线容易出现参考混淆，多主体身份与角色分配尚未很好解决。

Method: 提出 MSR 槽位感知条件方案：每张参考图独立编码为静态 clip 并表示为独立的 latent-token 组；用 Fourier-feature MLP 注入数值槽位嵌入，并以槽位相关的时间偏移修改该组的 rotary 坐标；参考组前置到含噪目标 token 序列中，在 target-only flow-matching 训练中充当干净上下文；整体通过 LoRA 低秩适配实现，并附有冻结视觉参数的音频参考（语音条件）扩展实验。

Result: 定性示例展示在写实与风格化场景中可组合包含不同角色与被引用环境的视频；开发观察表明相对早期连续参考基线减少了参考混淆；相似服装、复杂服饰与视角变化仍具挑战性；作者发布了权重与推理工作流。

Conclusion: 槽位感知的多参考条件机制能有效将多张参考图注入视频生成并缓解参考混淆，配合 LoRA 训练与开源发布，为多主体参考视频定制提供了可行范式；但在相似外观与视角变化下仍有改进空间。

Classification reason: 论文核心是以多张主体参考图条件化视频生成（subject-driven/reference-image 定制生成），与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 customization_generation（主体与概念驱动生成：subject-driven、reference image、ID 保持）高度契合，故直接复用该子主题；属 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: A modest but practical multi-subject reference conditioning scheme for LTX video generation that touches the user's video-generation interests, yet is an incremental LoRA-based engineering contribution rather than a core continuous/world-model advance. Signals: subject-driven video generation, slot-aware latent-token reference conditioning, target-only flow-matching training, LoRA on LTX, released weights

Abstract: Conditioning a video generator on multiple images requires preserving appearance while associating each reference with its intended role. We present MSR (Multiple Subject Reference), a slot-aware conditioning scheme for LTX-based video generation. Each reference image is independently encoded as a static clip and represented by a separate latent-token group. A compact Fourier-feature multilayer perceptron adds a numeric slot embedding, while slot-dependent temporal offsets modify the group's rotary coordinates. The reference groups are prepended to noisy target tokens and serve as clean context during target-only flow-matching training. We implement this scheme through low-rank adaptation and release the resulting weights and inference workflows. Qualitative examples demonstrate compositions containing distinct characters and referenced environments in realistic and stylized scenes. Development observations suggest reduced reference confusion relative to an earlier continuous-reference baseline, while similar clothing, complex garments, and viewpoint changes remain challenging. We describe the conditioning mechanism, the retained training configuration, and the observed strengths and limitations of the released system. A supplementary audio-reference experiment adds voice conditioning while keeping the visual parameters frozen.

</details>

### [48] [PDA++: Field-Aligned Planning and Scene-Adaptive Insertion in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18329)
*Xianchi Dong, Yingyan Hou, Chao Ren, Wanxuan Lu, Zihan Wei, Hongfeng Yu, Yixiao Wang, Chubo Deng, Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PDA++ 提出面向遥感影像的环境感知目标插入框架，按 Plan-Decouple-Assimilate 三阶段实现位姿规划、位姿条件背景引导与最优传输纹理对齐，在少样本/长尾场景下显著提升识别与检测性能（光学少样本 mAP50 +17.69，SAR 舰船检测 +4.10）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感识别常受稀有目标观测稀缺与标注成本高的限制，逼真的合成增广对少样本与长尾场景尤为宝贵。目标插入可在保留真实背景的同时提升目标多样性，但在俯视影像中实现逼真插入要求生成目标与周围环境连贯自适应，现有方法缺乏这种环境感知能力。

Method: 提出 Plan-Decouple-Assimilate 三阶段统一框架：(1) Planning——用结合几何间隙与结构/尺度线索的 affordance field 确定场景兼容的目标位姿；(2) Decoupling——引入位姿条件背景，提供精确空间引导与目标-场景上下文，使参考目标在保持身份的同时适配目标观测，并自然产出像素级掩码用于分割增广；(3) Assimilation——通过最优传输对齐多尺度纹理分布以提升局部一致性。

Result: 光学基准上全图 FID 达 6.28，少样本识别 mAP50 平均提升 17.69 点（相对真实数据基线提升 28.8%）；SAR 影像上舰船检测提升 4.10 mAP50，并在跨数据集迁移与无定形目标插入场景下依然有效。

Conclusion: 环境感知的 Plan-Decouple-Assimilate 框架能生成与场景高度连贯的合成遥感目标，为少样本与长尾遥感识别提供了高效实用的数据增广途径。

Classification reason: 论文属于遥感图像目标插入（object insertion）生成任务：通过 affordance field 位姿规划、位姿条件背景与最优传输纹理对齐实现逼真的可控图像合成与编辑，匹配 multimodal_generation 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 visual_editing（图像编辑/可控编辑/合成增广），与其他方向无实质关联。

Importance reason: A competent remote-sensing object-insertion/augmentation paper, but its domain hits the user's negative keywords and it does not touch any priority subtopics like continuous models, AR/diffusion video, or world models. Signals: remote sensing (negative keyword), object insertion for data augmentation, pose planning via affordance field, optimal transport texture alignment, few-shot/long-tail gains

Abstract: Remote sensing recognition is often constrained by scarce observations of rare targets and costly annotations, making realistic synthetic augmentation particularly valuable for few-shot and long-tailed scenarios. Object insertion provides an efficient way to increase target diversity while preserving authentic background scenes, but realistic insertion in overhead imagery requires the generated target to adapt coherently to its surrounding environment. To this end, we propose PDA++, a unified environment-aware object insertion framework organized as Plan, Decouple, and Assimilate. Planning determines scene-compatible poses through an affordance field that combines geometric clearance with structure- and scale-aware cues. Decoupling introduces a pose-conditioned background that provides precise spatial guidance together with target-scene context, allowing the reference object to preserve its identity while adapting to the target observation. This construction also naturally provides pixel-level masks for segmentation augmentation. Assimilation further improves local coherence by aligning multi-scale texture distributions through optimal transport. On the optical benchmark, PDA++ achieves a whole-image FID of 6.28 and improves average few-shot recognition mAP50 by 17.69 points, corresponding to a 28.8% relative gain over the real-data baseline. On SAR imagery, it improves ship detection by 4.10 mAP50 points and remains effective under cross-dataset transfer and amorphous-target insertion. Code is available at .

</details>


### [49] [Copy What Is Seen, Generate What Is Not: Training-Free Anomaly-Aware Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18836)
*Zhida Qu, Shengchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 值得关注 (76.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出免训练异常感知视频修复框架 AVR：用运动证据+开放词汇检测生成时空异常掩码，用背景先验填充异常暴露过的像素，仅对无任何帧证据的区域用扩散模型合成，并由冻结验证器在经典/先验锚定/背景条件三种修复器间选择；在三个监控数据集上实现领先的全帧保真度、匹配有训练修复器的区域效果，并抑制残余异常与闪烁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测与视频修复长期被割裂研究：免训练异常检测器只输出分数或标签便停止，而免训练视频编辑只响应用户提示而非检测结果。监控系统中"检测到异常后还需修复画面"的实际需求要求将两者闭合成统一流程。

Method: AVR 仅使用冻结预训练模型：1) 运动证据门控开放词汇检测提议，生成时空异常掩码；2) 从片段计算背景先验，填充异常曾暴露过的所有像素；3) 仅让扩散模型合成任何帧都未展示过的内容（即"复制所见、生成所无"）；4) 由冻结验证器按片段决定信任经典修复、先验锚定修复或背景条件修复三种策略之一。

Result: 在三个监控数据集上，于全参考异常注入与真实异常两种设定下实验：oracle 掩码下全帧保真度领先；在编辑区域内匹配三个有训练的视频修复器；在自产掩码上优于"检测-生成"流水线；同时抑制残余异常与自由扩散带来的闪烁。

Conclusion: 检测与修复可以免训练地闭环统一："复制已见证据、仅在无证据处生成"的策略能在监控场景中实现高质量异常移除与视频修复，兼顾保真度、异常抑制与时序稳定性。

Classification reason: 论文核心是用扩散模型对监控视频中的异常区域进行修复/移除，本质是免训练视频修复与inpainting，与多模态生成方向下的 canonical 子类 visual_editing（图像与视频编辑，明确包含 inpainting 与视频编辑）最匹配；不属于世界模型（无动力学建模/未来预测），也非连续语言/多模态模型方向。

Importance reason: A competent but niche training-free surveillance video restoration pipeline that only weakly overlaps the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive/diffusion generation, and world models. Signals: training-free video restoration, anomaly-aware inpainting, diffusion used only as fallback synthesis, surveillance-specific domain, no continuous tokens, autoregressive, or world-model contribution

Abstract: A surveillance system that detects an anomaly often has to repair the footage as well, yet the two tasks are studied in isolation: training-free anomaly detectors stop at a score or a label, while training-free video editing answers to a user prompt rather than to a detector. This paper proposes AVR (Anomaly-aware Video Restoration), which closes that gap with frozen pretrained models alone and generates content only where the clip offers no evidence to copy. Motion evidence first gates open-vocabulary proposals into spatio-temporal masks. A background prior computed from the clip then fills every pixel the anomaly ever uncovers, leaving diffusion to synthesize only what no frame showed, and a frozen verifier decides per clip whether to trust a classical, a prior-anchored, or a background-conditioned restorer. Extensive experiments on three surveillance datasets, under both full-reference anomaly injection and real anomalies, show that AVR leads full-frame fidelity under oracle masks, matches three trained video inpainters inside the edited region, and outperforms a detect-then-generate pipeline on the masks it produces itself, while suppressing both the residual anomaly and the flicker of free diffusion.

</details>
