### [50] [GenStream: Semantic Streaming Framework for Generative Reconstruction of Human-centric Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18634)
*Emanuele Artioli, Daniele Lorenzi, Shivi Vats, Farzad Tashtarian, Christian Timmerer*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (63.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出语义流式传输框架 GenStream：不再传输像素，而是传输骨骼关键点、相机视角参数与静态 3D 背景模型，客户端由生成模型重建照片级真实感人体并合成回 3D 场景，相比 HEVC 实现超 99.9% 的带宽节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流媒体占据全球主要互联网流量，但传统编解码流水线对所有像素（前景与背景）统一重编码，忽略场景语义结构。对于背景静态、运动集中于少数显著人物的内容（如体育、表演、交互媒体），这造成严重的带宽浪费，因此需要一种语义化、极端压缩的传输范式。

Method: GenStream 将每个场景编码为骨骼关键点、相机视角参数和静态 3D 背景模型等紧凑结构化元数据并发送至客户端；客户端由生成模型根据关键点重建照片级真实感人体，并从原始相机视角将其合成进 3D 背景场景，从而以语义信息流取代稠密像素流；在奥运会花样滑冰视频上进行部分验证。

Result: 相对 HEVC，连续数据流实现超过 99.9% 的带宽节省；在花样滑冰数据上展示了在极少数据量下达到高感知保真度的潜力；同时指出客户端计算成本显著上升与跨场景泛化性等挑战。

Conclusion: GenStream 以"语义元数据 + 生成式重建"取代像素级编解码，在以人为中心的内容流媒体上实现极端压缩，并提出体积化人物合成、跨视角规范 3D 人物融合与个性化观看体验等后续方向，为后编解码器时代的可扩展智能流媒体奠定基础。

Classification reason: 论文核心是用紧凑语义元数据（骨骼关键点、相机参数、3D 背景）替代像素传输，由客户端生成模型重建照片级真实感人体并合成回场景，属于多模态生成方向下"数字人与动作生成"的典型应用（人体/数字人合成与运动重建），故复用该 CANONICAL 子主题。

Importance reason: An interesting semantic-streaming idea (keypoint+viewpoint+3D metadata with client-side generative reconstruction) but it sits far from the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive/diffusion video generation, and world models. Signals: generative client-side reconstruction from skeletal keypoints, 99.9% bandwidth saving vs HEVC, post-codec streaming paradigm, only partial validation on figure skating footage, tangential to multimodal generation and compression interests

Abstract: Video streaming dominates global internet traffic, yet conventional pipelines remain inefficient for structured, human-centric content such as sports, performance, or interactive media. Standard codecs re-encode entire frames, foreground and background alike, treating all pixels uniformly and ignoring the semantic structure of the scene. This leads to significant bandwidth waste, particularly in scenarios where backgrounds are static and motion is constrained to a few salient actors. We introduce GenStream, a semantic streaming framework that replaces dense video frames with compact, structured metadata. Instead of transmitting pixels, GenStream encodes each scene as a combination of skeletal keypoints, camera viewpoint parameters, and a static 3D background model. These elements are transmitted to the client, where a generative model reconstructs photorealistic human figures and composites them into the 3D scene from the original viewpoint. This paradigm enables extreme compression, achieving over 99.9% bandwidth reduction compared to HEVC for the continuous data stream. We partially validate GenStream on Olympic figure skating footage and demonstrate potential for high perceptual fidelity under minimal data. While acknowledging the significant computational costs shifted to the client and challenges in generalization, GenStream opens new directions in volumetric avatar synthesis, canonical 3D actor fusion across views, and personalized viewing experiences, laying the groundwork for scalable, intelligent streaming in the post-codec era.

</details>


### [51] [InterMASH: A Unified Geometric Representation for Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18504)
*Xuanze Yang, Yumeng Liu, Haiyang Xin, Changhao Li, Haowei Shen, Kai Xu, Ligang Liu, Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 普通相关 (53.02) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出统一几何表征 InterMASH：用球面固定锚点建立人手与机械手的跨具身对应，并在每个锚点用低阶球谐紧凑编码局部手部几何、物体几何与接触，形成显式 token 序列；再以条件 Diffusion Transformer 在该表征空间联合生成手部几何与接触。在大规模 ShadowHand 基准上物理可行性指标达到 SOTA 竞争水平，支持多手联合训练，且人手抓取数据的跨具身微调能提升机械手抓取成功率与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抓取合成（生成稳定且物理合理的手-物交互）是人手建模与机器人操作的基础问题，但人手与机器人手在形态和表面建模上差异大，缺乏统一表征；既有方法依赖接触图或稠密隐式描述子，前者往往不完整，后者计算昂贵且冗余。

Method: 提出 InterMASH 表征：以球面固定锚点建立跨具身（人手/机械手）对应，在每个锚点处用低阶球谐函数紧凑编码局部手部几何、物体几何与接触信息，形成显式、可解释的 token 序列；在该表征空间上训练条件 Diffusion Transformer，联合生成手部几何与接触，并支持多手联合训练与跨具身微调。

Result: 在大规模 ShadowHand 基准上，关键物理可行性指标达到与 SOTA 方法相当的竞争力；支持多手联合训练；实验表明用人手抓取数据进行跨具身微调可提升机械手抓取的成功率与多样性。

Conclusion: 基于球锚-球谐的显式 token 化表征配合 DiT 联合生成，为跨人手与机械手的抓取合成提供了统一、可解释且物理合理的框架，并验证了跨具身迁移学习在该任务上的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文核心是用条件 Diffusion Transformer 生成人手/灵巧手与物体的抓取交互（手部几何+接触），属于人体/动作相关的 3D 生成任务，映射到 multimodal_generation 方向下的 canonical 子话题"数字人与动作生成"（人体动作生成范畴，人手抓取姿态生成是其分支）。论文虽涉及机器人操作（ShadowHand），但不含环境动力学建模、未来预测或世界模型内容，故不归入 world_model 方向的 embodied_world_model。

Importance reason: A solid cross-embodiment grasp synthesis contribution using tokenized geometric representations with a diffusion transformer, but it lies in robotics/manipulation rather than the user's core areas of continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: diffusion transformer on explicit tokens, unified human-robot hand representation, cross-embodiment transfer from human grasp data, sphere-anchor + spherical harmonic encoding, robotics/manipulation domain, weak overlap with video/language generation priorities

Abstract: Grasp synthesis aims to generate stable and physically plausible hand--object interactions, and has become a fundamental problem in both human hand modeling and robotic manipulation. However, a unified representation across human and robotic hands is still lacking, mainly due to differences in hand morphology and surface modeling. Prior methods typically rely on either contact maps or dense implicit descriptors to represent interaction, but these representations are often incomplete or computationally expensive and redundant. We propose InterMASH, a unified geometric representation that establishes cross-embodiment correspondence using sphere-fixed anchors. At each anchor, low-degree spherical harmonics compactly encode local hand geometry, object geometry, and contact, forming an explicit and interpretable token sequence. Building on this natively tokenized structure, we introduce a conditional Diffusion Transformer that operates directly in the proposed InterMASH representation space and jointly generates hand geometry and contact, improving consistency and physical plausibility. Our method achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods on key physical feasibility metrics in a large-scale ShadowHand benchmark, supports joint training across multiple hands, and shows that cross-embodiment fine-tuning with human grasp data can improve robotic grasp success and diversity. Project page is available at .

</details>


### [52] [DiT-Garment: Garment Dynamics with Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18510)
*Antoine Dumoulin, Laurence Boissieux, Joao Regateiro, Pierre Hellier, Stefanie Wuhrer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (72.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 DiT-Garment：利用 2D 扩散 Transformer 在 UV 空间学习服装 3D 形变的分布，以模板位置图、身体运动和物理参数为条件，可对未见设计与材质的服装在任意姿态下直接推断形变，仅用合成仿真训练即泛化到真实与美术制作的服装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服装动力学方法通常依赖共享模板、复杂图卷积结构，且难以泛化到未见设计与物理材质的服装，也无法直接对任意目标姿态推断形变；因此需要一种生成式、跨设计泛化的服装动力学建模方法。

Method: 将模板服装表示为与标准姿态人体空间对齐的 3D 三角网格，并用其 UV 空间中的 3D 位置图作为条件输入 2D 扩散 Transformer，从而隐式学习标准姿态下身体周围 3D 空间的形变，摆脱对公共模板或复杂图卷积的依赖；进一步以身体运动序列和物理参数（材质属性）为条件对模型进行物理接地，以生成式方式学习形变结果的分布而非单点预测。

Result: 在合成与真实数据上进行了定量与定性评估：仅用自动生成布料设计的仿真数据训练，方法即可泛化到真实捕捉与美术师制作的服装设计，并能处理未见物理材质，实现任意目标姿态的直接形变推断；代码与数据已开源。

Conclusion: 基于 UV 空间扩散 Transformer 的生成式动力学建模，可以在不依赖公共模板或图卷积的情况下，对任意设计、任意材质的服装实现任意运动下的形变推断，且仅用合成仿真数据训练即可泛化到真实与美术资产，为数字人服装动画与布料仿真提供了一条通用、可扩展的路线。

Classification reason: 论文核心是用扩散 Transformer 生成任意姿态下虚拟人服装的 3D 网格形变，属于数字人/虚拟人动画范畴，最贴合多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"数字人与动作生成"；其对布料物理动力学（物理参数条件、动力学建模）与世界模型方向的物理世界建模（neural simulator/dynamics model）弱相关，列为次级匹配。

Importance reason: Solid niche contribution applying diffusion transformers to 3D garment deformation with good generalization, but the digital human/cloth simulation domain sits far from the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models, video generation, and world models. Signals: diffusion transformer architecture, generative modeling of deformation distribution in UV space, conditioned on body motion and physical parameters, trains only on synthetic simulation yet generalizes to real garments, weak tangential fit to neural simulator/physics modeling

Abstract: We present DiT-Garment to model dynamic 3D clothing over human body models in arbitrary motion. Unlike existing methods, DiT-Garment can animate garments with unseen designs and physical materials, while allowing for direct inference of deformations for any target pose. To achieve this, we leverage a 2D diffusion transformer architecture to learn 3D deformations in a 2D UV-space. As the result is non-deterministic, our generative model learns the distribution of possible outcomes. The template garment is represented as a 3D triangle mesh spatially aligned with a 3D human body model in a standardized pose. To work with different garment designs without the need of a common template or complex graph convolution operations, the diffusion transformer is conditioned on a 3D position map of the template, represented in UV-space, which allows to implicitly learn a deformation of the 3D space around the body in standard pose. Further conditioning on body motion and physical parameters allows to physically ground the model. We quantitatively and qualitatively evaluate DiT-Garment on both synthetic and real data. While only trained on synthetic simulations of automatically generated cloth designs, our method generalizes to captured and artist-made garment designs. Code and data are available for research purposes at .

</details>


### [53] [VibeAvatar: Aligning Phonetic Kinematics and Human Aesthetics for High-Fidelity Talking Avatar Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18632)
*Qilin Wang, Mingyu Li, Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (68.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VibeAvatar 通过语音动力学适配器（PKA）在条件阶段将语音特征转为发音-运动学条件，并在后训练阶段用 GRPO 优化美学运动采样策略，结合紧凑 1D warp 潜空间中的轻量流运动生成器，在说话人视频合成中同时实现发音准确、运动美学与高效推理（10 秒 512px 视频生成用时 <10 秒、显存约 3GB），达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的说话头像生成方法难以同时实现准确的唇部发音、符合人类偏好的运动美感以及高效推理。作者观察到发音准确性与运动美感来源根本不同，应由互补阶段分别处理，而不是由单一生成器隐式学习。

Method: 提出 VibeAvatar：1) 语音动力学适配器（PKA），在条件阶段将面向识别的语音特征转换为语音-运动学条件，保证唇部发音准确；2) 美学运动策略（AMP），在后训练阶段通过 GRPO 优化流一致的随机采样策略以对齐人类运动美学偏好；3) 采用运行在紧凑 1D warp 潜空间中的轻量流式运动生成器，实现高效视频合成。

Result: 在客观指标与用户研究上，VibeAvatar 在发音准确性、美学质量和推理效率三方面均达到 SOTA；生成 10 秒 512px 视频耗时不到 10 秒，仅需约 3GB 显存。

Conclusion: 将发音准确性与运动美学解耦到条件阶段与后训练阶段分别处理，并配合紧凑的 1D warp 潜空间流生成器，能够同时实现高保真发音、符合人类偏好的运动美学与高效推理，为说话人头像合成提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 论文核心任务是从参考肖像与语音生成高保真说话视频（talking head/肖像动画），属于多模态生成方向；精确命中经典子话题 avatar_motion_generation（数字人与动作生成：说话头、肖像动画、人体动作），故直接复用该 canonical 子话题，与连续语言模型、世界模型等方向无关。

Importance reason: A technically solid talking-avatar paper with notable efficiency gains, but it sits in the niche digital-human subtopic with only partial overlap to the user's core priorities (diffusion video generation, efficiency) and none with continuous models or world models. Signals: diffusion-based talking avatar synthesis, phonetic-kinematic conditioning adapter, GRPO post-training for motion aesthetics, flow motion generator in compact 1D warp latent space, efficient inference (10s 512px video <10s, ~3GB VRAM)

Abstract: Multi-modal talking avatar synthesis aims to generate realistic talking videos from a reference portrait and speech. Despite rapid progress in diffusion-based methods, existing approaches still struggle to jointly achieve accurate lip articulation, human-preferred motion aesthetics, and efficient inference. We observe that phonetic accuracy and motion aesthetics arise from fundamentally different sources and should be addressed at complementary stages rather than learned implicitly by a single generator. Based on this insight, we propose VibeAvatar, which disentangles these two objectives through a Phonetic Kinematics Adapter (PKA) that converts recognition-oriented speech features into phonetic-kinematic conditions at the conditioning stage, and an Aesthetic Motion Policy (AMP) that optimizes a flow-consistent stochastic sampling policy via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) at the post-training stage. With a lightweight flow-based motion generator operating in a compact 1D warp-based latent motion space, VibeAvatar achieves state-of-the-art results in articulation, aesthetics, and efficiency on both objective metrics and user studies, while generating a 10-second 512px video in under 10 seconds with only $\sim$3GB VRAM.

</details>

### [54] [Multimodal Conditioning of Fine-Tuned Stable Diffusion XL for Controllable and Culturally Faithful Ulos Motif Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17987)
*Humasak Simanjuntak, Tamara Yunika Sianipar, Bronson T.M Siallagan, Difya Laurensya Ambarita, Arlinta Barus*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (74.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 LoRA 微调 SDXL + MLLM 的多模态条件可控生成框架，用于文化忠实的巴塔克 Ulos 织锦纹样生成；消融实验表明 Text+Image+Representation 三条件组合整体最平衡，条件机制并非越多越好，并发布了文生图/图生图 Web 原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统巴塔克 Ulos 织锦产业依赖手工设计，难以产出多样化、创新性的纹样；需要既可控又能保持文化忠实性的数字化生成工具，以支持文化遗产的保护与创新设计。

Method: 以 Stable Diffusion XL v1.0 经 LoRA 微调，并结合 LLaMA 1.5-7B 多模态大模型，引入四种互补条件机制（文本、图像、表征、经 ControlNet 的语义图）分别控制从语义意图到空间布局的生成过程；在形状变换、颜色变化、高复杂度输入三种场景下进行五级消融实验，并用 FID、CLIP Score、SSIM 与人工评价进行评估，同时开发支持文生图与图生图的 Web 原型。

Result: Text+Image+Semantic Map 取得最优 FID(270) 与 CLIP Score(0.65–0.70) 但 SSIM 最弱(0.65)；Text+Image+Representation 整体最平衡（SSIM 0.84、FID 280）；四种条件同时使用 FID 最差(330)，表明条件信号相互冲突；九名织工与三十名公众的主观评价均显著正向（Wilcoxon p=0.007 与 p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 多模态条件并非越多越好，需按任务在保真度与相似度之间权衡；LoRA 微调扩散模型结合多模态控制可作为传统文化纹样数字化设计与文化遗产保护的有效工具。

Classification reason: 论文核心是基于 LoRA 微调 Stable Diffusion XL 的可控文生图框架，结合文本、图像、表征与 ControlNet 语义图等多模态条件机制生成扩散图像，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"文生图与图像扩散"（text_to_image_diffusion）直接对应；非编辑、3D、对齐或连续潜空间语言建模工作。主要归入 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: An application paper that LoRA fine-tunes SDXL with MLLM/ControlNet conditioning for culturally faithful Ulos motif generation, touching image generation and controllable generation but offering limited methodological novelty beyond the user's core priorities. Signals: LoRA fine-tuned SDXL, controllable generation, MLLM conditioning, ablation shows more conditions hurt FID, cultural heritage application

Abstract: The traditional Batak Ulos weaving industry faces growing challenges in producing diverse, innovative motifs due to limitations in conventional, manually driven design methods. This study proposes a multimodal generative framework integrating a fine-tuned Latent Diffusion Model (Stable Diffusion XL v1.0 via LoRA) with a Multimodal Large Language Model (LLaMA 1.5-7B) to enable controllable, culturally faithful Ulos motif generation. Four complementary conditioning mechanisms: text, image, representation, and semantic map (via ControlNet) jointly guide the generation process, each governing a distinct aspect from semantic intent to spatial layout. A five level ablation study across three scenarios (shape transformation, colour variation, and high-complexity input) shows that conditioning effectiveness is not proportional to the number of mechanisms combined: Text + Image + Semantic Map achieved the best FID (270) and CLIP Score (0.65 - 0.70) but the weakest SSIM (0.65), while Text + Image + Representation offered the best overall balance, with stable SSIM (0.84) and competitive FID (280). Combining all four mechanisms yielded the weakest FID (330), indicating conflicting optimization signals. Qualitative evaluation by nine weavers and thirty public participants confirmed statistically significant positive acceptance (Wilcoxon, p=0.007 and p<0.001, respectively). A web-based prototype supporting text-to-image and image-to-image generation was also developed, offering a practical digital design tool for cultural heritage preservation.

</details>


### [55] [GazeDiT: Gaze-Accurate Diffusion Image Generation for Eye Tracking via Spatial Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17814)
*Dongze Wu, David Colmenares, Fengting Yang, Jogendra Nath Kundu, Yao Xie, Ali Behrooz, Conny Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (68.47) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GazeDiT 提出一种以 4D 注视标签为条件的扩散图像生成模型，通过内部构建的局部瞳孔/虹膜空间条件将全局低维标签锚定到局部几何，从而生成标签精确对应的合成眼部图像，显著降低尾部标签误差，并用生成数据提升下游眼动追踪性能（困难案例误差从 3.05° 降至 2.80°）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型日益被用于生成合成训练数据，但当条件信号为低维、粗糙的数值标签时，实现标签与图像的精确对应十分困难：文本条件图像只需宽泛的 prompt 一致性，而监督训练要求每张图像与数值标签精确对应。这在眼动追踪中尤为突出，因为 4D 双目注视通过细微且空间局域化的瞳孔/虹膜几何来表达。

Method: 提出 GazeDiT：通过内部构建的空间条件将全局 4D 注视标签锚定到局部瞳孔/虹膜几何。训练时用冻结的 SegFormer 从多样真实图像中提取瞳孔/虹膜几何，使模型学习以该几何为条件的真实外观；推理时由物理眼部渲染器通过改变解剖结构与相机状态采样注视一致的几何，无需源图像即可实现多样化合成。

Result: GazeDiT 的尾部注视标签误差显著低于其他扩散基线，接近同一冻结注视估计器在真实图像上的误差；其生成的数据提升了下游眼动追踪器，在最小组群中将困难案例的注视误差从 3.05° 降至 2.80°。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将低维数值标签条件转化为局部空间几何条件，可有效解决扩散模型在低维粗条件下的精确标签控制难题，生成标签精确对应的合成数据，并切实提升下游监督任务的性能，为合成数据生成中的精确条件控制提供了通用思路。

Classification reason: 论文核心是条件扩散图像生成方法（以低维注视标签为条件生成精确对应的图像），属于多模态生成方向。虽条件信号为 gaze 标签而非文本，但其本质仍是图像扩散生成与空间条件控制，故复用多模态生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"文生图与图像扩散"。

Importance reason: A competent but niche application of diffusion image generation for gaze-labeled eye-image synthesis, only tangentially matching the user's core interests in autoregressive/continuous multimodal generation and world models. Signals: diffusion image generation, controllable spatial conditioning, low-dimensional label grounding, synthetic training data for eye tracking, domain-specific application

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used to generate synthetic training data, but precise label control remains difficult when the conditioning signal is low-dimensional and coarse. Text-conditioned images are judged by broad prompt consistency, whereas supervised training requires precise correspondence between each image and its numerical label. This is challenging in eye tracking, where a 4D binocular gaze is expressed through subtle, spatially localized pupil and iris geometry. We introduce GazeDiT, a diffusion model that generates images for a requested 4D gaze through an internally constructed spatial condition that grounds the global gaze label in this local geometry. During training, a frozen SegFormer extracts pupil/iris geometry from diverse real images, allowing the model to learn realistic appearance conditioned on that geometry. At inference, a physical eye renderer samples gaze-consistent geometries by varying anatomy and camera state, enabling diverse synthesis without a source image. GazeDiT achieves substantially lower tail gaze-label error than other diffusion baselines, approaching the error of the same frozen gaze estimator on real images. Its generated data also improves the downstream eye tracker, reducing gaze error on difficult cases from 3.05° to 2.80° in the smallest cohort.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond Random Couplings: Contrastive Noise Alignment in Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18488)
*Lennart Wittke, Vinicius Azevedo*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (79.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出对比噪声对齐（CNA）：在流匹配/扩散训练中把噪声批建模为相互作用粒子系统，用 InfoNCE 目标直接优化噪声表示以构建动态的数据-噪声对比耦合，并用角度熵与范数正则保持高斯结构；该方法降低流曲率，在少步（2-4 NFE）像素空间图像生成中将 FID 较标准 rectified flow 降低超 50%、较 OT 基线至少降低 24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流匹配模型通常通过独立采样的高斯噪声来破坏数据，这一前向过程会产生任意的数据-噪声耦合，迫使网络学习连接无关端点的高曲率传输；现有最优传输（OT）方法虽通过重新分配固定噪声样本来减轻该负担，但源噪声分布本身仍是被动固定的。

Method: 提出训练时方法 Contrastive Noise Alignment（CNA）：将每个噪声批次建模为相互作用粒子系统，采用跨模态 InfoNCE 目标将噪声粒子与其配对数据目标对齐，形成动态对比耦合；同时以角度熵正则项与径向范数惩罚防止空间塌缩，并从理论上证明该平衡态渐近保持高斯结构，保证推理阶段依旧易处理。

Result: CNA 改善了噪声与数据之间的对齐、降低了流曲率，并以更少采样步数获得更好的生成质量；在少步像素空间生成（2-4 NFE）设定下，FID 相比标准 rectified flow 降低超过 50%，相比最优传输基线至少降低 24%。

Conclusion: 在保持高斯可处理性的前提下，训练时直接优化噪声表示可显著改善生成流的数据-噪声耦合，大幅提升少步生成质量，为扩散/流匹配训练提供了优于独立高斯噪声与固定 OT 重耦合的新范式。

Classification reason: 论文属于扩散/流匹配模型的图像生成训练方法：CNA 通过优化噪声表示改进数据-噪声耦合、降低流曲率并提升生成质量，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 text_to_image_diffusion（文生图与图像扩散，涵盖基础图像生成方法）最契合；其少步采样收益虽与高效生成相关，但核心贡献是训练耦合优化而非加速蒸馏，故主归文生图与图像扩散。

Importance reason: A solid flow-matching training innovation with strong few-step image generation gains, but limited to pixel-space images and only tangentially touching the user's top continuous-multimodal priority through the shared flow-matching machinery. Signals: novel contrastive noise-data coupling for flow matching, 2-4 NFE few-step generation with >50% FID reduction vs rectified flow, theory preserving Gaussian structure, pixel-space image generation only, no video, autoregressive, or continuous-language relevance

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching models are typically trained by corrupting data through independently sampled Gaussian noise. While simple and scalable, this forward process induces arbitrary data-noise couplings, forcing the network to learn high-curvature transports between unrelated endpoints. Existing optimal-transport methods reduce this burden by reassigning fixed noise samples to data, but the source noise distribution itself remains passive. To address this, we introduce Contrastive Noise Alignment (CNA), a training-time method that creates dynamic, contrastive couplings by optimizing the noise representations directly. By modeling the noise batch as an interacting particle system, CNA employs a cross-modal InfoNCE objective to align noise particles with their paired data targets. To prevent spatial collapse, this alignment is regularized using an angular entropy term and a radial norm penalty. We show theoretically that this equilibrium asymptotically preserves Gaussian structures, maintaining tractability during inference. Empirically, CNA improves the alignment between noise and data, reduces flow curvature, and provides better generation quality with fewer required sampling steps. For few-step, pixel-space generation (2-4 NFEs), CNA reduces FID by over 50\% compared to standard rectified flow, and by at least 24\% against Optimal Transport baselines.

</details>

### [57] [Accelerating Diffusion Sampling via Speculative Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17691)
*Marcello Bullo, Yanxiao Liu, Öykü Sıla Güner, Arpan Mukherjee, Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, stat.AP

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (63.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型推测采样中草稿只能构成线性链的拓扑限制，提出草稿树（draft trees）结构并引入相对熵编码（REC）视角下的贪心拒绝采样作为草稿-目标耦合，在保证目标分布精确的前提下提升接受率、减少昂贵目标评估，实验最多比反射耦合基线加速 8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的推测采样方法（尤其是基于反射最大耦合 reflection maximal coupling 的方法）在拓扑上受限：其前瞻草稿构成链式图（单一线性序列），天然限制了每次目标评估的接受率，难以进一步降低昂贵的目标模型评估次数。

Method: 建立扩散模型推测采样与相对熵编码（REC）的联系，说明前瞻草稿不必呈线性链式结构；提出"草稿树"（draft trees）以丰富每轮候选、降低目标函数评估次数；并采用 REC 算法中的贪心拒绝采样（greedy rejection sampling）作为草稿-目标耦合，在提高接受率的同时保证样本与目标分布完全一致。

Result: 在多种目标模型与草稿模型组合的实验中，相比反射耦合基线最多取得 8.3% 的采样加速，同时保证生成样本精确服从目标分布。

Conclusion: 草稿树结构打破线性推测采样的拓扑限制，结合相对熵编码视角下的贪心拒绝采样耦合，可在保持目标分布精确的前提下有效降低目标评估次数、加速扩散生成，为生成模型高效采样提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文核心是用推测采样（草稿树+贪心拒绝采样）加速扩散模型采样，减少昂贵的目标函数评估，精确对应 multimodal_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子题 efficient_generation（扩散加速与生成效率优化），故直接复用该子题。

Importance reason: A solid but incremental efficiency contribution that fits the user's model-efficiency subtopic, yet it does not touch the highest-priority areas of continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models, and the 8.3% speedup is modest. Signals: speculative sampling for diffusion, draft trees beyond linear chains, relative entropy coding perspective, greedy rejection coupling with exact target distribution, up to 8.3% speedup over reflection coupling

Abstract: Speculative sampling accelerates diffusion model generation by drafting inexpensive candidate states and correcting them under a coupling that preserves the target distribution exactly, reducing the number of expensive target evaluations. Existing diffusion samplers, notably those based on reflection maximal coupling, are topologically constrained: their lookahead drafts form a chain graph, a single linear sequence, which inherently limits the acceptance rate per target evaluation. We connect speculative sampling in diffusion models to relative entropy coding (REC). This perspective shows the lookahead need not be linear and motivates our central contribution, draft trees, which enrich the candidates considered per round and lower the target function evaluations. We further adopt greedy rejection sampling, an REC algorithm, as the draft-target coupling, improving acceptance while guaranteeing exact target samples. Experiments across diverse target and draft models demonstrate up to 8.3% acceleration over the reflection coupling baseline in practical settings.

</details>

### [58] [TTM-Bench: A Framework for Text-to-Music System Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18585)
*Giorgia Adorni, Michela Papandrea, Battista Rimoldi, Tiziano Leidi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.MM, cs.SD, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (43.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出文本到音乐系统基准框架 TTM-Bench，以统一协议从"音乐内容对齐"与"计算效率"两个可解释维度对现有 TTM 系统进行系统化、可复现评测，发现对齐质量与计算开销并不系统相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音乐（TTM）系统在架构、支持的调节条件信息和访问模式上差异巨大，且现有评测指标碎片化、无法跨系统统一应用，导致可靠、可复现的性能比较十分困难。

Method: 定义统一评测协议，沿两个维度评测：一是音乐内容对齐，通过与共同音乐规范对比的可解释语义、流派和音乐描述符一致性分数并汇总为聚合分数；二是计算效率，包括生成延迟、实时因子，以及本地模型的资源占用与托管服务的调用成本；并通过初步对比案例研究演示该框架。

Result: 案例研究表明，音乐内容对齐更高的系统并不必然伴随更低的计算开销；两个维度提供的证据互补，凸显单一总评指标的局限。

Conclusion: 评估文本到音乐系统应采用分离、可解释的多维指标而非单一总分；TTM-Bench 提供了系统性、可复现的基准框架，为文本到音乐系统性能比较建立了共同协议。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是提出文本到音乐生成系统的统一评测基准 TTM-Bench（评测协议、可解释指标与聚合分数），属于多模态生成方向下 [CANONICAL] 子主题"生成模型评测与数据集"的典型范畴；虽然研究对象是音频/音乐生成系统（与 audio_av_generation 相关），但论文本身并非新的生成方法，而是评测框架，故主归 generation_benchmark。

Importance reason: A solid but niche benchmarking framework for text-to-music systems that sits far from the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: text-to-music benchmark, unified evaluation protocol, alignment vs computational efficiency trade-off analysis, preliminary case study only, no overlap with continuous VLM, video generation, or world model priorities

Abstract: Text-to-music (TTM) systems are increasingly used to generate musical audio from natural-language descriptions. Robust evaluation is therefore essential, yet reliable performance comparison remains challenging. This difficulty stems from differences in system architecture, supported conditioning information, and access mode, as well as heterogeneous and fragmented metrics that cannot be applied uniformly across systems. To address these challenges, we introduce TTM-Bench, a framework that defines a common protocol for systematic, reproducible performance benchmarking of contemporary TTM systems. It evaluates performance along two dimensions: musical-content alignment, quantified by interpretable semantic, genre, and musical-descriptor agreement scores against a common musical specification and summarized by an aggregate score; and computational efficiency, characterized by generation latency and real-time factor, alongside resource use for local models and cost for hosted services. We demonstrate the framework through a preliminary comparative case study, illustrating the complementary evidence captured by these dimensions. The results show that higher musical-content alignment does not systematically coincide with lower computational demands, highlighting the importance of assessing TTM performance through distinct, interpretable measures rather than a reductive overall indicator.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Pixel Similarity: Task-Aware Evaluation of GAN-Based Synthetic Sonar Data for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18100)
*Hannan Ejaz Keen, Muhammad Moazam Fraz, Karsten Berns*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (42.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究 GAN 生成的合成声呐图像评测问题：在不同判别器感受野配置（PixelGAN、PatchGAN-16/70、ImageGAN）下，像素级保真度指标（SSIM/PSNR/MSE）与下游目标检测性能不一致，PatchGAN 配置虽像素相似度非最高却检测表现最佳，因此主张对用于机器人感知的合成传感器数据采用"任务感知"评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可降低机器人感知训练数据采集与标注成本，但生成的传感器观测（尤其是声呐图像）难以保证保留下游感知所需的特性。传统图像保真度指标（SSIM、PSNR、MSE）是否能反映合成声呐数据的下游感知性能尚不清楚，亟需验证并探索更贴合任务的评估方式。

Method: 采用 Pix2Pix 条件生成对抗网络，配置四种不同感受野的判别器（PixelGAN、PatchGAN-16、PatchGAN-70、ImageGAN），在两个声呐图像数据集上训练。一方面用 SSIM、PSNR、MSE 等常规图像保真度指标评估生成质量；另一方面进行任务导向评估：仅用真实声呐图像训练 YOLOX-S、YOLOX-L 与 Faster R-CNN 检测器，然后在相同测试样本与标注的 GAN 生成图像上评测检测性能，跨判别器配置进行对比。

Result: 实验揭示图像保真度与下游目标检测性能之间存在明显不一致：取得最佳 SSIM/PSNR/MSE 的配置并不总能带来最佳检测性能；PatchGAN 系列配置虽未取得最高像素级相似度，却在下游检测上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 仅靠像素级图像保真度指标无法稳定刻画合成传感器数据对下游感知任务的真实性；面向机器人感知的合成数据评估应引入任务感知评测，即以训练于真实数据、测试于合成数据的下游检测性能作为补充衡量标准。

Classification reason: 论文核心是用 Pix2Pix 条件 GAN 生成合成声呐图像，并系统比较像素级保真度指标（SSIM/PSNR/MSE）与下游目标检测任务性能，属于生成模型评测与评测指标研究，与 multimodal_generation 方向中"生成式评测"的定位一致，故映射到 [CANONICAL] 子话题"生成模型评测与数据集"(generation_benchmark)。虽然应用背景是机器人声呐感知，但不涉及环境动力学建模、动作条件生成或世界模型，也与连续语言/连续多模态方向无关。

Importance reason: A niche evaluation study of GAN-generated synthetic sonar data for robotic perception with no overlap with the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive/diffusion video generation, and world models. Signals: GAN synthetic sonar data, task-aware evaluation of synthetic data, pixel fidelity metrics misalign with downstream detection, Pix2Pix discriminator receptive field ablation, robotics perception focus

Abstract: Synthetic data can reduce the cost of collecting and annotating training data for robotic perception, but generating sensor observations that preserve the characteristics relevant to downstream perception remains challenging, particularly for sonar imagery. In this work, we investigate whether conventional image-fidelity metrics adequately reflect the downstream perception performance of GAN-generated synthetic sonar data. We employ a Pix2Pix conditional generative adversarial network with four discriminator configurations characterized by different receptive fields: PixelGAN, PatchGAN-16, PatchGAN-70, and ImageGAN. The models are trained using sonar imagery from two datasets and evaluated using conventional image-fidelity metrics, including Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and Mean Squared Error (MSE). To complement these pixel-level measures with task-oriented evaluation, YOLOX-S, YOLOX-L, and Faster R-CNN detectors are trained exclusively on real sonar imagery and subsequently evaluated on the GAN-generated images using identical test samples and annotations across all discriminator configurations. The results reveal a discrepancy between image-fidelity and downstream object-detection performance: the configuration achieving the best SSIM, PSNR, and MSE does not consistently yield the best detection performance. In particular, PatchGAN configurations achieve strong downstream detection results despite not achieving the highest pixel-level similarity scores. These findings suggest, for the datasets and models considered, pixel-level image-fidelity metrics alone may not consistently capture the task-relevant realism of synthetic sonar observations and motivate the use of task-aware evaluation for synthetic sensor data intended for robotic perception.

</details>


### [60] [Newer Is Not Fairer: Gender Stereotyping in Text-to-Image AI Across Model Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18007)
*Shesh Narayan Gupta, Nik Bear Brown*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CY, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (63.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 该研究对 4 代 Stable Diffusion 文生图模型开展大规模性别偏见审计（20 职业 × 5 模板，共 8,000 张图像），发现 76.4% 生成图像为男性、女性职业图像中 57.6% 仍为男性，相对美国 BLS 劳动力数据女性被低代表 20–46 个百分点；偏见从 SD 1.5 到 SDXL 加剧、SD 3 Medium 才部分恢复，GPT-image-1 偏见略低但效应很小——模型代际更新并未带来持续公平性提升，且无一模型实现性别平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图生成模型已在专业与创意场景被广泛使用，但其对职业相关性别刻板印象的呈现是否随模型代际迭代而改善，此前缺乏跨多代模型的系统性量化研究。作者希望回答"更新的模型是否更公平"这一关键问题，为生成模型的公平性审计提供可复现的评测方法与证据。

Method: 构建 20 个职业 × 5 种提示模板 × 4 个 SD 代际（SD 1.5、SD 2.1、SDXL、SD 3 Medium）的评测网格，生成 8,000 张图像（每格 100 张，n=5 提示 × 20 图像），使用 DeepFace 进行性别分类；对各职业-模型单元进行统计显著性检验并做 Benjamini-Hochberg 多重检验校正；将模型输出与美国劳工统计局（BLS）劳动力构成数据对比，量化各职业的性别代表性偏差（百分点差距）；另对 GPT-image-1 在 5 个职业上做探索性对比，并用 Cramer's V 衡量效应量。

Result: 在 8,000 张开源模型图像中，76.4% 呈现男性主体（95% CI [75.1%, 78.7%]，p < 2.2×10^-16，BH 校正）；57.6% 女性编码职业的生成图像仍为男性（BH 校正 p = 1.71×10^-21），全部 9 个显著检验均通过校正；相对 BLS 数据，模型平均将女性低代表 20–46 个百分点，性别均衡职业偏差最大（科学家 BLS 48% 女性但输出 82–99% 男性，清洁工 46% 女性但 80–92% 男性）；代际趋势上，偏见从 SD 1.5 到 SDXL 加剧后在 SD 3 Medium 部分回升（改善）；GPT-image-1 偏见略低于开源模型但效应量小（Cramer's V = 0.080）且对比仅为探索性；无任何模型实现性别平衡。

Conclusion: 新一代文生图模型并不必然更公平：性别刻板印象偏见代际演化非单调，且所有模型均未能实现性别平衡。生成模型的训练与部署流程中应引入系统性公平性基准与代表性校正机制，而非仅依赖模型规模和代际迭代。

Classification reason: 论文对多代文生图扩散模型（SD 1.5/2.1/SDXL/SD 3）进行大规模性别偏见审计与公平性评测，属于生成模型评测/基准研究，与 [CANONICAL] 子话题 generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）高度契合；虽涉及公平性议题，但非越狱/攻防类工作，故主方向归入 multimodal_generation。

Importance reason: A rigorous but method-free fairness audit of text-to-image models, tangential to the user's focus on generation architectures, continuous/latent models, and world models. Signals: 8,000-image gender bias audit across 4 Stable Diffusion generations, cross-generation bias comparison with BLS benchmarks, statistically rigorous (BH correction), evaluation-only, no new generation methods, weak overlap with priority subtopics (only text-to-image evaluation)

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models are widely used in professional and creative settings, yet how they represent gender across occupations -- and whether newer models are fairer -- remains poorly understood across multiple generations. We evaluate gender representation across 20 occupations, 5 prompt templates, and 4 Stable Diffusion model generations (SD 1.5, SD 2.1, SDXL, SD 3 Medium), generating 8,000 images with n = 100 per occupation-model cell (5 prompts x 20 images), and classifying all with DeepFace. Across the 8,000 open-source images, 76.4% show male subjects (95% CI [75.1%, 78.7%], p < 2.2 x 10^-16, Benjamini-Hochberg adjusted). More strikingly, 57.6% of images for historically female-coded occupations show male subjects (raw p = 3.43 x 10^-22, BH-adjusted p = 1.71 x 10^-21). All nine significant tests reported in this paper survive BH correction across 10 tests. When compared against U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce data, models underrepresent women by 20-46pp on average, with particularly large deviations for near gender-balanced occupations: scientist (48% female in BLS, 82-99% male in model outputs) and cleaner (46% female in BLS, 80-92% male in outputs). Model generations do not improve steadily: bias worsens from SD 1.5 to SDXL before partially recovering in SD 3 Medium. A preliminary comparison with GPT-image-1 on five occupations suggests lower bias than open-source models, though the practical effect is small (Cramer's V = 0.080) and the comparison is exploratory. No model achieves gender parity.

</details>


### [61] [Can MiniMax-H3 Reason About the Physical World? An Evaluation of Omni-Modal Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18323)
*Haoyu Zhao, Zihao Zhao, Tianyu Deng, Ziqin Xu, Zihao Zhang, Xudong Wang, Jinxiang Guo, Chen Gao, Ziyi Ye, Yeying Jin, Jiaxi Gu, Zuxuan Wu, Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (84.72) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出面向全模态生成模型（Omni-Model）的物理世界推理评测框架，以 MiniMax-H3 为评测对象，设计隐式提示+多帧、音频-图像、前缀视频、音频-视频四类需要跨模态互补信息整合的任务。在 517 个实例上模型整体成功率仅 41.97%（视频决策推理 56.00% 最高，音频消歧推理 27.40% 最低），表明多模态整合能力仍是全模态生成模型发挥世界推理潜力的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成与世界模型的评测框架通常受限于输入模态单一、且提示内容与目标视频高度匹配的设定，难以检验全模态生成模型的跨模态整合与世界推理能力。论文旨在回答两个问题：多模态对齐能否提升模型的世界推理能力，以及全模态输入能催生何种新的评测范式。

Method: 构建围绕四个互补维度的物理世界推理评测框架：(1) 隐式提示+多帧输入；(2) 音频-图像输入；(3) 前缀视频输入；(4) 音频-视频输入。每种模态仅提供部分证据，要求模型联合推理互补语义线索以推断潜在事件状态与未来动态，共构建 517 个评测实例，并以任务成功率量化评测 MiniMax-H3 的表现。

Result: 在 517 个评测实例上，MiniMax-H3 整体成功率为 41.97%；其中基于视频的决策推理成功率最高（56.00%），基于音频的消歧推理最弱（27.40%），表明有效的多模态整合仍是充分发挥多模态输入优势的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 全模态生成模型在物理世界推理上仍显著依赖跨模态整合能力，当前整合尚不充分；多模态互补输入提供了超越传统视频生成评测的新范式，该框架可作为统一全模态模型世界推理能力的评测基准。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是面向全模态生成模型（MiniMax-H3，联合音视频生成）的评测框架与基准任务集，属于生成模型评测，故主方向映射到 multimodal_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] generation_benchmark；同时其考察物理世界推理与未来动态预测，与 world_model 方向相关；被评模型为理解+生成一体的统一全模态模型，与 multimodal_understanding_generation 亦相关。

Importance reason: An evaluation-only study of a single omni-modal generative model's physical world reasoning, touching world-model and unified multimodal themes but offering no new methods for the user's top-priority continuous multimodal or video generation directions. Signals: omni-modal generation evaluation, physical world reasoning benchmark, MiniMax-H3, cross-modal integration bottleneck, implicit prompt and audio-video tasks

Abstract: Recent Omni-Modal Generative Models (Omni-Models) have advanced content generation toward unified modeling of text, images, video, and audio. MiniMax-H3 exemplifies this transition by combining multimodal context understanding with joint audio-visual generation in a shared latent framework. Its unified architecture raises a fundamental question: Can multimodal alignment improve the model's world reasoning, and what new evaluation paradigms do omni-modal inputs enable? To investigate this question, this work introduces a comprehensive evaluation framework organized around four complementary dimensions of physical world reasoning. Unlike existing evaluation frameworks for video generation and world models, which are often constrained by limited input modalities and evaluation settings where prompts closely match the target video content, our evaluation is specifically designed to exploit the multimodal inputs of Omni-Model. We construct a diverse set of novel tasks that require models to integrate complementary information across modalities. Specifically, we consider four scenarios, including implicit prompts paired with multiple frames, audio-image, prefix-videos, and audio-video inputs. Every single modality provides only partial evidence about the underlying event, requiring the model to jointly reason over the complementary semantic cues to infer latent event states and future dynamics. Across 517 evaluation instances, MiniMax-H3 achieves an overall success rate of 41.97%. Video-based Decision Reasoning yields the highest success rate at 56.00%, while Audio-based Disambiguation Reasoning is the weakest, reaching only 27.40%. These results indicate that effective multimodal integration remains key to fully exploiting the benefits of diverse input modalities. The project is available at .

</details>


### [62] [Reporting Practice Matters: The Impact of Reference Choice on Chest X-ray Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19093)
*Daniel P. Jeong, Charles Q. Li, Hossein Hosseiny, Nitya M. Bhalla, Fatma Uyar Morency, Pradeep Ravikumar, Zachary C. Lipton, Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文量化了放射报告书写规范差异对 AI 报告生成（RRG）评测的干扰：提出放射科医生指导的报告实践差异 taxonomy 与参考报告改写方法 ReRef，证明仅改变参考报告的书写风格即可颠覆九个 RRG 模型的排名（如 Libra 与 CheXone 的位次互换），并发布 MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef 数据集（120 对参考报告）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生遵循异质的报告书写规范：即使对同一张影像识别出相同的临床发现，两位医生也可能因术语、简写、格式与详略程度不同而写出表面差异明显的报告。现有 AI 报告生成（RRG）评测通常以机器生成报告与人工参考报告的一致性为准，这种报告规范差异构成了一个被低估的评测障碍。

Method: 提出放射科医生参与的放射报告书写实践差异 taxonomy（术语、简写、格式、详略程度等维度）；在此基础上提出 ReRef 方法，沿 taxonomy 各轴重写参考报告同时保持临床解释不变；并在 MIMIC-CXR 上用 RadCliQ-v1 等指标对九个 RRG 模型开展参考改写下的敏感性分析。

Result: 评测指标对报告书写实践变化高度敏感，足以改变模型排名：例如在 MIMIC-CXR 上使用 RadCliQ-v1 比较九个 RRG 模型时，压缩参考报告中正常发现的讨论后，Libra 从第一名降至第二名，CheXone 从第三名升至第一名。结果表明许多现有指标无法将临床解释与报告规范一致性解耦。作者发布放射科医生验证的 MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef 数据集，含 120 对（原始、改写）参考报告。

Conclusion: 参考报告的书写规范选择对 RRG 模型评测结果影响重大，实践中应选取准确反映期望报告规范的“正确”参考；现有指标需能解耦临床解释与规范一致性，作者开源的 MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef 数据集可支撑后续评测方法研究。

Classification reason: 论文核心是放射报告生成（RRG）这一视觉到文本生成任务的评测问题：量化评测指标对参考报告书写规范变化的敏感性并提出新基准数据集，与 canonical 子主题「生成模型评测与数据集」高度契合，故复用之；同时涉及视觉语言生成模型的医学评测，可关联多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: 虽然对生成模型评测方法学有扎实贡献，但主题是胸部X光报告的临床评测，与用户关注的连续多模态、视频/图像生成及世界模型方向几乎无关且命中负向关键词。 Signals: 生成模型评测方法学, 参考报告改写影响模型排名, 医学影像报告生成（负向: clinical/medical）, 无用户优先子topic命中, 发布MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集

Abstract: Radiologists follow heterogeneous reporting practices. Two radiologists examining the same image and identifying the same clinical findings might nevertheless compose superficially distinct reports, varying in terminology, shorthand, formatting, and level of detail. These variations in reporting norms represent an under-appreciated obstacle in efforts to evaluate AI-based radiology report generation (RRG) models, where machine-generated reports are typically assessed based on their concordance with human-generated references. In this paper, we quantify the sensitivity of established evaluation metrics to variations in reporting practices, revealing impacts large enough to alter the rankings of models. We introduce a radiologist-informed taxonomy of variations in radiology reporting practice and a method (ReRef) that rewrites reference reports along the axes of our taxonomy while preserving clinical interpretation. For instance, when comparing the performance of nine RRG models on MIMIC-CXR using RadCliQ-v1, condensing the discussion of normal findings in the reference reports causes Libra to drop from first to second place while CheXOne rises from third to first. Our results suggest that many current metrics fail to decouple clinical interpretation from conformity to reporting practices and that choosing the ``right'' references that accurately reflect the desired reporting practices can be important in practice. To support future research, we release MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef, a radiologist-validated dataset of 120 (original, alternative) reference report pairs derived from MIMIC-CXR.

</details>

### [63] [Visual Autoregressive Priors for RAW-to-sRGB Image Signal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18302)

Main category: eess.IV, cs.MM, cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次将 VAR next-scale 预测先验用于 RAW-to-sRGB ISP：冻结 1.1B VAR 主干仅训练约 33M 参数，配合频率分解颜色损失，在 Zurich RAW-to-sRGB 上 PSNR-Y 21.31→21.89 dB、LPIPS 0.276→0.218；诊断表明结构保持良好，但连续颜色迁移仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAW-to-sRGB ISP 需要在空间错位、相机元数据缺失等不完美条件下恢复感知真实的颜色与细节；传统回归式方法易过度平滑，作者希望借助强生成式自回归先验改善结构与细节重建。

Method: 以 RAW 图像为条件，复用冻结的 1.10B 参数 VAR 主干（next-scale 预测 + 离散 codebook），仅训练 32.93M（2.99%）参数完成 RAW 条件 ISP；提出频率分解颜色损失：低频色调用小波 LL 余弦相似度监督、色度边缘用细节带 ℓ1 监督。

Result: 在 Zurich RAW-to-sRGB 全量 1204 张测试集上，PSNR-Y 由 21.31 提升至 21.89 dB，LPIPS 由 0.276 降至 0.218；诊断实验显示 VAR 先验擅长保持结构，但连续颜色迁移是主要瓶颈：oracle 仿射校正可再提升 3.8 dB，而学习的颜色头收益有限。

Conclusion: 冻结的大规模自回归视觉先验（VAR）可低成本迁移到 RAW-to-sRGB ISP 并显著提升感知质量，但颜色域的连续校准仍是开放瓶颈，后续需针对连续颜色迁移设计更强的监督与机制。

Classification reason: 论文核心是以 VAR next-scale 预测（离散 codebook 自回归）作为视觉生成先验来做 RAW→sRGB 条件图像转换，属于自回归视觉生成范式（VAR）在低层视觉任务上的应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子话题 autoregressive_visual_generation（含 VAR/MAR/LlamaGen 等范式）直接相关，故复用该子话题；与连续语言/世界模型/理解方向无关。

Importance reason: A competent first application of frozen VAR next-scale priors to RAW-to-sRGB ISP with parameter-efficient adaptation, but it is a niche low-level vision restoration task rather than core generative, continuous-token, or world-model work aligned with the user's top priorities. Signals: VAR next-scale prior transferred to ISP, frozen 1.1B backbone with only 33M trainable params (efficient adaptation), benchmark gains PSNR-Y 21.31→21.89 and LPIPS 0.276→0.218 on Zurich RAW-to-sRGB, diagnosis that continuous color transfer is the bottleneck, autoregressive image modeling relevance but no video/world-model/continuous-latent content

Abstract: RAW-to-sRGB image signal processing (ISP) must recover perceptually faithful colors and fine details from sensor measurements, often under imperfect spatial alignment and missing camera metadata. This paper presents, to the best of our knowledge, the first application of visual autoregressive (VAR) next-scale prediction over a discrete image codebook to the RAW-to-sRGB ISP task. We adapt a frozen 1.10\,B-parameter VAR backbone for RAW-conditioned ISP with only 32.93\,M trainable parameters (2.99\%), and propose a frequency-decomposed color loss that separately supervises low-frequency tone via wavelet LL cosine similarity and chromatic edges via detail-band $\ell_1$. On the Zurich RAW-to-sRGB benchmark, the method improves PSNR-Y from 21.31 to 21.89\,dB and reduces LPIPS from 0.276 to 0.218 on the full 1,204-image test set. Diagnostic experiments show that the VAR prior preserves structure well, but continuous color transfer remains the dominant bottleneck: oracle affine correction recovers 3.8\,dB, while learned color heads yield marginal gains.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级视听语音增强（AVSE）模型面临计算效率与跨模态对齐精度之间的关键权衡：简单拼接缺乏关系表达能力，而稠密交叉注意力计算开销大，且在强声学干扰下容易产生不可靠的跨模态对应。

Method: 提出 SG-Mamba：将稀疏异构图与线性复杂度 Mamba 主干结合，图中通过内容自适应注意力建模模态内关系、并引入跨帧视听连接显式建模跨模态关系；Mamba 负责捕获长程时序上下文；另加入音频跳跃连接以在不牺牲降噪能力的前提下保留频谱细节。

Result: 在 LRS3 上达到纯噪声条件下 13.091 dB SI-SDR，性能相当或优于强轻量基线；在嘈杂多说话人条件下仍保持稳健，计算成本仅 3.45 G MACs（6.90 G FLOPs）；VoxCeleb2 上的结果表明显式结构先验提升了轻量 AVSE 的鲁棒性、泛化性与计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式稀疏图结构先验与线性复杂度 Mamba 的结合，使轻量级 AVSE 在计算效率、跨模态对齐精度与噪声鲁棒性之间取得更好平衡，显式结构先验有助于提升鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Classification reason: 论文为视听语音增强（AVSE），本质是以视觉为条件的音频生成/恢复类任务，与 multimodal_generation 下的 CANONICAL 子主题 audio_av_generation（音频生成、音视频联合生成、跨模态条件音频生成）最为接近；与连续语言/连续多模态、世界模型、多模态理解各方向均不匹配，故复用该现有子主题。

Importance reason: A solid lightweight audio-visual speech enhancement paper using Mamba, but it falls outside the user's core priorities in video/image generation and continuous multimodal models, with only a weak tie to model efficiency. Signals: linear-complexity Mamba backbone, audio-visual cross-modal fusion, speech enhancement domain mismatch, lightweight efficiency angle, no overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Lightweight audio-visual speech enhancement (AVSE) models face a critical trade-off between computational efficiency and cross-modal alignment accuracy. While simple concatenation lacks relational expressiveness, dense cross-attention incurs computational overhead and is prone to unreliable cross-modal correspondence under strong acoustic interference. We propose Sparse Graph-Guided Mamba (SG-Mamba), a lightweight AVSE framework that integrates a sparse heterogeneous graph with a linear-complexity Mamba backbone. The graph explicitly models modality-specific relations through content-adaptive attention and cross-frame audio-visual connections, while Mamba captures long-range temporal context. We further introduce an audio skip connection to preserve spectral detail without sacrificing noise suppression. Evaluated on LRS3, SG-Mamba achieves competitive or superior performance against strong lightweight baselines and reaches 13.091 dB SI-SDR under noise-only condition. It also remains robust in cluttered multi-speaker conditions with a competitive cost of 3.45 G MACs (or 6.90 G FLOPs). Results on VoxCeleb2 further suggest that explicit structural priors improve robustness, generalizability, and computational efficiency in lightweight AVSE.

</details>


### [65] [CPR: Combining global composing, local performing and full-sequence refining in piano rendering with continuous autoregressive modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18216)
*Chong Jing, Junan Zhang, Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 高优先级 (86.3) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CPR 框架用连续自回归建模做钢琴 MIDI-to-Music 渲染：Composer 自回归预测连续隐状态，Performer 通过局部流匹配生成 24kHz 声学 latent，Refiner 上采样至 48kHz，并引入 BREPA 与 MT-RoPE 增强音乐语义结构与跨模态时序对齐，兼得 AR 的条件跟随与流匹配的声学保真能力且避免量化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 prompt 条件钢琴 MIDI-to-Music 渲染方法主要遵循两种范式：离散 codec 的自回归模型具备因果时序建模能力，但离散量化会丢失声学细节；流匹配/扩散模型能更好保留声学结构，但需要全序列注意力、计算代价高且语义结构较弱。作者希望找到一种兼具 AR 条件跟随能力与流匹配分布建模能力、同时绕过量化瓶颈且计算更高效的音乐渲染建模范式。

Method: 提出 Composer-Performer-Refiner（CPR）三段式连续自回归框架：Composer 在连续隐状态上自回归预测（全局作曲，保持因果时序建模）；Performer 通过局部流匹配生成 24kHz 声学 latent（局部演奏，保留声学结构并降低全序列注意力开销）；Refiner 将波形上采样至 48kHz（全序列精修）。并引入两项技术：Bottlenecked Representation Alignment（BREPA）强化 Composer 隐状态中的音乐语义结构，Modality-Time RoPE（MT-RoPE）增强 MIDI 与音频模态间的时序对齐。

Result: CPR 将 AR 的条件跟随能力与流匹配的分布建模能力相结合，直接在连续表征上建模，绕过量化瓶颈并降低计算成本；配合 BREPA 与 MT-RoPE，在钢琴 MIDI-to-Music 渲染上实现了对目标音符的忠实渲染与对参考录音音色的再现（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 连续自回归建模是统一 AR 条件跟随与流匹配分布建模能力的有效范式；CPR 验证了该范式在音乐渲染任务上的优势，为基于连续表征的条件音频/音乐生成提供了新框架。

Classification reason: 论文核心任务是 prompt 条件的音乐音频生成（钢琴 MIDI-to-Music 渲染），直接对应 multimodal_generation 方向下的 canonical 子主题 audio_av_generation（音乐生成、跨模态条件音频生成）。方法上采用连续自回归建模 + flow matching 生成连续声学 latent、规避离散量化瓶颈，与 continuous_language_multimodal 方向"连续潜空间/latent flow"的方法学主题相关，故列为次匹配方向，但论文既非语言模型也非视觉-语言模型，主方向仍归为多模态生成。

Importance reason: Methodologically strong fit to the user's top continuous-autoregressive-plus-flow-matching priority, but applied to piano MIDI-to-audio rendering rather than the user's core vision/language/video focus. Signals: continuous autoregressive modeling, flow matching on acoustic latents, avoids discrete quantization bottleneck, cross-modal temporal alignment via MT-RoPE, audio/music domain off user's main video/image track

Abstract: Prompt-conditioned piano MIDI-to-Music rendering aims to faithfully render target notes while reproducing the timbre of a reference recording. Existing approaches primarily follow two paradigms: autoregressive (AR) modeling and flow matching (or diffusion). Discrete-codec AR models provide causal temporal modeling, but quantization can discard acoustic detail. Flow matching better preserves acoustic structure in the cost of full-sequence attention costs and worse semantic structure. Continuous autoregressive models operate directly on continuous representations. It not only combines the condition-following ability of AR models and distribution-modeling capacity of flow matching but also bypasses the quantization bottleneck with lower computational costs. Building on this principle, we present Composer--Performer--Refiner (CPR) framework. Composer autoregressively predicts continuous hidden states, Performer generates 24kHz acoustic latents through local flow matching and Refiner then upsamples the waveform to 48 kHz. We further introduce Bottlenecked Representation Alignment (BREPA) and Modality--Time RoPE (MT-RoPE) to strengthen musical semantic structure in Composer hidden states and temporal alignments across modalities. Codes are available at

</details>


### [66] [GrainSpeech: Less Context, More Detail for Compact Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18856)
*Zitao Liang, Chang Gao*

Main category: eess.AS, eess.SP, cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 值得关注 (71.25) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GrainSpeech 研究紧凑语音合成声学模型的质量-容量权衡：发现自注意力感受野扩展超过约 15 个音素不再带来增益，据此提出固定感受野卷积编码器，并设计 Mel 特定的梯度方差监督以恢复频谱细尺度细节。模型仅 264.8K 参数，在微控制器上实现 17.9 倍实时 Mel 生成，UTMOS 与大得多的模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型声学模型面临质量与容量的权衡。作者希望通过研究编码器上下文（感受野）与 Mel 频谱监督方式两个因素，理解并突破这一权衡，使语音合成模型能在资源受限的微控制器（MCU）上高效运行。

Method: 首先进行感受野缩放实验，考察自注意力上下文长度对声学预测的影响；据此设计固定感受野的卷积编码器；其次提出 Mel 特定的梯度方差监督方案，包括轴方向特定梯度、重叠局部统计与对数域方差匹配，用于恢复频谱细尺度纹理。

Result: 感受野缩放研究表明超过 15 个音素的自注意力不再带来音高、能量或时长预测的一致增益；固定感受野卷积编码器将三项预测误差分别降低 36.0%、17.3% 和 3.4%。GrainSpeech 仅 264.8K 参数，在 MCU 上达到 17.9 倍实时 Mel 生成，UTMOS 与参数量大得多的模型相当（参数量不足其 1.5%）。

Conclusion: 在紧凑声学建模中，有限感受野（约15音素）已足够，Mel 特定的梯度方差监督可在不损害预测质量的前提下恢复细尺度细节；GrainSpeech 以不足大模型 1.5% 的参数量在微控制器上实现可比音质的实时语音合成，为端侧部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 论文属于语音合成（TTS）声学模型研究，属于音频生成范畴。方向上匹配 multimodal_generation（关键词含 audio generation），子主题复用 [CANONICAL] 的 audio_av_generation（明确覆盖"音乐/语音生成、TTS"），与其他方向（连续语言模型、世界模型、多模态理解）无实质关联。

Importance reason: A well-executed but narrow compact TTS paper whose main overlap with the user's priorities is only the model-efficiency subtopic, with no connection to continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: compact 264.8K-parameter acoustic model, 17.9x real-time Mel generation on MCU, fixed receptive field encoder finding, Mel-specific gradient-variance supervision, partial match to model efficiency subtopic

Abstract: Compact acoustic models face a challenging quality-capacity trade-off. We investigate two factors in this regime: encoder context and Mel-spectrogram supervision. A receptive-field-scaling study shows that expanding self-attention beyond 15 phonemes provides no consistent gains in pitch, energy, or duration prediction. Guided by this finding, we introduce a fixed-receptive-field convolutional encoder that reduces the respective prediction errors by 36.0%, 17.3%, and 3.4%. We further show that directly transferring image-domain gradient-variance supervision restores fine-scale variation but degrades predicted quality, motivating a Mel-specific formulation with axis-specific gradients, overlapping local statistics, and log-domain variance matching. GrainSpeech contains only 264.8K parameters and achieves 17.9x real-time Mel generation on a microcontroller (MCU), while attaining UTMOS scores comparable to substantially larger models with less than 1.5% of their parameters. Source code and demos are available at .

</details>
