### [67] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh, Rhythm Bhatia, Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (49.38) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对 CLIP 系列视觉语言模型在大都会艺术博物馆档案藏品上的"杰作/质量/影响力"零样本估值进行了受控偏见审计：在控制媒介、年代、长宽比等混杂因素并使用 TOST 等效性检验后，未发现显著的艺术家性别效应，且零样本 logit 差值接近嵌入噪声底线，说明宏观零样本指标是粗糙的偏见测量工具；审计还须考虑档案幸存偏差等来源性混杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对视觉语言模型（VLM）进行社会偏见审计时，需要将算法本身的估值差异与档案元数据中的混杂因素（如机构幸存偏差、佚名作品、媒介与年代分布）区分开来，否则容易得出错误的公平性结论。

Method: 基于大都会艺术博物馆 Open Access 收藏（N=1,500 件，其中署名作品 743 件：男性 534、女性 209，618 件佚名）构建定量审计框架；对 OpenAI CLIP 与 OpenCLIP 计算三组语义提示对（杰作、质量、影响力）下的零样本 logit 差值；采用 TOST 双单侧等效性检验（Cohen's d≥0.25 界限）与控制媒介、创作年代、长宽比的多变量 OLS 回归进行条件效应分析。

Result: 未校正评估显示男女性别在 CLIP/OpenCLIP 零样本估值分数上无统计显著主效应（p>0.12），TOST 证实统计等效（pTOST<0.005）；控制混杂因素后艺术家性别仍无显著条件效应（p>0.20），且残差嵌入方差极高（R²<2%），表明全局零样本 logit 差值接近嵌入噪声底线，是粗糙的测量工具。研究同时指出两点局限：宏观等效不排除局部微观视觉偏见，且排除 41.2% 未署名藏品引入了机构幸存偏差。

Conclusion: 在文化遗产场景中评估 AI 公平性时，必须进行多变量混杂控制、等效性检验与档案来源/幸存偏差审计；宏观零样本指标等价不等于模型公平，应发展更细粒度的视觉-语义偏见测量手段。

Classification reason: 该论文是对 CLIP 等视觉语言模型的社会偏见审计（性别估值偏差、公平性评测），属于 VLM 安全评测范畴，故归入"多模态理解与生成"方向下既有的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态安全与攻防"（其"安全评测"涵盖偏见/公平性审计）；与连续语言模型、世界模型、多模态生成等其他方向无关。

Importance reason: A methodologically careful but narrow fairness audit of CLIP artwork valuation that touches none of the user's priority subtopics (continuous multimodal models, video generation, world models). Signals: CLIP zero-shot bias audit, TOST equivalence testing, multivariate confound control (medium/era/aspect ratio), no significant artist-gender effect, archival survival-bias caveat

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous). We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005). Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness. We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [68] [Not All Patches Are Equally Forgettable: Spatially Localized Domain Unlearning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17790)
*Akanksha Singh, Vinod K. Kurmi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段 patch 级选择框架，定位并衰减 VLM 中对遗忘域预测贡献过大的视觉 patch 区域，实现空间局部化的域遗忘，在改善遗忘-保留权衡的同时将保留域识别性能提升至多 3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）具备强大的跨域识别能力，但这种鲁棒性也会保留不希望的域特定行为；域相关信息与语义信息在表征空间中相互纠缠，导致选择性域遗忘困难。现有方法多依赖潜空间解耦或 prompt/特征级干预，未能直接归因并抑制各 patch token 的贡献。

Method: 提出两阶段 patch 选择框架：第一阶段估计每个视觉 patch 的域敏感度，第二阶段选择性地衰减那些对遗忘域预测贡献强于其语义效用的 patch 区域，利用 ViT 的空间结构实现局部化抑制，而非均匀压缩整体表征。

Result: 在 Office-Home、Mini DomainNet 和 DomainNet 上的实验表明，该框架相比已有方法取得更好的遗忘-保留权衡，保留域识别性能最高提升 3.8%；在视觉重叠与未见域设置下的额外评测进一步证明了其在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 利用 ViT 空间结构进行 patch 级选择性抑制，比均匀抑制整体表征更有效，可为 VLM 的选择性域遗忘与隐私安全防护提供更精细的干预范式。

Classification reason: 论文研究 VLM 的选择性域遗忘（机器遗忘），属于去除不良域知识、隐私与安全防护范畴，最贴合 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 canonical 子主题「多模态安全与攻防」；与连续语言/多模态、世界模型、多模态生成方向均无关，故不新建子主题。

Importance reason: A competent VLM domain-unlearning method, but its safety/unlearning focus matches none of the user's priority topics (continuous multimodal models, video/world models, generation, efficiency). Signals: patch-level selective unlearning, VLM domain forgetting, forgetting-retention tradeoff, vision transformer interpretability, no overlap with continuous models, video generation, or world models

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong cross-domain recognition performance even without additional training. However, this robustness can also preserve undesirable domain-specific behavior, as domain-related and semantic information often remain entangled within the learned representation space, making selective domain unlearning challenging. Existing approaches typically address this problem through latent-space disentanglement and prompt- or feature-level interventions, without directly attributing and attenuating individual patch-token contributions. However, here we suggest that rather than uniformly suppressing the full representation, it may be more effective to exploit the spatial structure of vision transformers to localize and suppress patch regions that contribute disproportionately to forget-domain prediction. Patches that strongly influence forget-domain prediction may not be equally important for semantic recognition, suggesting that forgetting should be guided according to the domain contribution of different visual regions. Specifically, we propose a two-stage patch-selective framework that first estimates patch-level domain sensitivity and then selectively attenuates patches whose contribution to forget-domain prediction is stronger than their semantic utility. We evaluate our framework on Office-Home, Mini DomainNet, and DomainNet. Experimental results demonstrate improved forgetting-retention tradeoffs compared to prior methods while improving retained-domain recognition by up to 3.8\%. Additional evaluations under visually overlapping and unseen-domain settings further demonstrate improved robustness under distribution shift.

</details>


### [69] [Visual Compliance via Executable Safety Rule Entailment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18328)
*Jisoo Kim, TaeYoon Kwack, Jinwoo Jang, Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (53.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 GuardEn：将安全策略编译为原子命题的可执行代码组合，测试时利用场景图提供的视觉上下文实例化命题并执行规则推理，实现可解释的视觉安全合规判定；在 SafetyVisionBench 上比最强基线平均提升 9.8 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 与 VLM 的进展使安全系统能够从简单风险模式识别走向更具上下文与语义层面的安全考量；但随风险模式不断演化、安全规则日趋复杂，现有基于训练的端到端防护方法在适应性和对复杂安全规则的可解释推理方面持续面临挑战，需要更灵活、可解释的防护范式。

Method: 提出 GuardEn 框架，分两阶段工作：(1) Safety-Rule Compilation：将安全策略分解为原子命题，并把命题间的组合关系建模为可执行代码；(2) Scene-Grounded Execution：测试时借助场景图获取上下文视觉信息，实例化各原子命题并执行规则推理，输出基于规则且可解释的安全判定。

Result: 在 SafetyVisionBench 上验证了该可编程防护方法对复杂视觉安全评估的有效性，相较最强基线平均提升 9.8 F1，同时具备规则接地的可解释推理能力。

Conclusion: 将安全策略编译为可执行的原子命题组合，并在测试时以场景图信息进行规则接地执行，可为复杂视觉安全评估提供比训练式端到端防护更适应规则演化、更可解释的解决方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出 GuardEn 可执行安全防护框架，用于视觉内容的安全规则推理与合规判定，属于多模态安全/防护评测范畴，映射到 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向的 [CANONICAL] 子话题 multimodal_safety_attack（多模态安全与攻防），故复用该子话题。

Importance reason: A reasonably novel interpretable VLM safety framework with solid benchmark gains, but it targets safety compliance rather than the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: VLM safety and compliance, executable safety-rule compilation, scene-graph grounded reasoning, new SafetyVisionBench benchmark with +9.8 F1, interpretable entailment-style safeguard

Abstract: Recent advances in LLMs and VLMs have enabled safety systems to reason beyond simple risk patterns toward more contextual and semantic safety concerns. However, as risk patterns continue to evolve and safety rules become more complex, existing training-based end-to-end safeguards face persistent challenges in adaptability and explainable reasoning over complex safety rules. To address these challenges, we propose GuardEn (Guarding by Safety Rule Entailment), an executable safeguard framework that decomposes safety policies into atomic propositions through Safety-Rule Compilation, modeling their composition as executable code. At test time, Scene-Grounded Execution instantiates these atomic propositions with contextual visual information derived from scene graphs, enabling rule-grounded and interpretable safety reasoning. Experiments on SafetyVisionBench demonstrate the effectiveness of programmable safeguard for complex visual safety assessment, achieving an average improvement of 9.8 F1 points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [70] [Stealthy in Semantics, Antagonistic in Space: Attacking Visible-Infrared Object Detectors via Object-Level Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18133)
*Yueqi Zhu, Qi Ming, Guo Cheng, Yongkang Zhang, Feiran Liu, Juan Fang, Jiahuan Zhou, Jiangmeng Li, Yuhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出针对可见光-红外目标检测器的隐蔽对抗攻击框架 CamoShift：通过语义伪装补丁与目标级红外偏移打破跨模态空间对齐、破坏融合过程，在攻击有效性与视觉隐蔽性之间取得优越平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外目标检测器用于恶劣光照与天气条件下的鲁棒感知，但现有物理对抗攻击需在空间对齐的目标区域粘贴显眼补丁，易被人类观察者察觉；且多数方法仅扰动对齐区域内的外观，未显式针对模态间的对应关系或跨模态融合过程。

Method: 提出 CamoShift 框架：1) 语义伪装模块（SCM）生成可附着于宿主目标的隐蔽伪装补丁，并通过 RGB-IR 适配器保证其在红外分支中的有效性；2) 目标级空间解耦模块（OSDM）以尺度感知方式偏移红外目标证据，破坏目标级对应关系并干扰跨模态融合；3) 谐波对抗损失（HarAdv loss）在优化中平衡攻击强度与视觉隐蔽性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CamoShift 在攻击有效性与视觉隐蔽性之间取得了优于现有方法的平衡；据作者所知，这是首个同时兼顾视觉隐蔽性与攻击成功率、面向可见光-红外目标检测的对抗攻击工作。

Conclusion: 结合视觉伪装与目标级红外偏移可实现既隐蔽又高效的跨模态失配攻击，揭示了可见光-红外检测器在物理对抗场景下的脆弱性，为多模态感知系统的安全性评估与防御研究提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 论文提出针对可见光-红外双模态目标检测器的对抗攻击框架，通过破坏跨模态空间对齐与融合实现隐蔽攻击，属于跨模态对抗攻击与多模态安全范畴，映射到多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: A niche adversarial-attack paper on visible-infrared object detection that does not touch any of the user's priority areas (continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, world models, unified MLLMs). Signals: adversarial attack framework, visible-infrared object detection, cross-modal spatial alignment disruption, multimodal security niche, no generative, continuous-token, diffusion, or world-model content

Abstract: Visible-infrared object detectors are used for robust perception under challenging illumination and weather conditions. Current physical attacks apply conspicuous patches to spatially aligned target regions, which are noticeable to human observers. Meanwhile, most of these methods only perturb the appearance within the aligned region, without explicitly targeting the correspondence between modalities or the fusion process. In this paper, we propose CamoShift, an adversarial framework for visible-infrared object detection. By combining visual camouflage with object-level infrared shifting, CamoShift breaks cross-modal spatial alignment and disrupts fusion. Specifically, the Semantic Camouflage Module (SCM) generates a stealthy camouflaged patch that can be attached to the host object and maintains its effectiveness in the infrared branch through an RGB-IR adapter. The Object-level Spatial Decoupling Module (OSDM) shifts the infrared target evidence in a scale-aware manner, so as to break object-level correspondence and disrupt cross-modal fusion. Then, the Harmonic Adversarial loss (HarAdv loss) further balances attack strength and visual stealth during optimization. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to target both visual stealthiness and attack success in visible-infrared object detection. Extensive experimental results show that CamoShift achieves a superior balance between attack effectiveness and visual stealth. Code and models will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [71] [Decodable but Misrouted: Sparse Features Uncover a Readout Gap in Vision-Language Models for Harmful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18860)
*Girish A. Koushik, Diptesh Kanojia, Helen Treharne*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (51.72) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 该论文用稀疏自编码器、角色条件探针与因果干预分析 Gemma-3 和 Qwen 在有害 meme 检测中的失败，发现模型内部已编码关键证据但未能正确"路由"到输出（readout gap）：稀疏读出在六个基准上全面优于原生预测（Qwen 0.740 vs 0.432 macro-F1），仅校准路由即可恢复 93.3% 的差距，探针蒸馏 LoRA 也能提升原生性能，表明路由而非表征是有害内容分类的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当视觉语言模型误判有害 meme 时，失败究竟源于内部缺失关键证据，还是证据已被表征却无法路由到输出？作者希望区分这两种失败模式，定位可解释的性能瓶颈，并为安全检测的模型改进提供依据。

Method: 在 Gemma-3 与 Qwen 上使用稀疏自编码器提取内部特征，构建 token 角色条件探针，进行静默特征消融与路由特征修补等因果干预及恢复实验；覆盖六个有害内容二分类基准，并扩展到西班牙语与印地语-英语混合数据；进一步尝试仅校准的路由方案与探针蒸馏 LoRA 微调以恢复原生预测能力。

Result: 稀疏读出在全部六个二分类任务上超过原生预测（Qwen 平均 0.740 vs 0.432 macro-F1，Gemma 从 0.532 提升至 0.714），说明差距反映的是"监督可及性"而非模型已有的原生决策规则；最有效的 token 角色因任务而异。静默特征消融对探针高度敏感（Qwen 24–63 倍），路由特征修补对输出高度敏感（字面 yes/no 任务 16–140 倍）。仅做校准的路由即可恢复平均 93.3% 的差距，探针蒸馏 LoRA 改善原生预测，但多任务共享适配出现负迁移。案例研究中 Gemma-3-12B 在 Facebook Hateful Memes 上的 rank-32 图像-提示交互达 0.756 vs 0.685。信号跨语言稳健、不仅由 OCR 解释，且依赖视觉配对证据。

Conclusion: 在有害 meme 分类中，"路由"而非"表征"本身是反复出现的瓶颈；内部特征可解码但未被有效利用。通过校准读出与特征蒸馏（LoRA）可在一定程度上恢复性能，但需注意多任务适配的负迁移风险。这为提升 VLM 安全检测提供了可解释性驱动的改进路径。

Classification reason: 论文以有害 meme 检测（多模态内容安全任务）为对象，用 SAE/探针/因果干预分析 VLM 失败原因，属于多模态安全方向；与连续语言/潜空间流、世界模型、多模态生成等方向无关，故映射到 multimodal_understanding_generation 下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: A solid mechanistic-interpretability finding (readout gap) for VLM safety, but it targets harmful meme detection rather than the user's core interests in continuous/multimodal generation, world models, or unified generative models. Signals: sparse autoencoder probing, readout gap vs representation bottleneck, harmful meme detection, MLLM interpretability, probe-distilled LoRA

Abstract: When a large vision-language model misclassifies a harmful meme, the failure may reflect missing internal evidence or an inability to route represented evidence to its output. We distinguish these cases in Gemma-3 and Qwen3.5 using sparse autoencoders, role-conditioned probes, causal interventions, and recovery experiments across six harmful content benchmarks, with additional Spanish and Hindi-English code-mixed evaluations. Sparse readouts outperform native prediction on all six primary binary tasks: Qwen averages $0.740$ versus $0.432$ native macro-F1, while residual reconstruction reaches $0.486$, whereas Gemma improves from $0.532$ to $0.714$. These differences reflect supervised accessibility rather than a pre-existing, native decision rule, and the most influential token role depends on the task. Under the evaluated score scales, Qwen silent-feature ablation is $24-63$ times more probe-sensitive, whereas routed-feature patching on literal yes/no tasks is $16-140$ times more output-sensitive. Calibration-only routing recovers $93.3$% of the mean gap, and probe-distilled LoRA improves native predictions, although shared multi-task adaptation causes negative transfer. A case study of Gemma-3-12B on Facebook Hateful Memes finds a distributed rank-32 image-prompt interaction, reaching $0.756$ versus $0.685$ native macro-F1. Robustness controls show that the signal extends beyond English, is not explained solely by accompanying OCR, and depends on paired visual evidence. Thus, routing, rather than representation alone, is a recurring bottleneck in harmful meme classification.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_hallucination'></div>

## 多模态幻觉与可靠性 [[Back]](#toc)

视觉幻觉缓解、事实性、可靠性、忠实性提升。

### [72] [AgenTeeth: A Model-Agnostic Framework for Suppressing Hallucination in Frozen Vision-Language Models on Dental X-Rays via Tool Evidence Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17800)
*Ahmed Rafid, Fariya Ahmed, Rumman Adib, Mehedi Ahamed, Ajwad Abrar, Tareque Mohmud Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (52.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AgenTeeth 提出一种模型无关的工具增强框架，利用七个牙科视觉专家模型为冻结 VLM 注入结构化证据（FDI 牙位与解剖区域 + 标注叠加图），以抑制其在全景牙科 X 光片上的幻觉；在 MMOral-OPG-Bench 上为四个骨干 VLM 提升 12.9–23.0 个百分点，最强配置开放式 VQA 达 65.66%，且冻结 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 配合该框架可超越经过 SFT+RL 工具训练的 OralGPT-Plus。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在全景牙科放射影像上可靠性差，倾向依赖训练中学到的解剖先验而非图像中的真实证据，这在牙齿定位与空间推理任务中尤为严重；即使针对牙科微调的 VLM 也会保留同样的空间偏置，因此需要一种无需微调、能将模型回答"接地"到图像证据的方法。

Method: 提出模型无关的工具增强框架 AgenTeeth：由问题感知的编排器（orchestrator）从七个专门化牙科视觉专家模型中选择相关工具，将专家检测结果映射为 FDI 牙位编号或解剖区域，以结构化发现连同标注图像叠加的形式注入证据；随后通过一次全新的合成调用（synthesis call）基于该证据回答问题，整个过程不微调底层冻结 VLM。

Result: 在 MMOral-OPG-Bench 上，AgenTeeth 使四个骨干 VLM 相对基线提升 12.9–23.0 个百分点；最强配置在开放式 VQA 上达到 65.66%，显著高于 OralGPT-Plus 的 45.35%；在同等规模下，冻结的 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 配合 AgenTeeth 达到 48.11%，超过基于同骨干经监督微调与强化学习训练的 OralGPT-Plus。作者同时发布框架、七个专家模型及牙医标注的牙槽骨吸收检测数据集。

Conclusion: 无需微调底层 VLM，通过专家工具证据注入即可有效抑制冻结模型在专业医学影像上的幻觉并增强空间定位能力；工具增强的"证据接地"是提升 VLM 可靠性的有效且模型无关的路径，且其性能可超过经过 SFT 与 RL 工具使用训练的同骨干模型。

Classification reason: 论文核心是通过专家工具证据注入抑制冻结 VLM 在牙科全景 X 光片上的幻觉、提升事实性与空间可靠性，与 [CANONICAL] multimodal_hallucination（多模态幻觉与可靠性）高度匹配；不涉及连续语言/多模态建模、生成或世界模型，仅归属多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: A domain-specific dental X-ray application of tool-augmented hallucination suppression in frozen VLMs, with solid but incremental technique value that sits far from the user's core interests in continuous language models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: tool-augmented evidence injection into frozen VLMs, hallucination suppression, model-agnostic framework, dental X-ray / clinical domain triggering negative-keyword overlap, no overlap with continuous LM, autoregressive generation, diffusion video, or world-model priorities

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) remain largely unreliable on panoramic dental radiographs and can rely on learned anatomical priors rather than evidence in the image. This is particularly problematic for tooth localization and spatial reasoning, and fine-tuned dental VLMs can retain the same spatial biases. We present AgenTeeth, a model-agnostic, tool-augmented framework that grounds frozen VLMs using seven specialized dental vision experts. A question-aware orchestrator selects the relevant tools, whose detections are mapped to FDI tooth numbers or anatomical regions and returned as structured findings together with annotated image overlays. A fresh synthesis call then answers the question using this evidence, without fine-tuning the underlying VLM. On MMOral-OPG-Bench, AgenTeeth improves four backbone VLMs by 12.9-23.0 percentage points over their baselines. Our strongest configuration reaches 65.66% on open-ended VQA, compared with 45.35% for OralGPT-Plus. The advantage also holds at matched scale: a frozen Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct with AgenTeeth reaches 48.11%, exceeding OralGPT-Plus built on the same backbone after supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning for tool use. We release the framework, all seven expert models, and a dentist-annotated dataset for alveolar bone-loss detection in panoramic radiographs.

</details>


### [73] [Can VLMs Reliably Assess Sidewalk Accessibility Attributes from Pedestrian-Level Imagery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17882)
*Seung Jae Lieu, Diego Morra, Chiara Cadoni, Wonseop Song, Martina Mazzarello, Carlo Ratti*

Main category: cs.CY, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (76.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 该文测试四个 VLM 能否从行人视角图像可靠评估人行道无障碍属性（有效宽度、纵坡、横坡、路面状况），首次将基于采样的 conformal prediction 用于 VLM 属性评估：校准后覆盖率达标但不同属性信息量差异大（宽度最佳约 1.0 m 半宽，路面状况退化为全等级）；未校准的采样离散区间覆盖率仅 17–47%，且自洽性最高时反而最多 96% 缺失实测值，证明"自洽≠准确"。结论：当前 VLM 达不到合规评估所需精度，但 CP 可仅凭校准数据识别可用的粗筛属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市无障碍性（尤其针对轮椅使用者和行动不便者）要求人行道符合可测量的规范要求。作者想探究 VLM 能否从行人视角图像可靠地评估有效宽度、纵坡、横坡与路面状况等物理属性，并为安全攸关的合规筛查提供统计上有保障的不确定性量化，而不是仅凭模型输出直接下结论。

Method: 在 514 张韩国首尔人行道行人视角图像（含实地测量的地面真值）上评测四个 VLM，首次将基于采样的 conformal prediction（CP）应用于 VLM 无障碍属性评估：通过校准集构建名义 90% 覆盖率的预测区间，比较不同模型与属性的区间信息量（宽度），并引入非对称校准以利用模型的系统性高估偏差。

Result: CP 校准后所有模型与属性均达到名义 90% 覆盖率，但信息量差异显著：有效宽度最有信息量（最佳模型平均区间半宽约 1.0 m）；所有模型均系统性高估宽度，非对称校准可在覆盖率不变下将区间缩短最多 33%；纵坡仅边缘可用，横坡区间过宽无法分辨法规阈值，路面状况区间在四个模型中的三个退化为全部 A–E 五级。未校准的原始采样离散区间仅覆盖 17–47% 的实测值，在自洽性最高的图像上最多 96% 缺失实测值。没有任何属性达到通用合规评估所需精度，但 CP 可仅凭校准数据判断哪些属性适合作为远离阈值的粗筛。

Conclusion: VLM 的自洽性不能作为准确性的证据，采样离散度未经校准前不能解释为不确定性；当前 VLM 尚无法以合规评估所需精度从行人视角图像测量人行道属性，但 CP 校准提供了一种仅凭校准数据即可判断哪些属性适合远离阈值筛查的原则性方法。作者公开发布了带实测标注的行人视角图像数据集。

Classification reason: 论文核心问题是 VLM 对真实城市人行道图像的定量属性评估是否可靠：关键结论包括"自洽性不等于准确性""未校准的采样离散度不能当作不确定性"，并以 conformal prediction 进行校准与可靠性量化，与"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（事实性、可靠性、忠实性）子主题高度契合；虽也涉及 VLM 评测与数据发布，但可靠性与校准是论文主线，故复用该 CANONICAL 子主题。

Importance reason: A solid applied evaluation introducing sampling-based conformal prediction for VLM reliability, but its urban-accessibility application and understanding-only scope sit far from the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, generation, and world models. Signals: first conformal prediction for VLM attribute assessment, shows self-consistency ≠ accuracy (up to 96% miss), released field-measured sidewalk dataset, VLM/MLLM reliability angle only weakly touches prioritized multimodal understanding, no overlap with continuous latent models, video/image generation, or world-model subtopics

Abstract: An important component of urban accessibility, particularly for wheelchair users and people with reduced mobility, is sidewalk compliance with measurable requirements. We test whether effective width, longitudinal slope, cross slope, and pavement condition can be assessed reliably from pedestrian-level imagery using vision-language models (VLMs). We present the first application of sampling-based conformal prediction (CP) for VLM-based accessibility assessment. We evaluate four VLMs on 514 sidewalk images from Seoul, South Korea, with field-measured ground truth. Conformal calibration attains the nominal 90% coverage for all models and attributes, but the calibrated regions differ in informativeness. Effective width yields the most informative estimates, with a mean interval half-width of about 1.0 m for the best model. Since every model overestimates width, asymmetric calibration shortens the intervals by up to 33% at unchanged coverage. Longitudinal slope is marginally informative, cross-slope intervals are too wide to resolve regulatory thresholds, and pavement-condition sets degenerate to all five grades (A-E) for three of the four models. Uncalibrated intervals from raw sampling dispersion cover only 17-47% of field-measured values at a nominal 90% level. Among the images with the most self-consistent responses, these intervals miss the field-measured value in up to 96% of cases. Response self-consistency is therefore not evidence of accuracy, and sampling dispersion cannot be interpreted as uncertainty until it has been calibrated against field-measured ground truth. No quantitative attribute reaches the precision required for general compliance assessment, but CP identifies from calibration data alone which attributes can support screening of segments far from the thresholds. We release the annotated pedestrian-level images and their corresponding field-measured attribute values.

</details>


### [74] [Transcribe, Then Reason: Two-Pass Decomposition for Multimodal Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18958)
*Bojie Li, Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (58.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 多模态模型单遍审阅长录音/长文档会"偷工减料"：丢失约三分之一内容并润饰虚构。这不是感知或模态问题，也不是加大推理预算能解决的，而是感知、推理与写作竞争同一输出预算所致。先转写再审阅的两遍分解能显著提升忠实性与覆盖率，但收益与单遍基线强弱负相关，且存在超长来源空间不足与失去接地后凭记忆虚构两种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让多模态模型一次性审阅长录音或长文档是自然做法，但作者发现这种单遍调用会悄然失败：模型"知足而止"（satisfice），丢弃约三分之一内容并对其余内容加以润饰虚构。论文旨在定位该失败的根源——究竟是感知、模态、推理预算不足，还是感知、推理与写作在单次输出中相互竞争（负载下的生成瓶颈）。

Method: 通过控制实验逐一排除备选解释：用同一来源的文本与图像对照排除模态因素；给单遍模型更大推理预算发现仍无法找回丢失内容，因为预算被用于规划审阅而非誊写来源。据此提出 transcribe-then-review 的两遍同权重任务分解：第一遍完整转写来源，第二遍对转写稿进行审阅，使每一步独占完整的输出预算；并在 21 个来源的评测套件上衡量忠实性与覆盖率，分析来源长度与模态对收益的影响。

Result: 两遍转写-审阅分解在 21 来源套件上同时提升忠实性与覆盖率；但收益不均匀：单遍基线越弱的场景收益越大，基线已强的场景收益有限，且该模式与来源长度和模态相关。同时揭示两个失败模式：超长来源时审阅遍的输出空间不足；移除接地来源后模型会凭记忆虚构（confabulation）。

Conclusion: 多模态长内容审阅失败的根源是"负载下的生成"而非感知能力；通过转写-审阅两遍分解让感知与审阅各自获得完整输出预算，可显著提升输出的忠实性与覆盖率，但需警惕超长来源的容量限制与接地缺失诱发的凭记忆虚构。

Classification reason: 论文核心是多模态模型长内容审阅中的遗漏与"润饰虚构"（不忠实）现象及其缓解：单遍审阅丢失约三分之一内容并 embellish，两遍转写-审阅分解提升忠实性与覆盖率，并分析移除接地来源后的 confabulation 失败模式。这正对应"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（忠实性、可靠性提升）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题；虽涉及推理时计算扩展与任务分解，但主线是可靠性问题，故复用该子主题。

Importance reason: A solid empirical study showing MLLMs satisfice on long-source review due to generation-under-load rather than perception, but it sits in multimodal reliability rather than the user's core continuous-model, generation, or world-model directions. Signals: multimodal hallucination/reliability finding, generation-under-load bottleneck, not perception or reasoning budget, transcribe-then-review two-pass decomposition, two failure modes on long sources, limited overlap with continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models priorities

Abstract: The natural way to review a long recording or document with a multimodal model is to hand it the raw source and ask for a review in one call. We show that this quietly fails: the model satisfices, dropping roughly a third of the content and embellishing the rest. The failure is not perception--almost all of the dropped content reappears when the same model is simply asked to transcribe the source. The bottleneck is generation under load: a single pass cannot perceive, reason over, and write a long faithful review at the same time, because doing all three competes for one output. We rule out the obvious alternatives. It is not the modality: models read text and an image of the same text equally well. And it is not merely a matter of thinking harder: giving the single pass a far larger reasoning budget does not recover the lost content, because the model spends that budget planning a review rather than writing the source down. What works is to split the labor across two same-weights passes--first transcribe, then review the transcript--so each step gets a full output budget of its own. This transcribe-then-review decomposition improves both faithfulness and coverage across a 21-source suite. The benefit is not uniform: we observe that it helps most where the one-pass baseline is weakest and little where that baseline is already strong, a pattern that also tracks the source's length and modality. Decomposition comes with two failure modes--the review pass running out of room on very long sources, and confabulating from memory once the grounding source is removed.

</details>


### [75] [EDCT-Bench: Uncovering Faithfulness Gaps in VLMs via Explanation-Driven Counterfactual Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17953)
*Sihao Ding, Santosh Vasa, Aditi Ramadwar, Thomas Monninger*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出解释驱动的反事实测试协议 EDCT 与配套基准 EDCT-Bench：抽取模型解释中引用的视觉概念并施加经验证的最小编辑，检验 VLM 的答案与解释是否仍与编辑后图像一致。跨 OK-VQA、DriveLM、3DSRBench 三域评测显示主流 VLM 存在显著忠实性差距，且 EDCT 反事实数据可作为高效微调信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 生成的自然语言解释（NLE）往往听起来合理，却可能与解释中所引用的视觉证据不一致，即解释的忠实性（faithfulness）存疑；现有评测多为相关性判断，缺乏基于干预的可验证手段来度量解释是否真正依据视觉证据。

Method: 提出 EDCT 干预式测试协议：从模型自然语言解释中抽取其引用的视觉概念，对图像施加经过验证的最小反事实编辑，再检验模型的答案与解释是否与编辑后图像保持一致；据此构建覆盖知识密集型 VQA（OK-VQA）、安全关键驾驶（DriveLM）、3D 空间推理（3DSRBench）三个领域的 EDCT-Bench，并进行多模型评测与微调实验。

Result: 在所评测的多个 VLM 上，EDCT 揭示了显著的忠实性差距，模型响应经常与经过验证的视觉变化不一致；此外微调实验表明，EDCT 生成的反事实样本能提供高价值的训练信号用于改进模型。

Conclusion: EDCT 为衡量 VLM 解释忠实性提供了可验证的干预式评测范式，揭示了当前模型"解释合理但证据不符"的系统性缺陷，并表明反事实驱动的训练信号可有效提升模型对视觉证据的忠实性。

Classification reason: 论文核心是通过反事实干预评测 VLM 解释的忠实性（faithfulness）与事实性，并揭示视觉证据与文本解释不一致的可靠性问题，正对应 canonical 子主题"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（关键词含 faithfulness/事实性）；虽形式上是基准，但主题聚焦忠实性而非通用评测协议，故归入该子主题。与连续语言模型、世界模型、多模态生成方向无关，唯一匹配方向为多模态理解与生成。

Importance reason: A well-designed counterfactual faithfulness benchmark for VLM explanations, but its reliability-evaluation focus is orthogonal to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video generation, and world models. Signals: VLM faithfulness benchmark, counterfactual intervention protocol, NLE consistency gap across 3 domains, fine-tuning signal from counterfactuals, only weak MLLM/VLM overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can produce Natural Language Explanations (NLEs) that sound plausible yet remain inconsistent with the visual evidence they cite. We present Explanation-Driven Counterfactual Testing (EDCT), an intervention-based protocol that extracts visual concepts cited in a model's explanation, applies verified minimal edits to them, and tests whether the resulting answer and explanation remain consistent with the edited image. Using this protocol, we create EDCT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning three complementary domains: knowledge-intensive visual question answering (OK-VQA), safety-critical driving (DriveLM), and 3D spatial reasoning (3DSRBench). Across the evaluated VLMs, EDCT reveals substantial faithfulness gaps, with models frequently producing responses inconsistent with verified visual changes. Finally, our fine-tuning study suggests that EDCT-generated counterfactuals provide high-impact training signals.

</details>

### [76] [Anchoring What Matters: A Dual-Level Learning Framework for Visually-Grounded Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18057)
*Xinxin Song, Siyuan Li, Tingxiong Xiao, Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (83.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 PIVOT：一种针对 LVLM 视觉锚定多模态推理的双层 RLVR 优化框架，通过自校准经验回放保留有价值的视觉推理轨迹，并用视觉引导的优势分配替代均匀 token 优势，在多个基准上显著增强多模态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 显著提升了大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的推理能力，但标准 on-policy RLVR 存在优化瓶颈：有价值的视觉锚定推理轨迹在单次更新后即被丢弃，且均匀的 token 优势分配无法强化关键的感知与推理步骤，导致模型难以保持和强化视觉锚定的推理行为。

Method: 提出 PIVOT 双层学习框架：(1) 自校准经验回放机制——选择性收集并回放具有视觉锚定的历史推理经验，作为策略优化的稳定参考锚，避免有价值轨迹在单次更新后被丢弃；(2) 视觉引导的优势分配机制——依据 token 的局部视觉支持程度及其对下游推理的影响，为其分配额外的视觉感知优势，从而强化关键感知与推理步骤。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上的大量实验表明，PIVOT 在提升 LVLM 多模态推理能力方面取得了极具竞争力的性能，验证了双层锚定框架对视觉锚定推理行为的强化效果。

Conclusion: 以视觉锚定的经验回放与视觉感知优势分配相结合的双层 RL 框架，能够有效缓解标准 RLVR 中关键视觉推理信号被丢弃与优势分配均匀化的问题，为提升 LVLM 的视觉锚定多模态推理提供了通用有效的优化范式。

Classification reason: 论文核心是针对 LVLM 的 RLVR 强化学习算法改进（经验回放、token 优势分配），目标是增强视觉锚定的多模态推理，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_rl_alignment（多模态 RL 与对齐）最匹配；虽然涉及推理，但方法本质是 RL 优化框架，而非推理链结构设计，故未选 multimodal_reasoning_cot。方向上仅匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation，与连续语言/多模态模型、世界模型、多模态生成均无关。

Importance reason: A solid but incremental RLVR optimization for LVLM visual grounding that touches the user's multimodal understanding direction yet misses their top-priority subtopics (continuous models, video generation, world models). Signals: RLVR for LVLM reasoning, multimodal RL and alignment, vision-guided token advantage allocation, self-calibrated experience replay, no overlap with continuous token or video/world-model priorities

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capabilities of large vision-language models (LVLMs). However, standard on-policy RLVR algorithms face a critical optimization bottleneck in preserving and reinforcing visually grounded reasoning behaviors: valuable visually-grounded reasoning trajectories are discarded after a single update, while uniform token advantage allocation prevents the model from reinforcing critical perception or reasoning steps. To bridge this gap, we propose PIVOT, a dual-level learning framework that anchors policy optimization around informative visual reasoning signals. Specifically, PIVOT introduces a self-calibrated experience replay mechanism, which selectively collects and replays visually-grounded historical experiences as stable reference anchors for policy optimization. Building upon this, we further design a vision-guided advantage allocation mechanism to allocate additional vision-aware advantages to tokens based on their local visual support and impact on downstream reasoning. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that PIVOT achieves highly competitive performance in enhancing the multimodal reasoning capabilities of LVLMs.

</details>


### [77] [Re2A: Situated Conversational Recommendation via Rubric-based Preference Reasoning and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18249)
*Dongding Lin, Jian Wang, Xiaoyan Zhao, Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Re2A 框架，将情境化对话推荐（SCR）建模为"先推理后对齐"过程：先用自动 rubric 引导模型显式推理用户偏好状态，再通过偏好条件化优化使回复生成同时满足用户偏好与场景一致性，在两个 SCR 数据集上超越现有 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实推荐场景常发生在用户与推荐系统共享的物理环境中，需要联合推理对话历史、共观场景与场景内物品属性。现有方法面临两个交织的挑战：难以在对话全程准确理解情境化的用户偏好，也难以生成同时满足用户需求与场景一致性的回复。

Method: 将 SCR 形式化为结构化的 reason-then-align 流程：(1) rubric-based preference reasoning——自动构建 rubric 引导模型产生显式的偏好状态（preference states）；(2) preference-conditioned optimization——以偏好状态为条件，对回复生成进行双目标对齐优化，同时满足用户偏好与情境一致性。

Result: 在两个 SCR 数据集上的大量实验表明，Re2A 持续优于现有 SOTA 方法，给出更精准、更符合上下文与场景的对话推荐。

Conclusion: 显式偏好状态推理与偏好条件化双目标对齐相结合，能够有效提升情境化对话推荐中用户偏好满足度与场景一致性，为共享物理环境下的推荐助手提供了可扩展的结构化建模范式。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是基于 rubric 的偏好推理与偏好条件化对齐优化，将回复生成与用户偏好满足、情境一致性双目标对齐，属于偏好学习与多模态对齐主题；同时任务需要在对话历史与共观场景间进行跨模态推理，故归入多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子题"多模态强化学习与对齐"。与连续语言模型、世界模型及视觉生成方向无关。

Importance reason: This is a conversational recommendation paper sitting squarely in the user's negative-keyword area (recommender systems) with only weak multimodal grounding, so it misses all priority subtopics despite reasonable technical novelty. Signals: recommender system (negative keyword area), rubric-based preference reasoning, preference-conditioned alignment optimization, scene-grounded dialogue, SOTA on two SCR datasets

Abstract: Real-world recommendation scenarios are commonly grounded in shared physical environments during user-recommender interactions. This motivates situated conversational recommendation (SCR), a complex task requiring recommender assistants to jointly reason over dialogue history, co-observed scenes, and in-scene item attributes. However, current approaches struggle with this setting due to two intertwined challenges: accurately understanding situated user preferences throughout the conversation and generating responses that simultaneously satisfy user needs and grounded situations. To this end, we propose Re2A, a framework that formulates SCR as a structured reason-then-align process. We introduce rubric-based preference reasoning, which uses automated rubrics to guide the model toward producing explicit preference states. Based on these states, we propose a preference-conditioned optimization to align response generation with dual objectives: user preference satisfaction and situation consistency. Extensive experiments on two SCR datasets demonstrate that Re2A consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, delivering more precise, context-aware conversational recommendations. Our code is available at .

</details>

### [78] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal, Sree Krishna Uppalapati, Dhruvkumar Patel, Ria Khatoniar, Tanuja Ganu, Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.33) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文提出五任务诊断实验，将多模态 LLM 在物理与几何推理基准上的失败解耦为感知失败与推理失败：图像解读错误会拖累纯文本即可解对的题目，人类撰写的描述能普遍提升准确率，修正描述下部分模型恢复显著；感知失败后的推理错误在物理域多表现为计算错误、几何域多为概念误用；InternS1-mini 虽经科学预训练仍全面垫底且思维链频繁截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 LLM 在科学推理基准上报告的强性能难以定位问题根源：大多数评测把感知与推理当作单一可测过程，无法区分模型是"看错了"还是"想错了"，因而缺乏针对性的改进方向，需要能显式分离两类失败的诊断方法。

Method: 设计五任务诊断实验，跨越物理与几何基准，通过逐步改变输入条件（原始图像 → 人类撰写的文字描述 → 修正后的描述）控制感知变量，将错误归因于感知、推理或两者兼有；比较纯文本作答与含图作答的性能差异，分析修正描述下的性能恢复情况，并按领域统计感知失败之后出现的推理错误类型；另对 InternS1-mini 的推理轨迹进行案例式讨论。

Result: 错误的图表解读会降低模型在纯文本即可答对题目上的准确率；准确率普遍随输入从原始图像过渡到人类撰写的描述而上升；部分模型在修正描述下恢复显著，可将感知受阻型失败与真正的推理瓶颈区分开；物理域的感知失败多表现为计算错误，几何域多表现为概念误用；案例讨论显示 InternS1-mini 尽管有大量科学预训练与思考能力，在所有任务上仍低于实验中最弱模型，且推理轨迹频繁截断。

Conclusion: 多模态科学推理评测必须将感知与推理解耦：基准高分不能等同于强推理能力，感知瓶颈会掩盖真实推理水平。未来需要显式分离感知与推理的诊断协议，并加强模型对图表的可靠感知与思维链完整性。

Classification reason: 论文核心是诊断多模态 LLM 在物理与几何（科学）推理任务中的失败来源，将感知与推理解耦分析，并研究推理错误类型与思维链行为，最贴合 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot（多模态 CoT、视觉推理、科学/数学推理）；其评测协议性质可作为次要视角，但主导贡献是推理失败诊断。论文不涉及连续语言/多模态建模、世界模型或内容生成，故仅匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 一个方向。

Importance reason: A solid diagnostic study decoupling perception vs. reasoning failures in MLLMs, relevant to multimodal understanding but far from the user's core interests in continuous latent models, video generation, and world models. Signals: MLLM diagnostic benchmark, perception vs. reasoning decoupling via five tasks, caption intervention improves accuracy, physics calculation vs. geometry conceptual error patterns, matches only the weak mllm boost signal

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>
