### [79] [Face-voice Association across LAnguages and Gender (FLAG) 2027 Challenge Evaluation Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17913)
*Marta Moscati, Swapnil Khandoker, Muhammad Saad Saeed, Shah Nawaz, Fatima Noor, Rohan Kumar Das, Mubashir Noman, Junaid Mir, Muhammad Haroon Yousaf, Khalid Malik, Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (49.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 FLAG 2027 挑战评测计划：将人脸-声音关联形式化为跨模态验证任务（给定语音从人脸库中识别说话人），并在未见身份、已见/未见语言、标准与同性别约束等设置下评测；结果显示现有模型在语言偏移和性别约束下性能显著退化，说明其依赖语言/性别捷径而非身份特异特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸-声音关联模型可能依赖语音中的语言或性别线索而非说话人特有的声音特征，导致在多语言说话人识别或同性别说话人区分时性能退化，缺乏系统性基准来诊断这类"捷径"依赖。

Method: 构建 FLAG 2027 挑战评测计划：将人脸-声音关联形式化为跨模态验证任务（给定语音，从包含目标人脸与负样本的 gallery 中识别说话人）；评测在未见身份、已见/未见语言（heard/unheard）以及标准库与同性别约束库两种设置下进行；提供基准数据集、预训练基线模型与统一评测框架。

Result: 现有基线模型在语言偏移（尤其是训练中未出现语言）与性别约束（同性别 gallery）设置下性能显著下降，表明模型确实依赖语言与性别等捷径特征，而非身份特异的声音表征。

Conclusion: 人脸-声音关联研究应超越语言与性别捷径，学习身份特异的跨模态表征；FLAG 2027 挑战通过跨语言与性别约束评测为此提供了系统性的数据、基线与评测框架。

Classification reason: 论文将"人脸-声音关联"形式化为跨模态验证任务（给定语音，从人脸库中检索/验证说话人），本质是音频-视觉跨模态匹配问题，归入 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_retrieval（跨模态检索）最为贴切；其同时提出新挑战基准，但任务性质以跨模态检索/验证为主，故复用现有子主题而非新建。

Importance reason: An evaluation plan for a niche face-voice cross-modal verification challenge with no overlap with the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: cross-modal face-voice verification, challenge/benchmark evaluation plan, focuses on language and gender shortcut biases, audio-visual speaker matching niche, no continuous/generative/world-model relevance

Abstract: Face--voice association models may rely on language or gender cues in the voice rather than on speaker-specific voice characteristics, which can lead to a performance deterioration when the model has to identify a multilingual speaker or distinguis same-gender speakers. To investigate these issues, we introduce the Face-voice Association across LAnguages and Gender (FLAG) 2027 Challenge. The challenge formulates face--voice association as a cross-modal verification task: given a voice, identify the speaker's face from a ``gallery'' of faces consisting of the speaker's face and a set of negative samples. Models are evaluated on identities not present in the training data (``unseen'') and both for languages present or absent from the training data (``heard'' and ``unheard''). Two evaluation settings are used to test models' reliance on gender: a standard, unconstrained and a gender-constrained one, where the latter uses a same-gender gallery. The performance of existing, baseline models in these settings reveals that models performance degrades under language shifts and in gender-constrained settings, highlighting the need to foster the development of models that capture identity-specific aspects beyond language and gender. The challenge provides a benchmark dataset, pretrained baseline models, and an evaluation framework to advance face--voice association.

</details>


### [80] [QMSR: Query-Conditioned Mask-wise Expert Routing for Robust Open-Vocabulary Underwater Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18752)
*Fuming Zhang, Dongyue Huang, Junjie Wen, Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (45.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 QMSR，一种查询条件化的掩码级专家路由框架：为每个查询-候选对自适应选择一个预训练水下图像增强专家并预测连续 Raw–Expert 融合强度，配合特权排序 oracle 与退火软路由训练，使水下开放词汇检索 NDCG@10 较共享路由器提升 17.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂水下环境中的开放词汇物体检索仍然困难。虽然水下图像增强（UIE）可以提升视觉质量，但固定的 UIE 策略在检索中甚至可能不如 Raw 表示，说明增强不应作为统一的预处理步骤，而应根据查询与候选自适应地施加。

Method: 提出 QMSR：为每个查询-候选对从多个预训练水下图像增强（UIE）专家中选择一个，并以 mask 级路由预测连续的 Raw–Expert 融合强度，实现自适应增强同时保留 Raw 图像有用语义；训练时引入特权排序 oracle 为专家选择与融合强度提供监督，并通过退火软路由松弛来优化硬 Top-1 路由策略。

Result: QMSR 的 NDCG@10 相比图像-查询共享路由器提升 17.6%，持续优于各类固定 UIE 增强策略，并在 held-out 查询类别上保持有效。

Conclusion: 查询条件化与候选特定的增强路由是水下开放词汇检索的有效方案：增强应按需自适应施加而非统一预处理，特权 oracle 监督与退火软路由可稳定优化离散选择问题，该方法对未见查询类别亦具泛化性。

Classification reason: 论文核心任务是开放词汇的水下跨模态物体检索（文本 query 与图像候选的排序检索），属于 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下 canonical 子话题「多模态检索」的范畴；虽发布于 cs.RO 且面向水下场景，但不涉及世界模型、生成建模或连续语言/潜空间建模，故仅匹配该方向并复用既有子话题。

Importance reason: A niche cs.RO application paper on underwater open-vocabulary retrieval with expert routing, showing no overlap with the user's priority subtopics in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: query-conditioned expert routing, open-vocabulary retrieval, underwater image enhancement, domain-specific application, no match to priority subtopics or boost keywords

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object retrieval remains challenging in complex underwater environments. Although underwater image enhancement (UIE) can improve visual quality, fixed UIE strategies may even underperform the Raw representation in retrieval, indicating that enhancement should not be applied as a uniform preprocessing step. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{QMSR}, a query-conditioned mask-wise expert routing framework for underwater open-vocabulary retrieval. Specifically, QMSR selects one pretrained UIE expert for each query--candidate pair and predicts a continuous Raw--Expert fusion strength, enabling adaptive enhancement while preserving useful Raw semantics. During training, a privileged ranking oracle provides expert-selection and fusion-strength supervision, while an annealed soft-routing relaxation facilitates optimization of the hard Top-1 routing policy. Experiments show that QMSR improves NDCG@10 by 17.6\% over an image--query shared router, while consistently outperforming fixed UIE strategies and remaining effective on held-out query categories. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of query-conditioned and candidate-specific enhancement routing for underwater open-vocabulary retrieval.

</details>


### [81] [VoiceTrace: A Benchmark and Retrieval Framework for Who-Said-What Speech Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18521)
*Aaron Yee, Fengjie Lu, Jiarui Hai, Chenang Jiang, Helin Wang, Siwei Tu, Weitao You, Lingyun Sun*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.AI, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (54.59) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 VoiceTrace-Bench，首个联合"说了什么（文本）+谁说的（参考语音）"的混合语音检索基准，并基于音频-语言模型构建两阶段框架 VoiceTrace（统一嵌入模型 VoiceTrace-Emb + 重排器 VoiceTrace-Reranker），在既有语义语音检索基准上达到 SOTA，并在混合检索设置上大幅超越级联式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着会议、讲座、播客和视频中的语音内容不断增长，语音检索日益重要。现有基准与模型主要关注"说了什么"（what）的语义检索，而忽视"谁说的"（who）；但真实场景中用户往往需要同时依据语义内容与目标说话人进行联合检索，且说话人可通过一段参考语音自然指定，而非预定义身份标签。现有工作缺乏对此混合检索设置的评测与方法。

Method: 首先构建 VoiceTrace-Bench，每个查询由文本（what）与参考语音（who）组成，要求模型从异构查询输入中联合整合语义与说话人信息；基于音频-语言模型（ALM）的联合音频-文本建模能力，提出两阶段检索框架 VoiceTrace：VoiceTrace-Emb 学习统一的音频-文本嵌入表示以支持大规模高效召回，VoiceTrace-Reranker 对每个查询-候选对进行细粒度相关性重排。

Result: VoiceTrace 在已有的语义语音检索基准上取得 SOTA 性能；在新提出的 VoiceTrace-Bench 上显著超越基于级联（cascade）的方法，验证了统一嵌入+重排的两阶段框架在传统语义检索与新混合检索设置下的有效性。

Conclusion: 音频-语言模型驱动的两阶段检索框架能够有效融合语义内容与说话人身份两类异构信息，VoiceTrace-Bench 为混合语音检索设立了新基准，VoiceTrace 为该任务提供了强基线，推动语音检索从纯语义检索走向"内容+说话人"的联合检索。

Classification reason: 论文提出文本+参考语音的混合语音检索基准与检索框架，属于跨模态/组合检索（多模态检索）范畴，与多模态检索方向直接对应，故映射到 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_retrieval；论文不涉及生成、世界模型或连续语言建模，不匹配其他方向。

Importance reason: A solid first-of-its-kind benchmark and embedding+reranking framework for hybrid who-said-what speech retrieval, but it sits in audio understanding far from the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, generation, and world models. Signals: first benchmark for joint content+speaker speech retrieval, audio-language model unified embeddings, two-stage retrieve-then-rerank SOTA, weak overlap with user's priority subtopics, no generation, world-model, or continuous-representation content

Abstract: Speech retrieval has become increasingly important as spoken content continues to grow across meetings, lectures, podcasts, and videos. Existing benchmarks and models have advanced semantic search over spoken content, but largely focus on \emph{what} is said while overlooking \emph{who} says it. In many real-world scenarios, however, users need to retrieve speech based jointly on semantic content and a target speaker, where the speaker may be specified naturally through a reference speech utterance rather than a predefined identity. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{VoiceTrace-Bench}, a benchmark for hybrid speech retrieval in which each query combines text specifying \emph{what} to retrieve with reference speech specifying \emph{who} to retrieve. This setting requires models to integrate complementary semantic and speaker information directly from heterogeneous query inputs. Motivated by the joint audio-text modeling capabilities of audio-language models (ALMs), we develop \textbf{VoiceTrace}, a two-stage retrieval framework consisting of \textbf{VoiceTrace-Emb}, an embedding model that learns unified representations for efficient large-scale retrieval, and \textbf{VoiceTrace-Reranker}, a reranking model that jointly examines each query--candidate pair for fine-grained relevance estimation. Experiments show that VoiceTrace achieves state-of-the-art performance on established semantic speech retrieval benchmarks, while substantially outperforming cascade-based approaches on VoiceTrace-Bench, demonstrating its effectiveness for both conventional semantic retrieval and the new hybrid retrieval setting.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_benchmark'></div>

## 多模态评测与基准 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态评测、新基准、评测协议、自动评估。

### [82] [ERPBench: A State-Grounded Evaluation Paradigm for Computer-Use Agents in Enterprise Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17885)
*Kratika Bhagtani, Kusha Sridhar, Maziyar Baran Pouyan, Yuying Zhao, Eugene Siow*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MA, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 ERPBench：在真实可复现的企业 ERP 系统上评测截图输入的计算机使用智能体，以数据库真值进行状态接地评分；发现通用 GUI 能力难以迁移到企业场景——部分智能体保存成功率高达 85%，但写入正确值的比例低至 3%，并刻画了企业工作流特有的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体发展迅速，但其评测仍集中于通用桌面与网页任务。ERP 系统支撑全球组织的财务、采购、库存与客户运营，对智能体提出独特挑战：界面密集、需要多步协同操作，且错误会写入持久化业务记录而非显性呈现在屏幕上。现有企业基准依赖专有平台或模拟近似软件，缺乏真实系统上的状态级真值评测。

Method: 构建 ERPBench：在真实且可复现的 ERP 系统上评测仅以截图为输入的智能体；每个任务以数据库中的 ground-truth 值进行状态接地打分；同时提供生产级 harness，将智能体操作置于人工审批门控之后以保障安全部署，并支持 ERPBench 自主运行；对 6 个闭源与开源智能体进行系统评测。

Result: 评测表明通用 GUI 能力无法迁移到企业可靠性：即使智能体到达正确表单并保存，存储的记录也常常是错的——部分智能体在高达 85% 的运行中完成保存，但写入正确值的比例最低仅约 3%；论文进一步刻画了企业工作流特有的失败模式。

Conclusion: 企业级软件场景需要状态接地的评测范式：通用 GUI 基准上的高分无法保证智能体在持久化业务系统中的数据正确性，未来计算机使用智能体研究应关注写入正确性的验证、企业工作流失败模式与安全部署机制。

Classification reason: 论文提出 ERPBench，一个面向截图输入的计算机使用（GUI）智能体的新评测基准，在真实 ERP 系统上以数据库真值对智能体的多模态（视觉+操作）行为进行状态接地评分，属于多模态智能体评测范畴，故复用规范子主题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准），主方向为多模态理解与生成；与连续语言模型、世界模型及生成类方向无实质关联。

Importance reason: A solid, reproducible computer-use agent benchmark with striking reliability findings, but it targets enterprise GUI agents and has almost no overlap with the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: computer-use agent benchmark, ERP enterprise GUI agents, screenshot-only multimodal input, database ground-truth scoring (85% save vs 3% correct), no generative modeling

Abstract: Computer-use agents that operate through screenshots and simulated actions are advancing rapidly, yet their evaluation remains anchored to general desktop and web tasks. Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) systems run the finance, procurement, inventory, and customer operations of organizations worldwide, and pose distinct challenges for computer-use agents: dense interfaces, coordinated multi-step interactions, and errors that alter persistent business records rather than surfacing on screen. Existing enterprise benchmarks rely on proprietary platforms or on simulated approximations of such software. We introduce ERPBench, a benchmark that evaluates screenshot-only agents on a live and reproducible ERP system and scores each task against ground-truth values in its database. Beyond the benchmark, we present a production-grade harness that gates agent actions behind human approval for safe deployment, which ERPBench runs autonomously. Evaluating six closed and open-source agents, we demonstrate that strong general GUI performance does not transfer to enterprise reliability. Even when an agent reaches the right form and saves it, the stored record is often wrong: some agents save in up to 85% of runs but write the correct value in as few as 3%. We further characterize failure modes specific to enterprise workflows.

</details>


### [83] [The Mirage of Calibrated Confidence: Trajectory-Independence of Verbalized Confidence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18453)
*Jisoo Yang, Jaeho Han, Trung X. Pham, Junyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (78.75) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文发现VLM的口头置信度与推理轨迹基本无关：即使轨迹有误，模型仍会报告高置信度，且校准训练反而可能加剧这种脱节。作者提出Trajectory-Grounding Score（TGS-self与TGS-pair两种形式）及覆盖10个基准的TGS-Bench评测套件，揭示ECE/AUROC等现有校准指标无法检测的这一评估盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个"校准良好"的VLM可能反复自我纠正、说"等等，我应该再检查一下"，最终得到错误答案却仍报告高置信度。研究者发现这是因为口头化置信度在所评测的VLM和校准方法中基本与推理轨迹无关。而ECE、AUROC等现有校准指标无法检测这种置信度与轨迹脱节的问题，构成评估盲点。

Method: 从三个互补视角分析置信度对轨迹的敏感性：轨迹内容变化、推理token掩码、模型自身犹豫标记。提出Trajectory-Grounding Score的两种形式：TGS-self比较模型在有/无访问自身轨迹时的置信度差异，TGS-pair检验模型是否对正确轨迹沿视觉、推理、答案三个轴赋予高于缺陷轨迹的置信度。并构建TGS-Bench——一个模型无关的评测套件，覆盖10个基准并包含受控的好/坏轨迹对。

Result: 实验表明VLM的置信度对推理轨迹实际内容不够敏感；校准训练反而可能加剧这种脱节（悖论效应）。同时，基于传统校准指标的方法排名与基于轨迹接地的排名出现分歧，TGS-Bench能够检测到传统指标无法发现的问题。

Conclusion: VLM口头置信度与推理轨迹脱节是当前校准实践的盲点；应将轨迹接地性作为校准评估的必要维度，与ECE/AUROC等传统指标互补使用。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是提出评估VLM置信度的新指标（TGS-self/TGS-pair）与跨10个基准的评测套件TGS-Bench，属于VLM评测方法与基准构建，映射到[CANONICAL]子主题"多模态评测与基准"；其对象是VLM的推理轨迹可靠性，与多模态推理子主题部分相关但非主贡献。

Importance reason: A well-constructed analysis exposing trajectory-independent confidence in VLMs with a new metric and benchmark, but it addresses calibration/evaluation rather than the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, generation, and world models. Signals: VLM verbalized confidence analysis, trajectory-independence finding, TGS-self/TGS-pair metric, TGS-Bench spanning 10 benchmarks, shows ECE/AUROC calibration blind spot

Abstract: A calibrated Vision-Language Model (VLM) can repeatedly self-correct, say "Wait, I should recheck," arrive at the wrong answer, and still report high confidence. We find that this occurs because verbalized confidence is largely trajectory-independent in the VLMs and calibration methods we evaluate. We examine this through three complementary lenses: content variation, token masking, and the model's own hesitation markers. We show that confidence is insufficiently sensitive to what the reasoning trajectory actually contains, and that calibration training can paradoxically worsen this disconnect. Since existing metrics like ECE and AUROC cannot detect this problem, we propose the Trajectory-Grounding Score (TGS) in two complementary forms: TGS-self, which compares confidence with and without access to the model's own trajectory, and TGS-pair, which tests whether the model assigns higher confidence to correct trajectories than to flawed ones along the vision, reasoning, and answer axes. We propose TGS-Bench, a model-agnostic suite spanning 10 benchmarks with controlled good/bad trajectory pairs, and show that conventional calibration rankings diverge from trajectory-grounding rankings, exposing a blind spot in current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [84] [Understanding Dynamic Scenes at Gigapixel Scale: Wide-Area Spatio-Temporal Perception from UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18210)
*Yuhang Zhu, Meiyi Zhu, Yunkai Dang, Zhangnan Li, Yuxuan Wang, Wenbin Li, Hongbing Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (79.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文提出吉像素级无人机宽域时空场景理解（WSTU）新任务，发布 12768×9564 超高清航拍数据集 HARD（标注目标检测、多目标跟踪与场景级 VQA 三级标签），并提出延迟感知跟踪评测指标 streaming-HOTA（s-HOTA）；基线实验揭示：端到端流水线对检测影响堪比检测器本身，高延迟主要冲击跟踪关联轴导致离线最优者在线失利，且 VLM 跨帧身份绑定能力弱、单帧增益无法迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机成像从兆像素迈向吉像素，使航空感知目标从识别单个目标转向理解整个动态场景；这要求宽域覆盖、单目标分辨率与时间连续性三者同时满足（即 WSTU），但现有数据集无法兼得；同时超高分辨率下单帧处理延迟达秒级，延迟在评测中不可忽略，亟需新数据与新评测范式。

Method: 构建超高清（12768×9564）机载遥感数据集 HARD，进行三级标注（目标检测、多目标跟踪、场景级 VQA）；提出流式多目标跟踪的延迟感知评测指标 streaming-HOTA（s-HOTA），将延迟显式纳入关联与检测两个评测轴；并在检测、跟踪、VQA 三类任务上开展大规模基线实验与流水线级分析。

Result: 检测任务中，端到端流水线对精度与速度的影响与检测器本身相当；跟踪任务中，高延迟对关联轴的冲击远大于检测轴，离线评测最优的流水线在 s-HOTA 下可能失去领先；VQA 任务中，视觉语言模型在跨帧身份绑定上能力薄弱，单帧性能增益无法迁移。现有基线均远未达到 WSTU 要求。

Conclusion: HARD 数据集与系统性基线为宽域时空场景理解（WSTU）提供了数据与评测基础；延迟感知评测（s-HOTA）应成为超高分辨率跟踪评测的必要标准；未来需发展能兼顾超高分辨率处理效率与跨帧身份绑定能力的多模态模型，方能真正推进 WSTU。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是面向多模态理解任务（目标检测、多目标跟踪、场景级 VQA）的新数据集 HARD 与评测协议（含延迟感知新指标 s-HOTA），属于典型的基准/数据集/评测工作，与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）最契合；虽涉及时序跟踪，但主贡献在基准构建与评测而非视频理解方法，故不选 video_understanding_temporal；论文不涉及世界模型、生成或连续建模，与其他方向无关。

Importance reason: A solid new gigapixel UAV benchmark with latency-aware tracking evaluation, but centered on airborne remote sensing and far from the user's continuous-multimodal and video/world-model priorities. Signals: gigapixel UAV spatio-temporal benchmark, airborne remote-sensing dataset (negative keyword), streaming-HOTA latency-aware tracking metric, VLM weak at cross-frame identity binding, detection/tracking/VQA baselines

Abstract: UAV-borne imaging has advanced from megapixel to gigapixel sensors, shifting aerial perception from recognizing individual targets to understanding entire dynamic scenes. We characterize this demand as Wide-area Spatio-temporal Scene Understanding (WSTU), which requires wide-area coverage, per-target resolution, and temporal continuity at once, a combination existing datasets lack. To fill this gap, we introduce an ultra-High-resolution (12768x9564) Airborne Remote-sensing Dataset (HARD) annotated at three levels for object detection, multi-object tracking, and scene-level visual question answering. Ultra-high-resolution imagery raises per-frame processing time to seconds. At that scale latency can no longer be ignored in evaluation. Thus, we propose a latency-aware metric for multi-object tracking called streaming-HOTA (s-HOTA). Extensive baseline experiments show how ultra-high-resolution processing reshapes each task. For detection, the end-to-end pipeline affects accuracy and speed as much as the detector itself does. For tracking, high latency charges the association axis far more unevenly than the detection axis, and association is where pipelines diverge. As a result, the pipeline that performs best offline can lose its lead under s-HOTA. For VQA, vision-language models remain weak at cross-frame identity binding and cannot transfer their single-frame gains to it. Together these findings show that the baselines we evaluate fall short of WSTU. HARD provides the data and the systematic baselines to advance it.

</details>


### [85] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu, Xun Wei Yee, Wei Li, Mun Thye Mak, Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.02) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MUSE 是一个面向情境化教育应用的大型视觉语言模型艺术图像理解评测基准，包含 12 个任务（视觉感知、语义/情感解读、文化理解、组合推理），通过图像标注与问题生成解耦实现可控难度和低标注成本。评测显示现有开源与闭源模型在情感解读和组合推理上存在显著短板，MUSE 有望成为教育场景多模态理解的标准化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在多模态理解上进展显著，但其在教育情境（尤其 AI 辅助语言学习）中的能力缺乏充分评测。模型需要解读艺术图像的语义、情感与文化内涵，并进行视觉情境推理；而现有基准多聚焦真实世界图像或领域特定教育推理，对艺术类教育内容覆盖有限。

Method: 提出 MUSE 基准，将图像标注与问题生成解耦，支持可控难度与多样化任务并降低标注成本；包含 12 个任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解和组合推理；图像精选自新加坡与东南亚多元文化语境及西方艺术传统，涵盖多主题、多难度层级。

Result: 对开源与闭源模型的评测显示各能力维度存在显著差距，情感解读与组合推理尤为薄弱；分析还揭示了常见失败模式，为开发面向教育的可信多模态模型指出了关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE 可作为情境化教育场景中多模态理解的标准化评测基准，推动面向教育的可信多模态模型发展；作者希望后续研究借此系统性提升模型在情感、文化与组合推理等维度的能力。

Classification reason: 该论文是面向视觉语言模型的多模态理解评测基准（MUSE），不涉及连续语言/潜空间建模、世界模型或生成方法，因此仅匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）与之最契合——论文核心是构建新基准、评测协议与失败模式分析，而非提出新 VLM 架构，故直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: A solid but domain-specific VLM benchmark for education/artistic imagery with no overlap with the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Signals: VLM evaluation benchmark, education-situated artistic image understanding, 12 tasks incl. affective and compositional reasoning, annotation-question decoupling method, Southeast Asian cultural focus

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [86] [HearInContext: A Benchmark for Implicit Context in Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18680)
*Yifan Gao, Yao Tian, Hongbin Suo*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (53.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 HearInContext：一个中英双语上下文语音识别基准，用同音词构建 3,764 个语义测试用例，区分隐式/显式上下文提示并设置无上下文与无关上下文对照。实验显示上下文能力模型能利用隐式语义线索，但显式提示带来更高目标召回；微调 Qwen3-ASR-1.7B 使隐式上下文目标召回在中文/英文分别提升 11.0/11.5 个百分点，且 AISHELL-1/LibriSpeech 上 CER/WER 变化小于 0.1 个百分点，增益还能迁移到显式条件与真实录音热词识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文 ASR 评测多依赖上下文中显式出现的目标词（hotword 形式），难以衡量模型从对话历史隐式语义线索中消解同音词歧义、推断正确词形的能力；缺乏能区分隐式线索、显式提示与无关历史各自贡献的受控基准。

Method: 构建共享同一合成语音、配对不同助手回复（对应不同同音词解释）的中英双语基准；围绕同音词构造 3,764 个语义测试用例，其中隐式上下文不含候选词、显式上下文点名目标词，并设置无上下文与无关上下文对照；对上下文能力模型进行评测，并微调 Qwen3-ASR-1.7B，以目标词召回率与 CER/WER 为指标衡量增益与通用性能保持。

Result: 具备上下文能力的模型可从隐式语义线索获益，但显式提示带来更高的目标词召回；微调后隐式上下文目标词召回在中文、英文分别提升 11.0 与 11.5 个百分点，而 AISHELL-1 与 LibriSpeech 上绝对 CER/WER 变化小于 0.1 个百分点；增益还扩展到未参与微调的显式条件以及真实录音上的中文热词识别。

Conclusion: HearInContext 提供了衡量上下文感知语音识别模型利用隐式与显式对话历史能力的受控基准；隐式上下文线索可通过微调被有效利用而不损害通用识别性能，为语音-语言跨模态理解模型的训练与评测提供了可复用的评测框架。

Classification reason: 论文提出 HearInContext——一个中英双语上下文语音识别评测基准，属于音频-文本跨模态理解的新基准与评测协议构建工作。虽非视觉语言方向，但语音理解是多模态理解的组成部分，且其受控对照设计与评测指标符合多模态理解与生成方向下 [CANONICAL] 子话题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）的范围，故复用该子话题；与连续语言模型、世界模型、多模态生成方向无实质关联。

Importance reason: A solid but niche contextual ASR benchmark that touches multimodal understanding only via audio, with no connection to the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models, world models, or visual generation. Signals: bilingual contextual ASR benchmark, homophone-based implicit vs explicit context cases, fine-tuned Qwen3-ASR-1.7B gains +11 points target recall, speech/audio domain, not visual multimodal, no match to continuous latent, world model, or video/image generation priorities

Abstract: Contextual ASR can benefit from semantic cues or from target words explicitly provided in the context. We introduce HearInContext, a Mandarin--English benchmark that pairs shared synthetic speech with assistant replies supporting different interpretations. The benchmark comprises 3,764 semantic test cases built around homophones. Implicit contexts exclude candidate words; explicit contexts name the target. No-context and unrelated-context controls measure the benefit of relevant history and sensitivity to irrelevant history. Context-capable models benefit from implicit cues but achieve higher target recall with explicit hints. Fine-tuning Qwen3-ASR-1.7B improves implicit-context target recall by 11.0 and 11.5 percentage points in Mandarin and English, respectively, while absolute CER/WER changes on AISHELL-1 and LibriSpeech remain below 0.1 percentage points. Gains extend to explicit conditions excluded from fine-tuning and to Mandarin hotword recognition on real recordings.

</details>


### [87] [ReFigBench: Benchmarking Scientific Figure Reconstruction as Editable PowerPoint Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18844)
*Liyang Fan, Chi Wei, Yitai Li, Xinping Bi, Guhong Chen, Chenghao Sun, Haoxiang Yang, Qingwen Li, Kai Yan, Hong Li, Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 ReFigBench：一个评测多模态编码 Agent 将 arXiv 论文概览图重建为可编辑 PPTX 产物的基准与评测框架，含 1000 张真实图表、直接生成与专用工作流两种模式及双 harness 共十种配置；发现感知仍是瓶颈、工作流收益取决于模型与 harness 的组合，并揭示保真度与可编辑性之间的核心张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态编码 Agent 需要将视觉输入转化为可用的产物，其行为依赖围绕模型的 harness（工具、上下文管理与执行环境）。现有评测多用短工具调用、API 轨迹或截图相似度作为代理指标，低分无法区分是模型感知差、规划差还是 harness 失效，缺乏可归因的评测手段。

Method: 构建 ReFigBench 基准与评测框架：从 arXiv 论文中检索 1000 张真实概览图（含完整溯源），要求 Agent 将源图重建为保留文本、拓扑、布局与原生文档结构的可编辑 PPTX 幻灯片；四种模型家族的编码 Agent 在"直接代码生成"与"专用 PPTX 工作流"两种工作流下重建所有图表，最强模型再置于两个商业 harness 中，共形成十种配置；评测结合确定性产物检查、来自两个模型家族裁判的重复自动评分以及盲测人工对比。

Result: 感知仍是主要瓶颈，迭代渲染只能部分弥补；工作流投入能否转化为质量取决于模型与 harness 的组合——同一模型在一种 harness 中从专用工作流获益、在另一种中反而受损，且相同直接提示下 harness 本身就会改变分数；专用工作流在所有配置中都会抹除原生连接器，但人工评委在多数对决中仍偏好其渲染结果；即使最强 Agent 也未达到评分细则的上限。

Conclusion: 保真度与可编辑性之间的张力是实用多模态文档 Agent 的核心挑战；ReFigBench 为多模态 Agent 及其 harness 的协同评测提供了具备完整溯源、可归因的基准与评测框架。

Classification reason: 论文核心是提出多模态编码 Agent 的评测基准与评测协议（ReFigBench），评估从图像输入到可编辑产物的重建质量，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的评测与基准主题，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark；与连续语言模型、世界模型及生成方法本身无关，故仅匹配该方向。

Importance reason: A well-constructed benchmark for figure-to-PPTX multimodal agents, but it sits far from the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: multimodal benchmark with 1000 arXiv figures, agent harness evaluation, perception bottleneck finding, fidelity-editability tension, no overlap with continuous token, video, or world model priorities

Abstract: Multimodal coding agents are expected to turn visual inputs into usable artifacts, and they act through a harness, the layer of tools, context management, and execution environment around the model. Existing evaluations often isolate short tool calls, API traces, or screenshot resemblance, and a low score under these proxies cannot say whether the model saw poorly, planned poorly, or was failed by its harness. We study scientific overview figure reconstruction, an agent task in which a source image must become an editable PowerPoint slide that preserves text, topology, layout, and native document structure. We introduce ReFigBench, a benchmark and evaluation framework built on 1,000 real overview figures retrieved from arXiv papers with full provenance. Coding agents from four model families reconstruct every figure under two workflows, direct code generation and a specialized PPTX workflow, and the strongest model runs inside two commercial harnesses, yielding ten configurations. Evaluation combines deterministic artifact checks, repeated automated scoring by judges from two model families, and blinded human comparisons. Perception remains a bottleneck that iterative rendering only partly repays. Whether workflow effort converts into quality depends on the model together with its harness, since the same model gains from the specialized workflow inside one harness and loses inside the other, and the harness shifts scores even under an identical direct prompt. The specialized workflow erases native connectors in every configuration, human judges still prefer its renderings in most matchups, and even the strongest agent falls short of the rubric ceiling. These results expose the tension between fidelity and editability as the central challenge for practical multimodal document agents.

</details>


### [88] [MIRAGE: How Conversation State Shapes Historical Evidence Use in Multimodal Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19059)
*Yu Liu, Wenxiao Zhang, Cheng Hu, Cong Cao, Fangfang Yuan, Xinyu Wang, Jin B. Hong, Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (71.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 MIRAGE 评测基准，在固定证据与问题的条件下仅改变对话状态（上下文压缩前后），系统评测多模态个人代理的历史证据使用能力；发现压缩前后呈非单调的不同失效模式，开源模型过度依赖上下文连续性、缺乏主动工具检索，且检索压力仅在深度压缩前有效、压缩后反而退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）代理日益被用作长时运行任务的个人助手，其效用取决于跨对话、文件与工作区状态的证据连续检索与使用。然而代理在历史信息访问退化时仍可能生成看似合理的答案，导致仅以结果正确性（outcome-only）的评测会高估其真实的证据使用能力，因此需要在受控的状态变化下专门评测历史证据使用。

Method: 构建 MIRAGE（Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation）受控评测：固定证据对象、问题与评分标准，仅系统改变对话状态（压缩前深度、压缩后延续），评测代理判断问题可答性、追溯正确证据来源并从来源作答的能力；在 7 个前沿与开源多模态骨干上进行对比，并施加检索压力等干预分析。

Result: 跨 7 个多模态骨干的主要发现：1) 压缩前深度与压缩后延续构成两个不同的非单调失效区间，而非单一退化曲线；2) 开源模型严重依赖上下文连续性，当来源失效时不愿自发切换到工具介导的检索；3) 检索压力在深度压缩前状态中提升来源归因（仅对遵循工具调用的模型有效），但在压缩后一致退化，因为此时存储证据已经劣化。

Conclusion: 历史证据使用能力应在对话状态变化条件下评测，而非仅凭最终答案正确性推断；未来多模态代理需加强来源归因、可答性判断以及在上下文退化时主动切换到工具式检索的能力。

Classification reason: 该论文是对多模态个人代理在对话状态变化下历史证据使用能力的受控评测研究，核心贡献是一个新评测基准（MIRAGE）与评测协议，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark [CANONICAL]）范畴；与连续语言模型、世界模型、多模态生成等方向无关，故归入 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: A well-designed controlled benchmark on MLLM agents' historical evidence use under context compaction, but it centers on conversational state and retrieval rather than the user's core priorities of continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: MLLM agent evaluation benchmark, context compaction and conversation-state analysis, tool-mediated retrieval failure modes, seven frontier/open backbones tested, no overlap with video/image generation, diffusion, or continuous latent modeling priorities

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents are increasingly used as personal assistants for long-running tasks. Their utility depends on continuity: agents must retrieve and use earlier evidence across dialogue, files, and workspace state. However, agents can generate plausible answers even when access to that history has degraded, causing outcome-only evaluation to overestimate true evidence use. We present MIRAGE (Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation), a controlled study of historical evidence use under conversation-state variation in multimodal personal agents. MIRAGE holds evidence objects, questions, and scoring fixed while varying only conversation state, and evaluates whether an agent can determine answerability, recover the correct source, and answer from it. Across seven frontier and open-weight multimodal backbones, we find that: 1) pre-compaction depth and post-compaction continuation form distinct, non-monotonic failure regimes rather than a single degradation curve; 2) open-weight models rely heavily on context continuity and are reluctant to spontaneously switch to tool-mediated retrieval when provenance fails; and 3) retrieval pressure improves source attribution in deep pre-compaction states for tool-compliant models, but consistently regresses after compaction, where stored evidence has already degraded. These findings show that historical evidence use should be evaluated under state variation, rather than inferred from outcome-only correctness.

</details>


### [89] [Lexara-RF: Reference-Free Metrics for Evaluating Conversational Visual Analytics Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17842)
*Srishti Palani, Vidya Setlur*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (48.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Lexara-RF：一套面向对话式可视化分析（CVA）智能体的免参考评测指标。它将评测重构为“验证”，用 13 项指标把可视化设计理论与 Gricean 合作原则转化为可计算的一致性、意图对齐与设计有效性检查；在人工标注语料上达到与基于参考评测相当的人类对齐度，优于表面相似度基线，并能高精度定位失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评测由大模型驱动的对话式可视化分析（CVA）智能体生成的多模态输出（可视化图表+自然语言解释）非常困难：构建带参考答案的基准成本高昂、无法覆盖所有合法回答的空间，且在生产环境中根本没有参考答案可用，因此需要仅依赖 prompt、数据和模型响应的免参考评测方法。

Method: 在既有 Lexara 评测框架基础上提出 Lexara-RF：将评测任务重构为“验证”（verification）问题，设计 13 项免参考指标，把可视化设计理论（如图表类型与编码的设计有效性）与 Gricean 合作原则（量、质、关系、方式）操作化为可计算的一致性检查、意图对齐检查与设计有效性检查，仅需 prompt、数据与模型响应即可打分。

Result: 在人工评分的 CVA 测试用例语料上，Lexara-RF 与人类评分的对齐程度与基于参考的评测方法相当，显著优于表面相似度类 NLG 基线，并能以较高准确率定位具有结构性依据的失败原因。

Conclusion: 验证式免参考评测可成为多模态生成式智能体（如 CVA）输出的可行评测范式：它摆脱了对昂贵参考基准的依赖，可部署于生产环境，并能提供可定位、可解释的失败诊断，为多模态智能体的规模化自动评测提供了新路径。

Classification reason: 论文核心是为 LLM 驱动的对话式可视化分析（CVA）智能体提出免参考的评测指标与评测协议，属于多模态输出的自动评估研究，与 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）高度契合；其评测对象为智能体生成的可视化+文本输出，与 multimodal_generation 中的生成式评测范围弱相关，故同时列入匹配方向。

Importance reason: A solid but niche HCI-oriented contribution on reference-free evaluation of conversational visualization agents, with little overlap with the user's priorities in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: reference-free multimodal evaluation, conversational visual analytics agents, evaluation-as-verification with 13 metrics, human-rated corpus validation, cs.HC leaning

Abstract: Conversational visual analytics (CVA) agents powered by large language models generate visualizations and natural-language explanations from open-ended queries. Evaluating these multimodal outputs is challenging: curated reference benchmarks are costly to author, cannot comprehensively capture the space of valid responses, and are unavailable in production. Building on the Lexara evaluation framework, we introduce Lexara-RF, a reference-free set of metrics that scores CVA outputs using only the prompt, data, and model response. We reformulate evaluation as verification: 13 metrics operationalize visualization design theory and Gricean cooperative principles as computable consistency, intent-alignment, and design validity checks. On a human-rated corpus of CVA test-cases, Lexara-RF achieves alignment comparable to reference-based formulations, outperforms surface-similarity NLG baselines, and localizes structurally grounded failures with high accuracy.

</details>


### [90] [TeleAntiFraud 2.0: A Refreshable, Profile-Grounded, and Audio-Based Benchmark for Telecom Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18748)
*Huiyuan Liu, Zhiming Ma, Yanxing Liu, Shun Zhang, Qifan Wang, Di Liu, Yifan Wang, Yuyang Deng, Haoyang Meng, Yijin Zhou, Yuxi Zhao, Chengxian Hu, Peidong Wang, Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.SD

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (51.47) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 TeleAntiFraud 2.0，一个可按月刷新、基于人物画像的音频电信诈骗检测基准；通过混合树生成流水线在共享语境下构造诈骗与非诈骗对话并合成语音，按月冻结评测集。实验表明模型在近域难负例下性能大幅下降（Macro-F1 从满分降至 0.65-0.68），并出现类先验捷径与预测坍缩，确立近域构造与崩溃感知报告作为音频诈骗评测的核心要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信诈骗话术快速演化且常伪装成日常服务对话，现有音频诈骗评测基准存在两大缺陷：一是无法纳入新观测到的诈骗模式且会覆盖既有测试集，二是依赖话题分离的负例，使模型可借话题捷径得分而无法区分诈骗与合法的近域电话。

Method: 提出 Mixed-Tree Anti-Fraud Generation Pipeline：将在线诈骗案例摘要转化为基于人物画像的场景，通过混合树生成在共享上下文下构造诈骗与非诈骗对话路径，再渲染为角色匹配语音；按月度冻结协议发布评测集，每套包含 900 通中文电话（600 诈骗 + 300 近域非诈骗），并冻结音频、标签、提示词、清单与溯源记录。评测涵盖文本分类器、全量音频模型与 ASR+LLM 流水线。

Result: 对照实验显示，三个文本分类器在无关或普通负例上取得满分 Macro-F1，但在近域同源负例上降至 0.65-0.68；全量音频与 ASR+LLM 评测进一步暴露类先验捷径、预测坍缩（prediction collapse）与快照敏感性问题。

Conclusion: 近域难负例构造与崩溃感知的报告方式，是在真实易混淆条件下评测音频电信诈骗模型的核心要求；可刷新的月度冻结基准为持续追踪模型在新型话术下的真实能力提供了标准化范式。

Classification reason: 映射到多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子话题"多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）。论文核心贡献是一个全新的音频电信诈骗检测评测基准：提出可刷新的月度冻结评测协议、近域难负例构造与崩溃感知报告，并系统评测文本分类器与 ASR+LLM 系统，属于典型的多模态/语音理解评测基准研究。诈骗检测虽含安全属性，但论文主体是基准与评测协议构建，故首选评测基准子话题。

Importance reason: A solid domain-specific audio fraud-detection benchmark with interesting shortcut-learning findings, but it has essentially no overlap with the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: audio telecom-fraud benchmark, monthly refreshable evaluation protocol, near-domain hard negatives cause F1 collapse (1.0 to 0.65-0.68), class-prior shortcut and prediction collapse analysis, ASR+LLM evaluation only

Abstract: Telecom fraud scripts evolve rapidly and are often designed to resemble routine service conversations, creating two key requirements for audio-based telecom-fraud evaluation. First, benchmarks must incorporate newly observed scam patterns without overwriting previously established test sets. Second, they must distinguish fraud from lawful, near-domain calls rather than relying on topic-separated negative examples. We present TeleAntiFraud 2.0, constructed with our Mixed-Tree Anti-Fraud Generation Pipeline and evaluated under a monthly frozen evaluation protocol. The pipeline transforms online fraud-case abstracts into profile-grounded scenarios, expands them through mixed-tree generation, realizes fraud and non-fraud dialogue paths under shared contexts, renders validated dialogues as role-matched speech, and freezes the resulting audio, labels, prompts, manifests, and provenance records for each monthly evaluation set. Each frozen set contains 900 Chinese calls, comprising 600 fraud and 300 near-domain non-fraud cases. Controlled text experiments show that three classifiers achieve perfect macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1) when evaluated against unrelated or ordinary negatives, but drop to 0.65-0.68 with near-domain sibling negatives. Full-set audio and automatic-speech-recognition plus large-language-model (ASR+LLM) evaluations further reveal class-prior shortcuts, prediction collapse, and snapshot sensitivity. Together, these findings establish near-domain construction and collapse-aware reporting as core requirements for evaluating audio-based telecom-fraud models under realistic confusable conditions. The accompanying research artifact includes the construction code, evaluation scripts, manifests, and documentation. Our dataset and code are available at .

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-efficient_multimodal'></div>

## 多模态高效化与轻量化 [[Back]](#toc)

高效 MLLM、PEFT、蒸馏、量化、token 压缩、稀疏推理。

### [91] [Not All Layers Need Tuning: Diagnosing and Directing Adaptation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18084)
*Shahram Najam Syed, Arthur Jakobsson, Prayuj Sachdev, Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (60.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 该论文提出针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的"观察-诊断-分配-适配"高效微调流水线：仅凭 10 个无标注目标域观测，用梯度、Monte Carlo Dropout 与 CKA 信号在微调前估计视觉编码器、语言主干、动作头等各区域的适配代价，再按参数预算分配变秩 LoRA 并冻结校准良好区域。在 LIBERO/CALVIN 与真实 xArm-7 上，以 0.04% 的可训练参数匹配全量微调，并在所有预算下匹配或超过均匀 LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型微调到新部署环境代价高昂，而现有方法普遍对网络所有区域施加统一容量的适配器，隐含"每个区域需要同等调整"的假设。作者在五个不同架构、93M-7B 参数的 VLA 上检验该假设，发现适配代价呈结构性分布：外观变化集中于视觉编码器、指令变化集中于语言主干、新物体变化集中于视觉编码器与动作头，因此适配资源应按区域差异化分配。

Method: 首先在五个架构各异的 VLA（OpenVLA-OFT、π0、SmolVLA、DTP、Octo）上用区域隔离微调下的归一化参数位移度量各区域适配代价，发现适配代价谱系；进而提出观察—诊断—分配—适配流水线：仅用 10 个无标注目标域观测，在不微调的前提下结合 reference-free 梯度信号、Monte Carlo Dropout 信号与对缓存源参考的 Centered Kernel Alignment 分数估计各区域代价；分配器据此在参数预算内构建变秩 LoRA 适配器并冻结校准良好的区域，最后用标准 LoRA 微调训练所得适配器。

Result: 诊断器对各区域代价排序的中位 Spearman 相关达 0.91；在 LIBERO 和 CALVIN 上，分配式变秩 LoRA 在所有测试预算下匹配或超过均匀 LoRA；在真实 xArm-7 上，面对指令措辞变化仅用 0.04% 的可训练参数即匹配全量微调；在五个未见场景、30 次滚动的零重训评测中取得 11-23 次成功，优于同预算或更大预算下的最强参数高效基线（8-18）及全量微调（2-11）。

Conclusion: VLA 的适配代价具有跨架构的结构性，且可在微调开始前被量化；基于诊断的变秩 LoRA 分配以极小参数预算即可达到与全量微调相当的部署适应效果，为 VLA 的高效迁移提供了通用、可复用的范式。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是面向 VLA（视觉语言模型延展出的动作策略模型）的参数高效微调：诊断各网络区域适配代价并按参数预算分配变秩 LoRA、冻结冗余区域，属于 PEFT/高效多模态主题，故复用 [CANONICAL] 子题 efficient_multimodal（多模态高效化与轻量化）；论文不涉及世界建模、动作条件生成或多模态生成，与 world_model、multimodal_generation 等方向不符。

Importance reason: A well-executed, broadly validated efficient-adaptation pipeline for VLAs, but it sits in robotics fine-tuning rather than the user's core directions (continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, world models), matching mainly the lower-weight model-efficiency subtopic. Signals: parameter-efficient fine-tuning, variable-rank LoRA allocation, pre-finetuning adaptation-cost diagnostic (gradient/MC-Dropout/CKA), VLA models (OpenVLA-OFT, pi0, SmolVLA, Octo), robotics benchmarks LIBERO/CALVIN/xArm-7

Abstract: Fine-tuning a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model for a new deployment environment is expensive, yet most methods apply uniform-capacity adapters to every network region as if every region requires equal adjustment. This paper tests that assumption on five architecturally diverse VLAs (OpenVLA-OFT, $\pi_0$, SmolVLA, DTP, Octo; 93M-7B parameters). Measuring per-region adaptation cost as normalized parameter displacement under region-isolated fine-tuning reveals an adaptation spectrum in which appearance shifts concentrate cost in the vision encoder, instruction shifts in the language backbone, and novel-object shifts in the vision encoder together with the action head, across all five architectures. To exploit this structure, we introduce a pipeline that observes, diagnoses, allocates, and adapts. From ten unlabeled target observations and without fine-tuning, the diagnostic estimates per-region cost by combining reference-free gradient and Monte Carlo Dropout signals with a Centered Kernel Alignment score against a cached source reference; the allocator converts the estimates into variable-rank LoRA adapters under a parameter budget and freezes well-calibrated regions; and standard LoRA fine-tuning trains the resulting adapters. The diagnostic ranks regions within each deployment at a median Spearman of 0.91, and the allocation matches or exceeds uniform LoRA at every budget we tested on LIBERO and CALVIN. On a physical xArm-7, the pipeline matches full fine-tuning under an instruction-wording shift with 0.04% of its trainable parameters, and on five held-out scenes evaluated without retraining it leads every baseline, with 11-23 successes of 30 rollouts against 8-18 for the strongest parameter-efficient baseline at equal or larger budgets and 2-11 for full fine-tuning. These results suggest that adaptation cost in VLAs is structured enough to measure before fine-tuning begins.

</details>


### [92] [rMuscle: Robotic Muscle Memory for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19104)
*Kaijun Zhou, Zhiyang Li, Le Chen, Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (58.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 rMuscle，一个面向 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的实时推理加速框架。它利用机器人重复执行任务间在观测、动作与内部模型状态上的相似性，构建双阶段"肌肉记忆"缓存：Context Cache 复用视觉 token 输出以减少计算，Action Cache 复用神经元激活模式以减少权重访问，并辅以在线缓存重计算、滑动窗口检索与去噪步间掩码共享控制开销。在 RTX 4090 与 Jetson Thor 上于 LIBERO、RoboTwin 及真实操作任务中取得 1.29–1.42 倍加速且不损失成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂作业是具身 AI 的早期落地场景，VLA 模型已成为此类机器人策略的主流范式，而 VLA 推理延迟直接影响机器人响应速度与运动平滑度。现有 VLA 推理框架既未充分利用具身工作负载的特性（重复执行间的任务相似性），也未针对推理各阶段的不同瓶颈做差异化优化，因此存在显著的加速空间。

Method: 作者首先系统刻画具身工作负载，发现重复的机器人执行在观测、动作轨迹乃至模型内部状态上具有高度相似性。基于此提出受人类肌肉记忆启发的 rMuscle 实时推理框架，采用双阶段肌肉记忆缓存：Context Cache 复用视觉 token 输出以减少计算量，Action Cache 复用神经元激活模式以减少权重访问；并通过在线缓存重计算、滑动窗口缓存检索以及相邻去噪步之间的掩码共享，控制缓存内存占用与访问开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 与真实物理操作任务上，rMuscle 于 RTX 4090 和 Jetson Thor 平台取得 1.29–1.42 倍推理加速，同时在真实机器人上保持与原模型相同的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 利用具身任务重复执行中的相似性进行"肌肉记忆"式缓存复用，可以在不损失策略成功率的前提下显著降低 VLA 推理延迟，为机器人在工厂等实时具身场景中的高效部署提供了系统层面的可行方案。

Classification reason: 论文核心贡献是 VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的推理加速框架：通过视觉 token 输出复用与神经元激活缓存减少计算与访存，属于多模态模型高效化/推理优化范畴，故映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 efficient_multimodal（多模态高效化与轻量化）。论文虽面向机器人具身场景，但本身不涉及世界建模、环境模拟或未来预测，因此不归入世界模型方向，仅匹配一个方向。

Importance reason: A solid VLA inference acceleration system with novel dual-phase caching, but its robotics focus sits at the periphery of the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video/world-model generation, matching mainly the lower-weighted efficiency subtopic. Signals: efficient inference, VLA model acceleration, context/action cache reuse, 1.29-1.42x speedup on consumer and edge GPUs, robotics embodied workloads

Abstract: Factory work is a promising early scenario for embodied AI: assigning repetitive manual jobs to robots has clear economic payoff, and a structured station keeps the jobs tractable for current policies. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models now dominate as the policy paradigm for these robots. The inference latency of VLA models directly affects robot responsiveness and motion smoothness. However, existing VLA inference frameworks do not fully exploit the characteristics of embodied workloads or account for the distinct bottlenecks across different stages of VLA inference. In this paper, we first characterize embodied workloads and identify substantial task similarity across repeated robot executions. We further find that such similarity extends beyond observations and action trajectories to internal model states. Drawing on these observations, we present rMuscle, a real-time VLA inference framework inspired by human muscle memory. It exploits cross-execution similarity through a dual-phase muscle-memory cache. The Context Cache reuses visual-token outputs to reduce computation, while the Action Cache reuses neuron activation patterns to reduce weight accesses. We keep both the cache memory footprint and access overhead low through online cache recomputation, sliding-window cache retrieval, and mask sharing across consecutive denoising steps. rMuscle achieves 1.29-1.42X speedup on RTX 4090 and Jetson Thor across LIBERO, RoboTwin, and physical manipulation tasks, while maintaining the original success rates on real-world robots.

</details>
